                    <div class="error-msg" id="confirmPassword-error"></div>
                </div>
            </div>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jquery/dist/jquery.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/layui-src/dist/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['form','layer'], function() {
        var form = layui.form;
        var layer = layui.layer;
        var $ = layui.jquery;
        var $sendBtn = $('#sendCodeBtn');
        var countdown = 60;
        var timer = null;
        // 输入验证函数
        function validateField($field, validator, errorMsg) {
            var value = $.trim($field.val());
            var $error = $('#' + $field.attr('name') + '-error');
            if (!validator(value)) {
                $field.addClass('input-error');
                $error.show().text(errorMsg);
                return false;
            } else {
                $field.removeClass('input-error');
                $error.hide();
                return true;
            }
        }
        // 手机号验证
        function validatePhone(phone) {
            return /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/.test(phone);
        }
        // 密码验证
        function validatePassword(pass) {
            return pass.length >= 8 && /[A-Za-z]/.test(pass) && /\d/.test(pass);
        }
        // 实时验证
        $('input[name="phone"]').on('input', function() {
            validateField($(this), validatePhone, '请输入有效的手机号');
        });
        $('input[name="password"]').on('input', function() {
            validateField($(this), validatePassword, '密码至少8位，且包含字母和数字');
            // 同时验证确认密码
            var confirmPassword = $('input[name="confirmPassword"]').val();
            if (confirmPassword) {
                validateField($('input[name="confirmPassword"]'), function(val) {
                    return val === $('input[name="password"]').val();
                }, '两次输入的密码不一致');
            }
        });
        $('input[name="confirmPassword"]').on('input', function() {
            validateField($(this), function(val) {
                return val === $('input[name="password"]').val();
            }, '两次输入的密码不一致');
        });
        // 验证规则
        form.verify({
            pwd: [
                /^(?=.*[A-Za-z])(?=.*\d)[A-Za-z\d@$!%*#?&]{8,}$/,
                '密码至少8位，且包含字母和数字'
            ],
            confirmPwd: function(value){
                var pwd = $('input[name="password"]').val();
                if (value !== pwd) {
                    return '两次输入的密码不一致';
                }
            },
            phone: [
                /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/,
                '请输入有效的电话号码'
            ]
        });
        function startCountdown() {
            var left = countdown;
            $sendBtn.prop('disabled', true).text(left + 's后重试');
            timer = setInterval(function() {
                left--;
                if (left <= 0) {
                    clearInterval(timer);
                    $sendBtn.prop('disabled', false).text('获取验证码');
                } else {
                    $sendBtn.text(left + 's后重试');
                }
            }, 1000);
        }
        $sendBtn.on('click', function() {
            if ($sendBtn.prop('disabled')) return;
            var phone = $.trim($('input[name="phone"]').val());
            if (!validatePhone(phone)) {
                layer.msg('请输入有效的手机号码后再获取验证码', {icon:5});
                $('input[name="phone"]').addClass('input-error');
                $('#phone-error').show().text('请输入有效的手机号');
                return;
            }
            $.ajax({
                url: '/client/send-code',
                method: 'POST',
                data: { phone: phone },
                success: function(res) {
                    if (res && (res.code === 0 || res.status === 1)) {
                        layer.msg('验证码已发送', {icon:1});
                        startCountdown();
                    } else {
                        layer.msg((res && (res.message || res.msg)) || '发送失败', {icon:5});
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    if (xhr && xhr.status === 401) {
                        layer.msg('请先登录', {icon:5}, function(){ location.href = '/login.html'; });
                    } else {
                        layer.msg('发送失败，请稍后重试', {icon:5});
                    }
                }
            });
        });
        // 提交注册
        $('#registerForm').on('submit', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            // 验证所有字段
            var phoneValid = validateField($('input[name="phone"]'), validatePhone, '请输入有效的手机号');
            var passwordValid = validateField($('input[name="password"]'), validatePassword, '密码至少8位，且包含字母和数字');
            var confirmPasswordValid = validateField($('input[name="confirmPassword"]'), function(val) {
                return val === $('input[name="password"]').val();
            }, '两次输入的密码不一致');
            var code = $.trim($('input[name="code"]').val());
            var codeValid = true;
            if (!code) {
                $('input[name="code"]').addClass('input-error');
                $('#code-error').show().text('请输入验证码');
                codeValid = false;
            } else {
                $('input[name="code"]').removeClass('input-error');
                $('#code-error').hide();
            }
            if (!(phoneValid && passwordValid && confirmPasswordValid && codeValid)) {
                // 滚动到第一个错误
                var $firstError = $('.input-error').first();
                if ($firstError.length) {
                    $('html, body').animate({
                        scrollTop: $firstError.offset().top - 100
                    }, 300);
                }
                return;
            }
            var postData = {
                username: $.trim($('input[name="username"]').val()),
                password: $('input[name="password"]').val(),
                phone: $('input[name="phone"]').val(),
                code: code
            };
            var $submit = $('#submitBtn');
            $submit.prop('disabled', true).text('提交中...');
            $.ajax({
                url: '/client/register',
                method: 'POST',
                data: postData,
                success: function(res) {
                    $submit.prop('disabled', false).text('注册');
                    if (res && (res.code === 0 || res.status === 1)) {
                        layer.msg('注册成功，已跳转登录页', {icon:1, time:1200}, function(){
                            location.href = '/login.html';
                        });
                    } else {
                        var msg = (res && (res.message || res.msg)) || '注册失败';
                        // 清理后端可能带的前缀
                        msg = msg.replace('register.username: ', '');
                        layer.msg(msg, {icon:5});
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    $submit.prop('disabled', false).text('注册');
                    if (xhr && xhr.status === 401) {
                        layer.msg('未登录或会话已过期', {icon:5}, function(){ location.href = '/login.html'; });
                    } else {
                        layer.msg('网络或服务器错误，请稍后重试', {icon:2});
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<div class="panel" role="region" aria-label="修改密码表单">
    <div class="panel-header">
        <div class="icon">🔒</div>
        <div>
            <h2>修改密码</h2>
            <div class="panel-desc">请输入手机号、原密码与新密码，并确认以完成修改</div>
        </div>
    </div>
    <form class="layui-form" id="passwordForm" lay-filter="passwordForm" autocomplete="off">
        <div class="layui-form-item">
            <label for="phone" class="layui-form-label">手机号</label>
            <div class="layui-input-block">
                <input id="phone" type="tel" inputmode="tel" name="phone" placeholder="请输入手机号" class="layui-input" autocomplete="tel" />
                <div class="error-msg" id="phone-error"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="layui-form-item">
            <label for="oldPass" class="layui-form-label">原密码</label>
            <div class="layui-input-block password-container">
                <input id="oldPass" type="password" name="pass" placeholder="请输入当前密码" class="layui-input" autocomplete="current-password" />
                <button type="button" class="password-toggle" aria-label="显示密码">👁️</button>
                <div class="error-msg" id="oldPass-error"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="layui-form-item">
            <label for="newPass" class="layui-form-label">新密码</label>
            <div class="layui-input-block password-container">
                <input id="newPass" type="password" name="pass1" placeholder="请输入新密码（至少8位，包含字母和数字）" class="layui-input" autocomplete="new-password" />
                <button type="button" class="password-toggle" aria-label="显示密码">👁️</button>
                <div class="error-msg" id="newPass-error"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="layui-form-item">
            <label for="confirmPass" class="layui-form-label">确认新密码</label>
            <div class="layui-input-block password-container">
                <input id="confirmPass" type="password" name="pass2" placeholder="请再次输入新密码" class="layui-input" autocomplete="new-password" />
                <button type="button" class="password-toggle" aria-label="显示密码">👁️</button>
                <div class="error-msg" id="confirmPass-error"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="layui-form-item">
            <div class="layui-input-block action-row">
                <button id="saveBtn" type="button" class="layui-btn layui-btn-normal" lay-filter="save">保存</button>
                <button id="cancelBtn" type="button" class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary">取消</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </form>
    <div class="divider"></div>
    <div class="helper" aria-live="polite">
        <a href="login.html">修改成功，立即登录</a>
    </div>
</div>
<script>
    layui.use(['form','layer'], function(){
        var form = layui.form;
        var layer = layui.layer;
        var $ = layui.jquery;
        // 密码显示/隐藏功能
        $('.password-toggle').on('touchstart click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var $this = $(this);
            var $input = $this.prevAll('input');
            var type = $input.attr('type');
            if (type === 'password') {
                $input.attr('type', 'text');
                $this.text('🔒');
            } else {
                $input.attr('type', 'password');
                $this.text('👁️');
            }
        });
        // 输入验证函数
        function validateField($field, validator, errorMsg) {
            var value = $.trim($field.val());
            var $error = $('#' + $field.attr('id') + '-error');
            if (!validator(value)) {
                $field.addClass('input-error');
                $error.show().text(errorMsg);
                return false;
            } else {
                $field.removeClass('input-error');
                $error.hide();
                return true;
            }
        }
        // 手机号验证
        function validatePhone(phone) {
            return /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/.test(phone);
        }
        // 密码验证
        function validatePassword(pass) {
            return pass.length >= 8 && /[A-Za-z]/.test(pass) && /\d/.test(pass);
        }
        // 实时验证
        $('#phone').on('input', function() {
            validateField($(this), validatePhone, '请输入有效的手机号');
        });
        $('#newPass').on('input', function() {
            validateField($(this), validatePassword, '密码至少8位，且包含字母和数字');
            // 同时验证确认密码
            var confirmPass = $('#confirmPass').val();
            if (confirmPass) {
                validateField($('#confirmPass'), function(val) {
                    return val === $('#newPass').val();
                }, '两次输入的新密码不一致');
            }
        });
        $('#confirmPass').on('input', function() {
            validateField($(this), function(val) {
                return val === $('#newPass').val();
            }, '两次输入的新密码不一致');
        });
        // 回车快捷提交
        $('#passwordForm').on('keydown', 'input', function(e){
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                $('#saveBtn').trigger('click');
            }
        });
        // 取消按钮：清空表单或关闭弹窗
        $('#cancelBtn').on('click', function(){
            try {
                var idx = parent.layer.getFrameIndex(window.name);
                if (idx !== undefined) {
                    parent.layer.close(idx);
                    return;
                }
            } catch (e) { /* 忽略 */ }
            $('#passwordForm')[0].reset();
            $('.input-error').removeClass('input-error');
            $('.error-msg').hide();
        });
        var saving = false;
        $('#saveBtn').on('click', function(){
            if (saving) return;
            // 验证所有字段
            var phoneValid = validateField($('#phone'), validatePhone, '请输入有效的手机号');
            var oldPassValid = validateField($('#oldPass'), function(val) { return val.length > 0; }, '请输入当前密码');
            var newPassValid = validateField($('#newPass'), validatePassword, '密码至少8位，且包含字母和数字');
            var confirmPassValid = validateField($('#confirmPass'), function(val) {
                return val === $('#newPass').val();
            }, '两次输入的新密码不一致');
            if (!(phoneValid && oldPassValid && newPassValid && confirmPassValid)) {
                // 滚动到第一个错误
                var $firstError = $('.input-error').first();
                if ($firstError.length) {
                    $('html, body').animate({
                        scrollTop: $firstError.offset().top - 100
                    }, 300);
                }
                return;
            }
            saving = true;
            var $btn = $('#saveBtn');
            $btn.prop('disabled', true).text('保存中...');
            var payload = {
                phone: $('#phone').val(),
                pass: $('#oldPass').val(),
                pass1: $('#newPass').val(),
                pass2: $('#confirmPass').val()
            };
            // 通过现有 ajax_post 封装提交
            try {
                ajax_post('/client/updatepass', payload, function(json){
                    saving = false;
                    $btn.prop('disabled', false).text('保存');
                    if (json && (json.status > 0 || json.code === 0 || json.success === true)) {
                        layer.msg(json.msg || '密码更新成功', {icon:1, time:1400}, function(){
                            try {
                                var idx = parent.layer.getFrameIndex(window.name);
                                if (idx !== undefined) {
                                    parent.layer.close(idx);
                                    if (parent && parent.location) parent.location.reload();
                                    return;
                                }
                            } catch(e) {}
                        });
                    } else {
                        var msg = (json && (json.msg || json.message)) ? (json.msg || json.message) : '密码修改失败';
                        layer.msg(msg, {icon:5});
                    }
                }, function(err){
                    saving = false;
                    $btn.prop('disabled', false).text('保存');
                    layer.msg('网络错误，请稍后重试', {icon:2});
                });
            } catch (e) {
                // 如果环境未提供 ajax_post，回退到 jQuery
                $.ajax({
                    url: '/client/updatepass',
                    method: 'POST',
                    contentType: 'application/json',
                    data: JSON.stringify(payload),
                    success: function(json){
                        saving = false;
                        $btn.prop('disabled', false).text('保存');
                        if (json && (json.status > 0 || json.code === 0 || json.success === true)) {
                            layer.msg(json.msg || '密码更新成功', {icon:1, time:1400}, function(){
                                try {
                                    var idx = parent.layer.getFrameIndex(window.name);
                                    if (idx !== undefined) {
                                        parent.layer.close(idx);
                                        parent.location.reload();
                                        return;
                                    }
                                } catch(e) {}
                            });
                        } else {
                            var msg = (json && (json.msg || json.message)) ? (json.msg || json.message) : '密码修改失败';
                            layer.msg(msg, {icon:5});
                        }
                    },
                    error: function(xhr){
                        saving = false;
                        $btn.prop('disabled', false).text('保存');
                        if (xhr && xhr.status === 401) {
                            layer.msg('登录已过期，请重新登录', {icon:5}, function(){
                                location.href = '/login.html';
                            });
                        } else {
                            layer.msg('网络或服务器错误，请稍后重试', {icon:2});
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="text/javascript">
    var $;
    var layer;
    layui.use(["jquery","layer"], function(){
        $ = layui.jquery;
        layer = layui.layer;
        var $form = $("#loginForm");
        var $btn = $("#loginBtn");
        var $phone = $("#phone");
        var $pass = $("#password");
        // 回车触发表单提交
        $form.on("submit", function(e){
            e.preventDefault();
            submitLogin();
        });
        // 快速键：回车在输入框中也提交
        $form.find("input").on("keydown", function(e){
            if(e.key === "Enter"){
                e.preventDefault();
                submitLogin();
            }
        });
        // 手机号验证函数
        function validatePhone(phone) {
            var reg = /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/;
            return reg.test(phone);
        }
        function submitLogin(){
            if($btn.prop("disabled")) return;
            var phone = $.trim($phone.val());
            var pass = $.trim($pass.val());
            // 基本校验
            if(!phone){
                layer.msg("请输入手机号", {icon:5});
                $phone.focus();
                return;
            }
            // 手机号格式验证
            if(!validatePhone(phone)){
                layer.msg("请输入正确的手机号格式", {icon:5});
                $phone.focus();
                return;
            }
            if(!pass){
                layer.msg("请输入密码", {icon:5});
                $pass.focus();
                return;
            }
            // 按钮进入加载状态，防止重复提交
            $btn.prop("disabled", true).text("登录中...");
            // 使用正确的参数名和设置 Content-Type
            $.ajax({
                url: "/client/login",
                type: "POST",
                contentType: "application/json;charset=UTF-8", // 关键：设置 Content-Type
                data: JSON.stringify({
                    "tel": phone,  // 改为 tel 而不是 phone
                    "pass": pass
                }),
                success: function(json){
                    // 恢复按钮状态
                    $btn.prop("disabled", false).text("登录");
                    if(json && json.code === 200){
                        // 登录成功
                        localStorage.setItem('id',json.data.user.id);
                        layer.msg("登录成功，正在跳转...", {icon:1, time:1500}, function(){
                            // 根据权限跳转到不同页面
                            if (json.data && json.data.user && json.data.user.power === 1) {
                                location.href = "/admin/index.html";
                            } else {
                                location.href = "/Client/user.html";
                            }
                        });
                    } else {
                        // 登录失败
                        var msg = (json && json.msg) ? json.msg : "登录失败，请检查手机号或密码";
                        layer.msg(msg, {icon:5});
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status, error){
                    // 网络错误处理
                    $btn.prop("disabled", false).text("登录");
                    if(xhr.status === 415) {
                        layer.msg("请求格式错误，请联系管理员", {icon:5});
                    } else {
                        layer.msg("网络错误，请稍后重试", {icon:2});
                    }
                    console.error("登录请求失败:", error);
                }
            });
        }
        // 页面加载时将焦点放在手机号输入框
        $phone.focus();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

package com.syd.medicalbutler.serviceImpl;

package com.syd.medicalbutler.serviceImpl;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.impl.ServiceImpl;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ProjectItemMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Project;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.ProjectDetailVO;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.ProjectItem;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.ProjectItemsResult;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.ProjectItemService;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.ProjectService;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ProjectMapper;
import org.springframework.beans.BeanUtils;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
@Service
public class ProjectServiceImpl extends ServiceImpl<ProjectMapper, Project> implements ProjectService {
    @Autowired
    private ProjectItemService projectItemService;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectMapper projectMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectItemMapper projectItemMapper;
    @Override
    public List<ProjectDetailVO> getProjectDetailList(String name, Integer status, int page, int size) {
        QueryWrapper<Project> wrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        wrapper.eq("isdel", 0);
        if (StringUtils.hasText(name)) {
            wrapper.like("name", name);
        }
        if (status != null) {
            wrapper.eq("status", status);
        }
        Page<Project> projectPage = new Page<>(page, size);
        Page<Project> resultPage = this.page(projectPage, wrapper);
        List<Project> projects = resultPage.getRecords();
        if (projects == null || projects.isEmpty()) {
            return new ArrayList<>();
        }
        List<String> allItemIds = projects.stream()
                .map(Project::getItemIds)
                .filter(StringUtils::hasText)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        Map<Integer, ProjectItem> allItemsMap = getAllProjectItemsMap(allItemIds);
        List<ProjectDetailVO> result = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Project project : projects) {
            ProjectDetailVO vo = new ProjectDetailVO();
            BeanUtils.copyProperties(project, vo);
            if (StringUtils.hasText(project.getItemIds())) {
                List<ProjectItem> projectItems = getProjectItemsFromMap(project.getItemIds(), allItemsMap);
                vo.setProjectItems(projectItems);
            } else {
                vo.setProjectItems(new ArrayList<>());
            }
            result.add(vo);
        }
        return result;
    }
    @Override
    public int countProjects(String name, Integer status) {
        QueryWrapper<Project> wrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        wrapper.eq("isdel", 0);
        if (StringUtils.hasText(name)) {
            wrapper.like("name", name);
        }
        if (status != null) {
            wrapper.eq("status", status);
        }
        return Math.toIntExact(this.count(wrapper));
    }
    private Map<Integer, ProjectItem> getAllProjectItemsMap(List<String> allItemIds) {
        if (allItemIds == null || allItemIds.isEmpty()) {
            return new HashMap<>();
        }
        Set<Integer> allUniqueIds = new HashSet<>();
        for (String itemIds : allItemIds) {
            if (StringUtils.hasText(itemIds)) {
                for (String idStr : itemIds.split(",")) {
                    try {
                        allUniqueIds.add(Integer.parseInt(idStr.trim()));
                    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (allUniqueIds.isEmpty()) {
            return new HashMap<>();
        }
        QueryWrapper<ProjectItem> queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        queryWrapper.in("id", allUniqueIds);
        queryWrapper.eq("isdel", 0);
        List<ProjectItem> allItems = projectItemService.list(queryWrapper);
        return allItems.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(ProjectItem::getId, item -> item));
    }
    private List<ProjectItem> getProjectItemsFromMap(String itemIds, Map<Integer, ProjectItem> allItemsMap) {
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(itemIds) || allItemsMap.isEmpty()) {
            return new ArrayList<>();
        }
        List<ProjectItem> result = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String idStr : itemIds.split(",")) {
            try {
                Integer id = Integer.parseInt(idStr.trim());
                ProjectItem item = allItemsMap.get(id);
                if (item != null) {
                    result.add(item);
                }
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
    @Override
    public ProjectItemsResult getProjectItemsByProjectId(Integer projectId) {
        ProjectItemsResult result = new ProjectItemsResult();
        Project project = projectMapper.selectById(projectId);
        if (project == null) {
            result.setSelectedIds(Collections.emptyList());
            result.setSelectedItems(Collections.emptyList());
            return result;
        }
        String itemsIdStr = project.getItemIds();
        if (itemsIdStr == null || itemsIdStr.trim().isEmpty()) {
            result.setSelectedIds(Collections.emptyList());
            result.setSelectedItems(Collections.emptyList());
            return result;
        }
        String[] parts = itemsIdStr.split(",");
        List<Integer> ids = new ArrayList<>();
        List<String> selectedIdsAsString = new ArrayList<>();
        Set<Integer> seen = new HashSet<>();
        for (String p : parts) {
            if (p == null) continue;
            String t = p.trim();
            if (t.length() == 0) continue;
            try {
                Integer parsed = Integer.valueOf(t);
                if (seen.add(parsed)) {
                    ids.add(parsed);
                    selectedIdsAsString.add(String.valueOf(parsed));
                }
            } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            }
        }
        if (ids.isEmpty()) {
            result.setSelectedIds(Collections.emptyList());
            result.setSelectedItems(Collections.emptyList());
            return result;
        }
        List<ProjectItem> items = projectItemMapper.selectBatchIds(ids);
        if (items != null && !items.isEmpty()) {
            Map<Integer, ProjectItem> map = items.stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(ProjectItem::getId, item -> item));
            List<ProjectItem> ordered = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Integer id : ids) {
                ProjectItem it = map.get(id);
                if (it != null) ordered.add(it);
            }
            result.setSelectedItems(ordered);
        } else {
            result.setSelectedItems(Collections.emptyList());
        }
        result.setSelectedIds(selectedIdsAsString);
        return result;
    }
    @Override
    public ProjectDetailVO getProjectDetailById(Integer id) {
        Project project = this.getById(id);
        if (project == null) {
            return null;
        }
        ProjectDetailVO vo = new ProjectDetailVO();
        BeanUtils.copyProperties(project, vo);
        vo.setProjectItems(getProjectItemsByIds(project.getItemIds()));
        return vo;
    }
    @Override
    public List<ProjectDetailVO> getProjectDetailList() {
        QueryWrapper<Project> wrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        wrapper.eq("isdel", 0);
        List<Project> projects = this.list(wrapper);
        List<ProjectDetailVO> result = new ArrayList<>();
        if (projects == null || projects.isEmpty()) {
            return result;
        }
        for (Project project : projects) {
            ProjectDetailVO vo = new ProjectDetailVO();
            BeanUtils.copyProperties(project, vo);
            vo.setProjectItems(getProjectItemsByIds(project.getItemIds()));
            result.add(vo);
        }
        return result;
    }
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public boolean createProject(ProjectDetailVO projectDetail) {
        Project project = new Project();
        BeanUtils.copyProperties(projectDetail, project);
        boolean success = this.save(project);
        if (success && projectDetail.getProjectItems() != null && !projectDetail.getProjectItems().isEmpty()) {
            StringBuilder itemIdsBuilder = new StringBuilder();
            for (ProjectItem item : projectDetail.getProjectItems()) {
                if (itemIdsBuilder.length() > 0) {
                    itemIdsBuilder.append(",");
                }
                itemIdsBuilder.append(item.getId());
            }
            project.setItemIds(itemIdsBuilder.toString());
            this.updateById(project);
        }
        return success;
    }
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public boolean updateProject(ProjectDetailVO projectDetail) {
        Project project = new Project();
        BeanUtils.copyProperties(projectDetail, project);
        if (project.getItemIds() == null) {
            project.setItemIds("");
        }
        return this.updateById(project);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean deleteProject(Integer id) {
        Project project = new Project();
        project.setId(id);
        project.setIsdel(1);
        return this.updateById(project);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean batchDeleteProject(List<Integer> ids) {
        List<Project> projects = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Integer id : ids) {
            Project project = new Project();
            project.setId(id);
            project.setIsdel(1);
            projects.add(project);
        }
        return this.updateBatchById(projects);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean batchUpdateStatus(List<Integer> ids, Integer status) {
        List<Project> projects = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Integer id : ids) {
            Project project = new Project();
            project.setId(id);
            project.setStatus(status);
            projects.add(project);
        }
        return this.updateBatchById(projects);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean updateStatus(Integer id, Integer status) {
        Project project = new Project();
        project.setId(id);
        project.setStatus(status);
        return this.updateById(project);
    }
    private List<ProjectItem> getProjectItemsByIds(String itemIds) {
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(itemIds)) {
            return new ArrayList<>();
        }
        List<Integer> ids = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String idStr : itemIds.split(",")) {
            try {
                ids.add(Integer.parseInt(idStr.trim()));
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            }
        }
        if (ids.isEmpty()) {
            return new ArrayList<>();
        }
        QueryWrapper<ProjectItem> queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        queryWrapper.in("id", ids);
        queryWrapper.eq("isdel", 0); 
        return projectItemService.list(queryWrapper);
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.serviceImpl;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.impl.ServiceImpl;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Client;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.ClientService;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ClientMapper;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.util.DigestUtils;
@Service
public class ClientServiceImpl extends ServiceImpl<ClientMapper, Client>
    implements ClientService{
    @Autowired
    private ClientMapper clientMapper;
    @Override
    public Client login(String tel, String pass) {
        try {
            QueryWrapper<Client> queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
            queryWrapper.eq("tel", tel);
            queryWrapper.eq("isdel", 0); 
            Client client = clientMapper.selectOne(queryWrapper);
            if (client == null) {
                return null; 
            }
            String encryptedPass = DigestUtils.md5DigestAsHex(pass.getBytes()).toLowerCase();
            if (encryptedPass.equals(client.getPass())) {
                return client; 
            }
            return null; 
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            return null;
        }
    }
    private String encryptPassword(String password) {
        return DigestUtils.md5DigestAsHex(password.getBytes()).toLowerCase();
    }
    @Override
    public boolean isTelRegistered(String tel) {
        QueryWrapper<Client> queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        queryWrapper.eq("tel", tel);
        return this.count(queryWrapper) > 0;
    }
    @Override
    public Client getClientByTel(String tel) {
        QueryWrapper<Client> queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        queryWrapper.eq("tel", tel);
        return this.getOne(queryWrapper);
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.serviceImpl;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.Wrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.metadata.IPage;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.ResultInfo;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.SearchInfo;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.User;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import java.lang.reflect.ParameterizedType;
import java.lang.reflect.Type;
import java.util.List;
public class BaseServiceImpl<T> {
    @Autowired
    BaseMapper<T> mymapper;
    User current;
    private  Class<T> getTClass(){
        Class<?> subclass = this.getClass();
        Type genericSuperclass = subclass.getGenericSuperclass();
        if (genericSuperclass instanceof ParameterizedType) {
            ParameterizedType pt = (ParameterizedType) genericSuperclass;
            return (Class<T>) pt.getActualTypeArguments()[0];
        }
        return null;
    }
    public ResultInfo select(SearchInfo sea, QueryWrapper<T> ex) {
        ResultInfo info=new ResultInfo();
        QueryWrapper<T> q=sea.getQuery(ex,getTClass());
        Class cls=getTClass();
        try {
            cls.getDeclaredField("isdel");
            q.eq("isdel",0);
        }catch (Exception e){
        }
        if(sea.getLimit()<10000){
            IPage<T> po=sea.getPageInfo(getTClass());
            po=  mymapper.selectPage(po,q);
             info.setData(po.getRecords());
             info.setCount(po.getTotal());
        }else{
            info.setData(mymapper.selectList(q));
        }
        info.setParams(sea.getSea_val());
            return info;
    }
    public List<T> SelectList(Wrapper<T> q){
        return mymapper.selectList(q);
    }
    public void insert(T u) {
        mymapper.insert(u);
    }
    public void update(T u) {
        mymapper.updateById(u);
    }
    public void delete(int id) {
        Class cls=getTClass();
        try {
            cls.getDeclaredField("isdel");
            mymapper.update(null,new UpdateWrapper<T>().eq("id",id).set("isdel","1"));
        }catch (Exception e){
            mymapper.deleteById(id);
        }
    }
    public T getById(int id) {
        return mymapper.selectById(id);
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.config;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.HandlerInterceptor;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
public class LoginIntercepor implements HandlerInterceptor {
    private static final List<String> WHITELIST_PREFIX = Arrays.asList(
            "/",
            "/login.html",
            "/loginapp.html",
            "/register.html",
            "/api/user/register",
            "/User/login",
            "/layui/",
            "/static/",
            "/css/",
            "/js/",
            "/images/",
            "/pic/"
    );
    private boolean isAjax(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String xrw = request.getHeader("X-Requested-With");
        String accept = request.getHeader("Accept");
        return (xrw != null && "XMLHttpRequest".equalsIgnoreCase(xrw))
                || (accept != null && accept.contains("application/json"));
    }
    private boolean isWhitelisted(String uri) {
        if (uri == null) return true;
        for (String p : WHITELIST_PREFIX) {
            if (p.endsWith("/")) {
                if (uri.startsWith(p)) return true;
            } else {
                if (uri.equals(p) || uri.startsWith(p)) return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    private void sendJsonUnauthorized(HttpServletResponse response) throws IOException {
        response.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED);
        response.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        response.getWriter().write("{\"code\":401, \"message\":\"未登录或登录已过期\"}");
    }
    @Override
    public boolean preHandle(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, Object handler) throws Exception {
        String uri = request.getRequestURI();
        System.out.println("Incoming request: " + uri);
        if (isWhitelisted(uri)) {
            return true;
        }
        Object loginType = request.getSession().getAttribute("logintype");
        if (loginType == null) {
            if (isAjax(request)) {
                sendJsonUnauthorized(response);
            } else {
                if (uri.startsWith("/app/")) {
                    response.sendRedirect("/loginapp.html");
                } else {
                    response.sendRedirect("/login.html");
                }
            }
            return false;
        }
        try {
            if (uri.startsWith("/app/")) {
                if (request.getSession().getAttribute("client") != null) {
                    return true;
                } else {
                    if (isAjax(request)) {
                        sendJsonUnauthorized(response);
                    } else {
                        response.sendRedirect("/loginapp.html");
                    }
                    return false;
                }
            } else {
                if (request.getSession().getAttribute("user") != null) {
                    return true;
                } else {
                    if (isAjax(request)) {
                        sendJsonUnauthorized(response);
                    } else {
                        response.sendRedirect("/login.html");
                    }
                    return false;
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            if (isAjax(request)) {
                sendJsonUnauthorized(response);
            } else {
                response.sendRedirect("/login.html");
            }
            return false;
        }
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.utils;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
public class SimpleCacheCode {
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<String, String> codeMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<String, Long> expireMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final ScheduledExecutorService cleaner = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor();
    private final long expireSeconds;
    public SimpleCacheCode(long expireSeconds) {
        this.expireSeconds = expireSeconds;
        cleaner.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::cleanup, 60, 60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    public void put(String phone, String code) {
        codeMap.put(phone, code);
        expireMap.put(phone, Instant.now().getEpochSecond() + expireSeconds);
    }
    public String get(String phone) {
        Long exp = expireMap.get(phone);
        if (exp == null) return null;
        if (Instant.now().getEpochSecond() > exp) {
            codeMap.remove(phone);
            expireMap.remove(phone);
            return null;
        }
        return codeMap.get(phone);
    }
    public void remove(String phone) {
        codeMap.remove(phone);
        expireMap.remove(phone);
    }
    private void cleanup() {
        long now = Instant.now().getEpochSecond();
        for (Map.Entry<String, Long> e : expireMap.entrySet()) {
            if (e.getValue() < now) {
                String phone = e.getKey();
                expireMap.remove(phone);
                codeMap.remove(phone);
            }
        }
    }
    public void shutdown() {
        cleaner.shutdownNow();
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.utils;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
public class DateUtil {
	public static String formatDate(Date date,String format){
		String result="";
		SimpleDateFormat sdf=new SimpleDateFormat(format);
		if(date!=null){
			result=sdf.format(date);
		}
		return result;
	}
	public static Date formatString(String str,String format) throws Exception{
		if(StringUtil.isEmpty(str)){
			return null;
		}
		SimpleDateFormat sdf=new SimpleDateFormat(format);
		return sdf.parse(str);
	}
	public static String getCurrentDateStr(){
		Date date=new Date();
		SimpleDateFormat sdf=new SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMddhhmmssSSSSSSSSS");
		return sdf.format(date);
	}
	public static String getCurrentDatePath()throws Exception{
		Date date=new Date();
		SimpleDateFormat sdf=new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy/MM/dd/");
		return sdf.format(date);
	}
	public static void main(String[] args) {
		try {
			System.out.println(getCurrentDateStr());
		} catch (Exception e) {
			e.printStackTrace();
		}
	}
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.ResultData;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;
import java.io.File;
@RestController
public class FileUpload_controller {
    @Value("${file.uploadFolder}")
    private String uploadFolder;
    @Value("${file.staticAccessPath}")
    private String staticAccessPath;
@RequestMapping("file_upload")
    public ResultData upload(MultipartFile file)  {
        String basicdit="pics";
            String fileName = file.getOriginalFilename();
            String filePath = uploadFolder +basicdit+ "\\";
            File targetFile = new File(filePath);
            if (!targetFile.exists()) {
                targetFile.mkdirs();
            }
            try {
                String add=staticAccessPath.substring(0,staticAccessPath.length()-2);
                fileName=System.currentTimeMillis()+".png";
                file.transferTo(new File(filePath + fileName));
                 String url=add+basicdit+"/" +fileName;
                return new ResultData(1,url);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                return new ResultData(-1);
            }
    }
}

    private String avatarBaseDir;
    @Value("${app.upload.avatar-max-size:2097152}") 
    private long avatarMaxSize;
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        codeCache = new SimpleCacheCode(6 * 60);
    }
    @PreDestroy
    public void destroy() {
        if (codeCache != null) {
            codeCache.shutdown();
        }
    }
    @Autowired
    private ClientService clientService;
    @PostMapping("/login")
    public R login(@RequestBody Map<String, String> params, HttpServletRequest request) {
        String tel = params.get("tel");
        String pass = params.get("pass");
        if (tel == null || tel.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return R.error("手机号不能为空");
        }
        if (pass == null || pass.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return R.error("密码不能为空");
        }
        if (!tel.matches("^1[3-9]\\d{9}$")) {
            return R.error("手机号格式不正确");
        }
        try {
            Client client = clientService.login(tel, pass);
            if (client != null) {
                if (client.getIsdel() != null && client.getIsdel() == 1) {
                    return R.error("账号已被删除");
                }
                HttpSession session = request.getSession();
                session.setAttribute("client", client);
                session.setAttribute("logintype", "client");
                session.setMaxInactiveInterval(30 * 60); 
                Client responseClient = new Client();
                responseClient.setId(client.getId());
                responseClient.setNike(client.getNike());
                responseClient.setTel(client.getTel());
                responseClient.setStatus(client.getStatus());
                responseClient.setLevelId(client.getLevelId());
                responseClient.setPoint(client.getPoint());
                responseClient.setPower(client.getPower());
                responseClient.setCreatedate(client.getCreatedate());
                return R.ok("登录成功").data("user", responseClient);
            } else {
                return R.error("手机号或密码错误");
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            return R.error("登录失败，请稍后重试");
        }
    }
    @PostMapping("uploadAvatar")
    public ResultInfo uploadAvatar(@RequestParam(value = "id", required = false) String idParam,
                                   @RequestParam(value = "avatar", required = false) MultipartFile avatarFile,
                                   HttpServletRequest request) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (idParam == null || idParam.trim().isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("缺少 id 参数");
            return info;
        }
        Integer id;
        try {
            id = Integer.valueOf(idParam);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("无效的 id 参数");
            return info;
        }
        if (avatarFile == null || avatarFile.isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请上传图片文件");
            return info;
        }
        if (avatarFile.getSize() > avatarMaxSize) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("文件不能超过 " + (avatarMaxSize / 1024) + " KB");
            return info;
        }
        String contentType = avatarFile.getContentType();
        if (contentType == null || !contentType.startsWith("image/")) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("只支持图片文件");
            return info;
        }
        Client client = clientMapper.selectById(id);
        if (client == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("未找到该用户");
            return info;
        }
        HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);
        if (session != null) {
            Object sessObj = session.getAttribute("client");
            if (sessObj instanceof Client) {
                Client sessClient = (Client) sessObj;
            }
        }
        try {
            Path base = Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
            if (!Files.exists(base)) {
                Files.createDirectories(base);
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("服务器文件保存目录不可用");
            return info;
        }
        String original = avatarFile.getOriginalFilename();
        String ext = "";
        if (original != null) {
            int idx = original.lastIndexOf('.');
            if (idx >= 0) ext = original.substring(idx).toLowerCase();
        }
        if (ext.isEmpty()) {
            if ("image/png".equals(contentType)) ext = ".png";
            else if ("image/jpeg".equals(contentType) || "image/jpg".equals(contentType)) ext = ".jpg";
            else ext = ".img";
        }
        String filename = "user_" + id + "_" + System.currentTimeMillis() + ext;
        Path targetFile = Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath().resolve(filename).normalize();
        if (!targetFile.startsWith(Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath().normalize())) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("非法的文件路径");
            return info;
        }
        try {
            avatarFile.transferTo(targetFile.toFile());
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("保存文件失败");
            return info;
        }
        String oldAvatar = null;
        try {
            java.lang.reflect.Method gm = null;
            try {
                gm = client.getClass().getMethod("getAvatar");
            } catch (NoSuchMethodException nsme) {
                gm = null;
            }
            if (gm != null) {
                Object oldObj = gm.invoke(client);
                if (oldObj != null) oldAvatar = oldObj.toString();
            }
        } catch (Exception ignore) {}
        String savedUrl = "/uploads/avatars/" + filename;
        try {
            try {
                java.lang.reflect.Method sm = client.getClass().getMethod("setAvatar", String.class);
                sm.invoke(client, savedUrl);
            } catch (NoSuchMethodException nsme) {
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
        }
        int rows = clientMapper.updateById(client);
        if (rows > 0) {
            if (oldAvatar != null && !oldAvatar.trim().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    Path oldPath = Paths.get(oldAvatar);
                    if (!oldPath.isAbsolute()) {
                        oldPath = Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath().resolve(oldAvatar).normalize();
                    }
                    if (oldPath.startsWith(Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath())) {
                        Files.deleteIfExists(oldPath);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ex) {
                    ex.printStackTrace();
                }
            }
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("上传成功");
            info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(java.util.Collections.singletonMap("avatar", savedUrl)));
        } else {
            try {
                Files.deleteIfExists(targetFile);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                ex.printStackTrace();
            }
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("保存头像信息失败");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("removeAvatar")
    public ResultInfo removeAvatar(@RequestBody Map<String, Object> payload, HttpServletRequest request) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (payload == null || !payload.containsKey("id")) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("缺少 id 参数");
            return info;
        }
        Integer id;
        try {
            Object o = payload.get("id");
            if (o instanceof Number) id = ((Number) o).intValue();
            else id = Integer.valueOf(o.toString());
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("无效的 id 参数");
            return info;
        }
        Client client = clientMapper.selectById(id);
        if (client == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("用户不存在");
            return info;
        }
        String oldAvatar = null;
        try {
            java.lang.reflect.Method gm = null;
            try {
                gm = client.getClass().getMethod("getAvatar");
            } catch (NoSuchMethodException nsme) {
                gm = null;
            }
            if (gm != null) {
                Object v = gm.invoke(client);
                if (v != null) oldAvatar = v.toString();
            }
        } catch (Exception ignore) {}
        try {
            java.lang.reflect.Method sm = client.getClass().getMethod("setAvatar", String.class);
            sm.invoke(client, (String) null);
        } catch (NoSuchMethodException nsme) {
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
        }
        int rows = clientMapper.updateById(client);
        if (rows > 0) {
            if (oldAvatar != null && !oldAvatar.trim().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    Path oldPath = Paths.get(oldAvatar);
                    if (!oldPath.isAbsolute()) {
                        oldPath = Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath().resolve(oldAvatar).normalize();
                    }
                    if (oldPath.startsWith(Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath())) {
                        Files.deleteIfExists(oldPath);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ex) {
                    ex.printStackTrace();
                }
            }
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("已移除头像");
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("移除失败");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("send-code")
    public ResultInfo sendCode(@RequestParam("phone") String phone) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请输入有效的手机号码");
            return info;
        }
        String code = gen6Code();
        codeCache.put(phone, code);
        System.out.println(code);
        info.setCode(0);
        info.setMsg("验证码已生成并发送");
        return info;
    }
    @GetMapping("get")
    public ResultInfo getClientById(@RequestParam(value = "id", required = false) String idParam,
                                    HttpServletRequest request) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (idParam == null || idParam.trim().isEmpty()) {
            HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);
            if (session != null) {
                Object obj = session.getAttribute("client");
                if (obj != null && obj instanceof Client) {
                    Client sClient = (Client) obj;
                    Client fresh = clientMapper.selectById(sClient.getId());
                    if (fresh == null) {
                        info.setCode(1);
                        info.setMsg("未找到会话中的用户");
                        return info;
                    }
                    fresh.setPass(null);
                    info.setCode(0);
                    info.setMsg("success");
                    info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(fresh));
                    return info;
                }
            }
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("缺少 id 参数且未登录");
            return info;
        }
        Client client = null;
        try {
            Integer id = Integer.valueOf(idParam);
            client = clientMapper.selectById(id);
        } catch (NumberFormatException nfe) {
            client = clientMapper.selectOne(new QueryWrapper<Client>().eq("tel", idParam));
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
        }
        if (client == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("用户不存在");
            return info;
        }
        if (client.getIsdel() != null && client.getIsdel() == 1) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("账号已被删除");
            return info;
        }
        if (client.getStatus() != null && client.getStatus() != 0) {
        }
        client.setPass(null);
        info.setCode(0);
        info.setMsg("success");
        info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(client));
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("updatepass")
    public ResultInfo updatePassword(@RequestBody Map<String, String> params, HttpServletRequest request) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        String phone = params.get("phone");
        String oldPass = params.get("pass");
        String newPass = params.get("pass1");
        String confirmPass = params.get("pass2");
        if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请输入有效的手机号码");
            return info;
        }
        if (StringUtils.isEmpty(oldPass)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请输入原密码");
            return info;
        }
        if (StringUtils.isEmpty(newPass)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请输入新密码");
            return info;
        }
        if (StringUtils.isEmpty(confirmPass)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请确认新密码");
            return info;
        }
        if (!newPass.equals(confirmPass)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("两次输入的新密码不一致");
            return info;
        }
        if (newPass.length() < 8 || !newPass.matches("^(?=.*[A-Za-z])(?=.*\\d).+$")) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("新密码至少8位，且必须包含字母和数字");
            return info;
        }
        try {
            Client client = clientMapper.selectOne(new QueryWrapper<Client>().eq("tel", phone));
            if (client == null) {
                info.setCode(1);
                info.setMsg("该手机号未注册");
                return info;
            }
            if (client.getIsdel() != null && client.getIsdel() == 1) {
                info.setCode(1);
                info.setMsg("账号已被删除");
                return info;
            }
            if (client.getStatus() != null && client.getStatus() != 0) {
                info.setCode(1);
                info.setMsg("账号状态异常");
                return info;
            }
            String md5OldPass = DigestUtils.md5Hex(oldPass);
            if (!md5OldPass.equals(client.getPass())) {
                info.setCode(1);
                info.setMsg("原密码不正确");
                return info;
            }
            if (oldPass.equals(newPass)) {
                info.setCode(1);
                info.setMsg("新密码不能与原密码相同");
                return info;
            }
            String md5NewPass = DigestUtils.md5Hex(newPass);
            client.setPass(md5NewPass);
            int rows = clientMapper.updateById(client);
            if (rows > 0) {
                info.setCode(0);
                info.setMsg("密码更新成功");
                HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);
                if (session != null) {
                    session.invalidate();
                }
            } else {
                info.setCode(1);
                info.setMsg("密码更新失败，请稍后重试");
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("系统错误，请稍后重试");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("register")
    public ResultInfo register(@RequestParam(value = "username", required = false) String username,
                               @RequestParam("password") String password,
                               @RequestParam("phone") String phone,
                               @RequestParam("code") String code) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请输入有效的手机号码");
            return info;
        }
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(password)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("密码不能为空");
            return info;
        }
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(code)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请输入验证码");
            return info;
        }
        String cached = codeCache.get(phone);
        if (cached == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("验证码已过期或不存在");
            return info;
        }
        if (!cached.equals(code)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("验证码不正确");
            return info;
        }
        Client existing = clientMapper.selectOne(new com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper<Client>().eq("tel", phone));
        if (existing != null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("该手机号已被注册");
            return info;
        }
        String md5pass = DigestUtils.md5Hex(password);
        Client client = new Client();
        client.setNike(username);
        client.setPass(md5pass);
        client.setTel(phone);
        client.setStatus(0);
        client.setIsdel(0);
        client.setPower(0);
        client.setCreatedate(java.time.LocalDateTime.now().toString());
        int rows = clientMapper.insert(client);
        if (rows > 0) {
            codeCache.remove(phone);
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("注册成功");
            client.setPass(null);
            info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(client));
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("注册失败，请稍后重试");
        }
        return info;
    }
    private String gen6Code() {
        Random random = new Random();
        int v = random.nextInt(900000) + 100000; 
        return String.valueOf(v);
    }
    private boolean isValidPhone(String phone) {
        if (phone == null) return false;
        return phone.matches("^1[3-9]\\d{9}$");
    }
    @GetMapping("index")
    public ResultInfo index(
            @RequestParam(value = "name", required = false) String name,
            @RequestParam(value = "page", required = false, defaultValue = "1") long page,
            @RequestParam(value = "limit", required = false, defaultValue = "10") long limit) {
        QueryWrapper<User> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
        if (name != null && !name.trim().isEmpty()) {
            qw.like("name", name.trim());
        }
        Page<User> pager = new Page<>(page, limit);
        IPage<User> resultPage = mapper.selectPage(pager, qw);
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        info.setCode(0);
        info.setMsg("success");
        info.setCount(resultPage.getTotal());
        info.setData(resultPage.getRecords());
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("insert")
    public ResultInfo insert(User u){
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (u == null || u.getName() == null || u.getName().trim().isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("姓名不能为空");
            return info;
        }
        int rows = mapper.insert(u);
        if (rows > 0) {
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("添加成功");
            info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(u));
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("添加失败");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @GetMapping("delete")
    public ResultInfo delete(@RequestParam("id") int id){
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        int rows = mapper.deleteById(id);
        if (rows > 0) {
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("删除成功");
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("删除失败，ID不存在");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("update")
    public ResultInfo update(@RequestBody Map<String, Object> payload) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (payload == null || payload.isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("请求参数不能为空");
            return info;
        }
        Object idObj = payload.get("id");
        if (idObj == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("缺少 id 参数");
            return info;
        }
        Integer id;
        try {
            if (idObj instanceof Number) {
                id = ((Number) idObj).intValue();
            } else {
                id = Integer.valueOf(idObj.toString());
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("无效的 id 参数");
            return info;
        }
        Client client = clientMapper.selectById(id);
        if (client == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("未找到该用户");
            return info;
        }
        boolean updated = false;
        if (payload.containsKey("nike")) {
            Object val = payload.get("nike");
            if (val != null) {
                client.setNike(val.toString().trim());
                updated = true;
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("sex")) {
            Object val = payload.get("sex");
            if (val == null || String.valueOf(val).trim().isEmpty()) {
                client.setSex(null);
            } else {
                try {
                    client.setSex(Integer.valueOf(val.toString()));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    info.setCode(1);
                    info.setMsg("非法的 sex 值");
                    return info;
                }
                updated = true;
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("tel")) {
            Object val = payload.get("tel");
            if (val != null) {
                client.setTel(val.toString().trim());
                updated = true;
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("tel2")) {
            Object val = payload.get("tel2");
            if (val != null) {
                client.setTel2(val.toString().trim());
                updated = true;
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("qq")) {
            Object val = payload.get("qq");
            if (val != null) {
                client.setQq(val.toString().trim());
                updated = true;
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("weixin")) {
            Object val = payload.get("weixin");
            if (val != null) {
                client.setWeixin(val.toString().trim());
                updated = true;
            }
        }
        if (!updated) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("未提供需要更新的字段");
            return info;
        }
        int rows = clientMapper.updateById(client);
        if (rows > 0) {
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("更新成功");
            info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(client));
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("更新失败");
        }
        return info;
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.config;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.ResourceHandlerRegistry;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.WebMvcConfigurer;
@Configuration
public class StaticResourceConfig implements WebMvcConfigurer {
    @Value("${avatar.baseDir:D:/MedicalButler/uploads/avatars}")
    private String avatarBaseDir;
    @Override
    public void addResourceHandlers(ResourceHandlerRegistry registry) {
        String resourceLocation = "file:" + avatarBaseDir.replace("\\", "/") + "/";
        registry.addResourceHandler("/uploads/avatars/**")
                .addResourceLocations(resourceLocation)
                .setCachePeriod(3600); 
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Card;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Client;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.CardService;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.ClientService;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Random;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/card")
public class CardController {
    @Autowired
    private CardService cardService;
    @Autowired
    private ClientService clientService;
    @PostMapping("/recharge")
    @Transactional
    public Map<String, Object> recharge(@RequestBody Map<String, Object> payload) {
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
        if (payload == null) {
            result.put("code", 400);
            result.put("msg", "请求体不能为空");
            return result;
        }
        Integer clientId = null;
        try {
            Object c = payload.get("clientId");
            if (c != null) {
                if (c instanceof Number) clientId = ((Number) c).intValue();
                else clientId = Integer.valueOf(String.valueOf(c));
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            result.put("code", 400);
            result.put("msg", "clientId 格式错误");
            return result;
        }
        Double amount = null;
        try {
            Object a = payload.get("amount");
            if (a != null) {
                if (a instanceof Number) amount = ((Number) a).doubleValue();
                else amount = Double.valueOf(String.valueOf(a));
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            result.put("code", 400);
            result.put("msg", "amount 格式错误");
            return result;
        }
        if (clientId == null || amount == null || amount <= 0) {
            result.put("code", 400);
            result.put("msg", "参数错误：clientId 与 amount 必须提供且 amount > 0");
            return result;
        }
        Client client = clientService.getById(clientId);
        if (client == null) {
            result.put("code", 404);
            result.put("msg", "找不到客户信息");
            return result;
        }
        Card card;
        boolean isNewCard = false;
        if (client.getCardId() != null) {
            card = cardService.getById(client.getCardId());
            if (card == null) {
                card = createNewCard(clientId, amount);
                isNewCard = true;
            } else {
                card.setCount(card.getCount() + amount);
                card.setTocount(card.getTocount() + amount);
                boolean updated = cardService.updateById(card);
                if (!updated) {
                    result.put("code", 500);
                    result.put("msg", "更新卡信息失败");
                    return result;
                }
            }
        } else {
            card = createNewCard(clientId, amount);
            isNewCard = true;
        }
        if (isNewCard) {
            boolean saved = cardService.save(card);
            if (!saved) {
                result.put("code", 500);
                result.put("msg", "保存卡信息失败");
                return result;
            }
            client.setCardId(card.getId());
        }
        Integer oldPoint = client.getPoint() == null ? 0 : client.getPoint();
        client.setPoint(oldPoint + amount.intValue());
        Integer newLevelId = determineLevelByAccumulated(client.getPoint());
        client.setLevelId(newLevelId);
        boolean updatedClient = clientService.updateById(client);
        if (!updatedClient) {
            throw new RuntimeException("更新 client 失败");
        }
        result.put("code", 0);
        result.put("msg", "充值成功");
        Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("card", card);
        data.put("client", client);
        data.put("isNewCard", isNewCard);
        result.put("data", data);
        return result;
    }
    private Card createNewCard(Integer clientId, Double amount) {
        String code = generate8DigitCode();
        Card card = new Card();
        card.setCode(code);
        card.setCount(amount);
        card.setTocount(amount);
        card.setPrice(amount);
        card.setClientId(clientId);
        card.setStatus(1);
        card.setIsdel(0);
        card.setComplte(1);
        card.setCreatedate(LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")));
        return card;
    }
    private String generate8DigitCode() {
        Random r = new Random();
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            sb.append(r.nextInt(10));
        }
        String code = sb.toString();
        QueryWrapper<Card> queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
        queryWrapper.eq("code", code);
        long count = cardService.count(queryWrapper);
        while (count > 0) {
            sb = new StringBuilder();
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
                sb.append(r.nextInt(10));
            }
            code = sb.toString();
            queryWrapper = new QueryWrapper<>();
            queryWrapper.eq("code", code);
            count = cardService.count(queryWrapper);
        }
        return code;
    }
    @GetMapping("/info")
    public Map<String, Object> getCardInfo(@RequestParam Integer clientId) {
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
        if (clientId == null) {
            result.put("code", 400);
            result.put("msg", "clientId不能为空");
            return result;
        }
        Client client = clientService.getById(clientId);
        if (client == null) {
            result.put("code", 404);
            result.put("msg", "找不到客户信息");
            return result;
        }
        Card card = null;
        if (client.getCardId() != null) {
            card = cardService.getById(client.getCardId());
        }
        result.put("code", 0);
        result.put("msg", "获取成功");
        Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("client", client);
        data.put("card", card);
        result.put("data", data);
        return result;
    }
    private Integer determineLevelByAccumulated(Integer accumulated) {
        if (accumulated == null) return 1;
        if (accumulated >= 20000) return 5;
        if (accumulated >= 10000) return 4;
        if (accumulated >= 5000) return 3;
        if (accumulated >= 2000) return 2;
        return 1;
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.config;
import org.aspectj.lang.ProceedingJoinPoint;
import org.aspectj.lang.annotation.Around;
import org.aspectj.lang.annotation.Aspect;
import org.aspectj.lang.annotation.Pointcut;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.RequestContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.ServletRequestAttributes;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
@Aspect
@Component
public class SessionAspect {
    @Pointcut("execution(* com.syd.medicalbutler.serviceImpl.*.*(..))")
    public void executeService() {}
    @Around("executeService()")
    public Object doAroundAdvice(ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint) throws Throwable {
        long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Object user = null;
        try {
            if (RequestContextHolder.getRequestAttributes() instanceof ServletRequestAttributes) {
                ServletRequestAttributes attrs = (ServletRequestAttributes) RequestContextHolder.getRequestAttributes();
                HttpServletRequest request = attrs.getRequest();
                if (request != null) {
                    HttpSession session = request.getSession(false); 
                    if (session != null) {
                        user = session.getAttribute("user");
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("SessionAspect: 获取 session/user 时发生异常: " + e.getMessage());
        }
        try {
            RequestContext.setCurrentUser(user);
            Object result = joinPoint.proceed();
            return result;
        } finally {
            RequestContext.clear();
            long cost = System.currentTimeMillis() - start;
            System.out.println("SessionAspect - method: " + joinPoint.getSignature() + " executed in " + cost + "ms");
        }
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.ResultData;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.ResultInfo;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.SearchInfo;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.BaseService;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.ParameterizedType;
import java.lang.reflect.Type;
import java.util.List;
public class BaseController<T> {
    @Autowired
    BaseService<T> myservice;
    private BaseService<T> getService() throws NoSuchFieldException, IllegalAccessException {
        return myservice;
    }
    private  Class<T> getTClass(){
        Class<?> subclass = this.getClass();
        Type genericSuperclass = subclass.getGenericSuperclass();
        if (genericSuperclass instanceof ParameterizedType) {
            ParameterizedType pt = (ParameterizedType) genericSuperclass;
            return (Class<T>) pt.getActualTypeArguments()[0];
        }
        return null;
    }
    @RequestMapping("getArray")
    public String[]   getArray(String name) throws Exception {
        Class<T> cls=getTClass();
       Field f= cls.getField(name);
       return (String[])f.get(null);
    }
    @RequestMapping("getAll")
    public List<T> getAll() throws  Exception{
        SearchInfo info=new SearchInfo();
        info.setLimit(100000l);
        ResultInfo result= getService().select(info,null);
        return result.getData();
    }
    @RequestMapping("select")
    public ResultInfo select(SearchInfo sea) throws  Exception{
        return getService().select(sea,null);
    }
    @RequestMapping("insert")
    public ResultData insert(T t)throws Exception{
        getService().insert(t);
        return new ResultData(1);
    }
    @RequestMapping("update")
    public ResultData update(T t)throws Exception{
        getService().update(t);
        return new ResultData(1);
    }
    @RequestMapping("delete")
    public ResultData delete(int id)throws Exception{
        getService().delete(id);
        return new ResultData(1);
    }
    @RequestMapping("get")
    public T get(int id)throws Exception{
       return getService().getById(id);
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.config;
public final class RequestContext {
    private RequestContext() {}
    private static final ThreadLocal<Object> CURRENT_USER = new ThreadLocal<>();
    public static void setCurrentUser(Object user) {
        CURRENT_USER.set(user);
    }
    public static Object getCurrentUser() {
        return CURRENT_USER.get();
    }
    public static void clear() {
        CURRENT_USER.remove();
    }
}
