package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.metadata.IPage;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.ResultInfo;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.ProjectItem;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.ProjectItemService;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.*;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ProjectItemController {
    private final ProjectItemService itemService;
    @Value("${avatar.baseDir:D:/MedicalButler/uploads/avatars}")
    private String avatarBaseDir;
    @Value("${avatar.max-size:2097152}")
    private long avatarMaxSize;
    @GetMapping("/items")
    public ResultInfo list(
            @RequestParam(value = "page", defaultValue = "1") long page,
            @RequestParam(value = "size", defaultValue = "10") long size,
            @RequestParam(value = "name", required = false) String name
    ) {
        ResultInfo r = new ResultInfo();
        try {
            Page<ProjectItem> pg = new Page<>(page, size);
            LambdaQueryWrapper<ProjectItem> qw = new LambdaQueryWrapper<>();
            qw.eq(ProjectItem::getIsdel, 0);
            if (StringUtils.hasText(name)) {
                qw.like(ProjectItem::getName, name);
            }
            qw.orderByAsc(ProjectItem::getId);
            IPage<ProjectItem> result = itemService.page(pg, qw);
            r.setCode(0);
            r.setMsg("ok");
            r.setCount(result.getTotal());
            r.setData(result.getRecords());
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("查询失败: " + ex.getMessage());
            r.setCount(0);
            r.setData(Collections.emptyList());
        }
        return r;
    }
    @GetMapping("/items/{id}")
    public ResultInfo getById(@PathVariable("id") Integer id) {
        ResultInfo r = new ResultInfo();
        try {
            ProjectItem item = itemService.getById(id);
            if (item == null || (item.getIsdel() != null && item.getIsdel() == 1)) {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("未找到记录");
                r.setData(Collections.emptyList());
                r.setCount(0);
            } else {
                r.setCode(0);
                r.setMsg("ok");
                r.setData(Collections.singletonList(item));
                r.setCount(1);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("查询失败: " + ex.getMessage());
            r.setData(Collections.emptyList());
            r.setCount(0);
        }
        return r;
    }
    @PostMapping("/items")
    public ResultInfo create(@RequestBody ProjectItem item) {
        ResultInfo r = new ResultInfo();
        try {
            item.setId(null);
            if (item.getIsdel() == null) item.setIsdel(0);
            boolean ok = itemService.save(item);
            if (ok) {
                r.setCode(0);
                r.setMsg("新增成功");
                r.setData(Collections.singletonList(item));
                r.setCount(1);
            } else {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("新增失败");
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("新增异常: " + ex.getMessage());
        }
        return r;
    }
    @PutMapping("/items/{id}")
    public ResultInfo update(@PathVariable("id") Integer id, @RequestBody ProjectItem item) {
        ResultInfo r = new ResultInfo();
        try {
            ProjectItem exist = itemService.getById(id);
            if (exist == null || (exist.getIsdel() != null && exist.getIsdel() == 1)) {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("记录不存在");
                return r;
            }
            item.setId(id);
            boolean ok = itemService.updateById(item);
            if (ok) {
                r.setCode(0);
                r.setMsg("更新成功");
                r.setData(Collections.singletonList(itemService.getById(id)));
                r.setCount(1);
            } else {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("更新失败");
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("更新异常: " + ex.getMessage());
        }
        return r;
    }
    @DeleteMapping("/items/{id}")
    public ResultInfo delete(@PathVariable("id") Integer id) {
        ResultInfo r = new ResultInfo();
        try {
            ProjectItem exist = itemService.getById(id);
            if (exist == null || (exist.getIsdel() != null && exist.getIsdel() == 1)) {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("记录不存在");
                return r;
            }
            exist.setIsdel(1);
            boolean ok = itemService.updateById(exist);
            if (ok) {
                r.setCode(0);
                r.setMsg("删除成功");
            } else {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("删除失败");
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("删除异常: " + ex.getMessage());
        }
        return r;
    }
    @PostMapping("/items/batch-delete")
    public ResultInfo batchDelete(@RequestBody IdsDto idsDto) {
        ResultInfo r = new ResultInfo();
        if (idsDto == null || idsDto.getIds() == null || idsDto.getIds().isEmpty()) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("参数错误");
            return r;
        }
        try {
            List<Integer> ids = idsDto.getIds();
            List<ProjectItem> list = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Integer id : ids) {
                ProjectItem pi = new ProjectItem();
                pi.setId(id);
                pi.setIsdel(1);
                list.add(pi);
            }
            boolean ok = itemService.updateBatchById(list);
            if (ok) {
                r.setCode(0);
                r.setMsg("删除成功");
            } else {
                r.setCode(1);
                r.setMsg("删除失败");
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            r.setCode(1);
            r.setMsg("删除异常: " + ex.getMessage());
        }
        return r;
    }
    @PostMapping(value = "/upload", consumes = MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA_VALUE)
    public Map<String, Object> upload(@RequestParam("file") MultipartFile file) {
        Map<String, Object> res = new HashMap<>();
        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {
            res.put("code", 1);
            res.put("msg", "未上传文件");
            return res;
        }
        if (file.getSize() > avatarMaxSize) {
            res.put("code", 1);
            res.put("msg", "文件不能超过 " + (avatarMaxSize / 1024) + " KB");
            return res;
        }
        String contentType = file.getContentType();
        if (contentType == null || !contentType.startsWith("image/")) {
            res.put("code", 1);
            res.put("msg", "只支持图片文件");
            return res;
        }
        try {
            Path base = Paths.get(avatarBaseDir).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
            if (!base.isAbsolute()) {
                base = Paths.get(System.getProperty("user.dir")).resolve(base).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
            }
            if (!Files.exists(base)) {
                Files.createDirectories(base);
            }
            String original = file.getOriginalFilename();
            String ext = "";
            if (original != null) {
                int idx = original.lastIndexOf('.');
                if (idx >= 0) ext = original.substring(idx).toLowerCase();
            }
            if (ext.isEmpty()) {
                if ("image/png".equals(contentType)) ext = ".png";
                else if ("image/jpeg".equals(contentType) || "image/jpg".equals(contentType)) ext = ".jpg";
                else ext = ".img";
            }
            String filename = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmssSSS"))
                    + "_" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().replaceAll("-", "").substring(0, 6) + ext;
            Path target = base.resolve(filename).normalize();
            if (!target.startsWith(base)) {
                res.put("code", 1);
                res.put("msg", "非法的文件路径");
                return res;
            }
            file.transferTo(target.toFile());
            String url = "/uploads/avatars/" + filename;
            res.put("code", 0);
            res.put("msg", "上传成功");
            res.put("url", url);
            Map<String, String> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("url", url);
            data.put("path", url);
            res.put("data", data);
            return res;
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            res.put("code", 1);
            res.put("msg", "保存失败: " + ex.getMessage());
            return res;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            res.put("code", 1);
            res.put("msg", "上传异常: " + ex.getMessage());
            return res;
        }
    }
    @Data
    public static class IdsDto {
        private List<Integer> ids;
    }
}

}

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/project")
public class ProjectController {
    @Autowired
    private ProjectService projectService;
    @Value("${avatar.baseDir:D:/MedicalButler/uploads/avatars}")
    private String avatarBaseDir;
    @Value("${avatar.max-size:2097152}")
    private long avatarMaxSize;
    @GetMapping("/list")
    public ResultInfo getProjectList(
            @RequestParam(value = "page", defaultValue = "1") Integer page,
            @RequestParam(value = "size", defaultValue = "10") Integer size,
            @RequestParam(value = "name", required = false) String name,
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) Integer status) {
        try {
            if (page < 1) page = 1;
            if (size < 1) size = 10;
            if (size > 100) size = 100; 
            List<ProjectDetailVO> projectList = projectService.getProjectDetailList(name, status, page, size);
            int totalCount = projectService.countProjects(name, status);
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            result.setCode(0);
            result.setMsg("success");
            result.setCount(totalCount);
            result.setData(projectList);
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            result.setCode(500);
            result.setMsg("获取项目列表失败: " + e.getMessage());
            return result;
        }
    }

        }
    }
    @PostMapping
    public ResultInfo createProject(@RequestBody ProjectDetailVO projectDetail) {
        try {
            boolean success = projectService.createProject(projectDetail);
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            if (success) {
                result.setCode(0);
                result.setMsg("创建成功");
            } else {
                result.setCode(400);
                result.setMsg("创建失败");
            }
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            result.setCode(500);
            result.setMsg("创建项目失败: " + e.getMessage());
            return result;
        }
    }
    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public ResultInfo updateProject(@PathVariable Integer id, @RequestBody ProjectDetailVO projectDetail) {
        try {
            projectDetail.setId(id);
            boolean success = projectService.updateProject(projectDetail);
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            if (success) {
                result.setCode(0);
                result.setMsg("更新成功");
            } else {
                result.setCode(400);
                result.setMsg("更新失败");
            }
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            result.setCode(500);
            result.setMsg("更新项目失败: " + e.getMessage());
            return result;
        }
    }
    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResultInfo deleteProject(@PathVariable Integer id) {
        try {
            boolean success = projectService.deleteProject(id);
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            if (success) {
                result.setCode(0);
                result.setMsg("删除成功");
            } else {
                result.setCode(400);
                result.setMsg("删除失败");
            }
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {

            result.setMsg("删除项目失败: " + e.getMessage());
            return result;
        }
    }
    @PostMapping("/batch-delete")
    public ResultInfo batchDeleteProject(@RequestBody BatchIdsRequest request) {

            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            if (success) {
                result.setCode(0);
                result.setMsg("批量删除成功");
            } else {
                result.setCode(400);

        }
    }
    @PostMapping("/batch-status")
    public ResultInfo batchUpdateStatus(@RequestBody BatchStatusRequest request) {
        try {
            boolean success = projectService.batchUpdateStatus(request.getIds(), request.getStatus());
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            if (success) {
                result.setCode(0);
                result.setMsg("批量更新状态成功");
            } else {
                result.setCode(400);
                result.setMsg("批量更新状态失败");
            }
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            result.setCode(500);
            result.setMsg("批量更新状态失败: " + e.getMessage());
            return result;
        }
    }
    @PostMapping("/{id}/status")
    public ResultInfo updateStatus(@PathVariable Integer id, @RequestBody Map<String, Integer> payload) {
        try {
            Integer status = payload != null ? payload.get("status") : null;
            if (status == null) {
                ResultInfo resultNull = new ResultInfo();
                resultNull.setCode(400);
                resultNull.setMsg("更新状态失败: status 为空");
                return resultNull;
            }
            boolean success = projectService.updateStatus(id, status);
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            if (success) {
                result.setCode(0);
                result.setMsg("更新状态成功");
            } else {
                result.setCode(400);
                result.setMsg("更新状态失败");
            }
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            ResultInfo result = new ResultInfo();
            result.setCode(500);
            result.setMsg("更新状态失败: " + e.getMessage());
            return result;
        }
    }
    public static class BatchIdsRequest {
        private List<Integer> ids;
        public List<Integer> getIds() {
            return ids;
        }
        public void setIds(List<Integer> ids) {
            this.ids = ids;
        }
    }
    public static class BatchStatusRequest {
        private List<Integer> ids;
        private Integer status;
        public List<Integer> getIds() {
            return ids;
        }
        public void setIds(List<Integer> ids) {
            this.ids = ids;
        }
        public Integer getStatus() {
            return status;
        }
        public void setStatus(Integer status) {
            this.status = status;
        }
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Card;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Payinfo;
import lombok.Data;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.CardService;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.PayinfoService;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.*;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/pay")
public class PayController {
    @Autowired
    private PayinfoService payinfoService;
    @Autowired
    private CardService cardService;
    private static final DateTimeFormatter DT_FMT = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    @PostMapping("/purchase")
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public ApiResponse purchase(@RequestBody PurchaseRequest req) {
        if (req == null || req.getCart() == null || req.getCart().isEmpty()) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("购物车为空");
        }
        if (req.getPayMode() == null || (!"online".equals(req.getPayMode()) && !"card".equals(req.getPayMode()))) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("不支持的支付方式");
        }
        double total = req.getCart().stream()
                .mapToDouble(it -> (it.getPrice() == null ? 0d : it.getPrice()) * (it.getQty() == null ? 0 : it.getQty()))
                .sum();
        String now = LocalDateTime.now().format(DT_FMT);
        Integer clientId = req.getClientId();
        if ("card".equals(req.getPayMode())) {
            String cardCode = req.getCardCode();
            if (cardCode == null || cardCode.trim().isEmpty()) {
                return ApiResponse.fail("请提供卡号");
            }
            QueryWrapper<Card> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
            qw.eq("code", cardCode.trim()).eq("isdel", 0);
            Card card = cardService.getOne(qw);
            if (card == null) {
                return ApiResponse.fail("卡号不存在");
            }
            if (card.getStatus() == null || card.getStatus() != 1) {
                return ApiResponse.fail("卡不可用");
            }
            double balance = card.getCount() == null ? 0d : card.getCount();
            if (balance < total) {
                return ApiResponse.fail("卡余额不足");
            }
            card.setCount(balance - total);
            card.setComplte(1);
            cardService.updateById(card);
            List<Payinfo> inserted = new ArrayList<>();
            for (PurchaseItem it : req.getCart()) {
                Payinfo p = new Payinfo();
                p.setDate(now);
                p.setClientId(clientId);
                p.setProjectId(it.getProjectId());
                p.setPrice(it.getPrice() * it.getQty());
                p.setPaystatus(1);
                p.setIsdel(0);
                payinfoService.save(p);
                inserted.add(p);
            }
            if (!inserted.isEmpty()) {
                card.setPayinfoId(inserted.get(0).getId());
                cardService.updateById(card);
            }
            Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("total", total);
            data.put("payMode","card");
            data.put("cardCode", cardCode);
            data.put("payRecords", inserted);
            return ApiResponse.ok(data);
        }
        if ("online".equals(req.getPayMode())) {
            List<Payinfo> inserted = new ArrayList<>();
            for (PurchaseItem it : req.getCart()) {
                Payinfo p = new Payinfo();
                p.setDate(now);
                p.setClientId(clientId);
                p.setProjectId(it.getProjectId());
                p.setPrice(it.getPrice() * it.getQty());
                p.setPaystatus(1);
                p.setIsdel(0);
                payinfoService.save(p);
                inserted.add(p);
            }
            Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("total", total);
            data.put("payMode","online");
            data.put("payRecords", inserted);
            return ApiResponse.ok(data);
        }
        return ApiResponse.fail("未知错误");
    }
    @Data
    public static class PurchaseRequest {
        private List<PurchaseItem> cart;
        private String buyType; 
        private Integer clientId;
        private String clientTel;
        private String payMode; 
        private String cardCode;
        private String comments;
    }
    @Data
    public static class PurchaseItem {
        private Integer projectId;
        private String name;
        private Double price;
        private Integer qty;
    }
    @Data
    public static class ApiResponse {
        private int code; 
        private String message;
        private Object data;
        public static ApiResponse ok(Object data) {
            ApiResponse r = new ApiResponse();
            r.setCode(0);
            r.setMessage("ok");
            r.setData(data);
            return r;
        }
        public static ApiResponse ok() { return ok(null); }
        public static ApiResponse fail(String msg) {
            ApiResponse r = new ApiResponse();
            r.setCode(-1);
            r.setMessage(msg);
            r.setData(null);
            return r;
        }
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.service;

package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.metadata.IPage;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ClientMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.OrderDetailsMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.OrderMainMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ProjectItemMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ProjectMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ReserverMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Client;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.OrderDetails;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.OrderMain;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Project;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.ProjectItem;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/order")
public class OrderController {
    @Autowired
    private OrderMainMapper orderMainMapper;
    @Autowired
    private OrderDetailsMapper orderDetailsMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectMapper projectMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectItemMapper projectItemMapper;
    @Autowired(required = false)
    private ReserverMapper reserverMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ClientMapper clientMapper;
    @GetMapping
    public Map<String, Object> list(
            @RequestParam(value = "page", required = false, defaultValue = "1") Integer page,
            @RequestParam(value = "size", required = false, defaultValue = "12") Integer size,
            @RequestParam(value = "q", required = false) String q,
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(value = "range", required = false) String range
    ) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        try {
            Page<OrderMain> pager = new Page<>(page, size);
            QueryWrapper<OrderMain> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
            qw.eq("isdel", 0);
            if (!StringUtils.isEmpty(q)) {
                qw.and(w -> w.like("info", q).or().like("date", q).or().eq("user_id", q));
            }
            IPage<OrderMain> pageResult = orderMainMapper.selectPage(pager, qw);
            List<Map<String, Object>> records = new ArrayList<>();
            for (OrderMain m : pageResult.getRecords()) {
                Map<String, Object> row = new HashMap<>();
                row.put("id", m.getId());
                row.put("main_id", m.getId());
                Client client = clientMapper.selectById(m.getClientId());
                row.put("clientName", client.getNike());
                row.put("date", m.getDate());
                row.put("info", m.getInfo());
                row.put("comment", m.getInfo());
                row.put("total", null);
                row.put("status", "paid"); 
                String itemName = "";
                if (m.getProjectId() != null) {
                    Project p = projectMapper.selectById(m.getProjectId());
                    if (p != null) itemName = p.getName();
                }
                row.put("item_name", itemName);
                records.add(row);
            }
            Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("total", pageResult.getTotal());
            data.put("records", records);
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "ok");
            resp.put("data", data);
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "服务器错误：" + ex.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public Map<String, Object> getOrder(@PathVariable("id") Integer id) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        try {
            OrderMain main = orderMainMapper.selectById(id);
            if (main == null) {
                resp.put("code", 1);
                resp.put("msg", "订单不存在");
                return resp;
            }
            Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("id", main.getId());
            data.put("main_id", main.getId());
            data.put("date", main.getDate());
            data.put("info", main.getInfo());
            data.put("project_id", main.getProjectId());
            if (main.getClientId() != null) {
                Client client = clientMapper.selectById(main.getClientId());
                if (client != null) {
                    data.put("clientId", client.getId());
                    data.put("clientName", client.getNike());
                    data.put("clientStatus", client.getStatus());
                }
            }
            Integer projectId = main.getProjectId();
            List<ProjectItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
            if (projectId != null) {
                Project project = projectMapper.selectById(projectId);
                if (project != null) {
                    data.put("project_name", project.getName());
                    String itemIdsStr = project.getItemIds();
                    if (!StringUtils.isEmpty(itemIdsStr)) {
                        String normalized = itemIdsStr.trim().replaceAll("；", ",").replaceAll("\\s+", "");
                        String[] parts = normalized.split(",");
                        List<Integer> ids = Arrays.stream(parts)
                                .filter(s -> s != null && !s.isEmpty())
                                .map(s -> {
                                    try { return Integer.parseInt(s); } catch (Exception ex) { return null; }
                                })
                                .filter(Objects::nonNull).collect(Collectors.toList());
                        if (!ids.isEmpty()) {
                            items = projectItemMapper.selectBatchIds(ids);
                            Map<Integer, ProjectItem> map = items.stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(ProjectItem::getId, pi -> pi));
                            List<ProjectItem> ordered = new ArrayList<>();
                            for (Integer iid : ids) {
                                if (map.containsKey(iid)) ordered.add(map.get(iid));
                            }
                            items = ordered;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            List<Map<String, Object>> itemDtos = items.stream().map(pi -> {
                Map<String, Object> m = new HashMap<>();
                m.put("id", pi.getId());
                m.put("name", pi.getName());
                m.put("info", pi.getInfo());
                m.put("point", pi.getPoint());
                m.put("pic", pi.getPic());
                return m;
            }).collect(Collectors.toList());
            data.put("items", itemDtos);
            List<OrderDetails> details = orderDetailsMapper.selectList(new QueryWrapper<OrderDetails>().eq("main_id", main.getId()).eq("isdel", 0));
            List<Map<String, Object>> detailsOut = details.stream().map(d -> {
                Map<String, Object> mm = new HashMap<>();
                mm.put("id", d.getId());
                mm.put("mainId", d.getMainId());
                mm.put("date", d.getDate());
                mm.put("info", d.getInfo());
                mm.put("itemId", d.getItemId());
                mm.put("status", d.getStatus());
                mm.put("userId", d.getUserId());
                mm.put("isdel", d.getIsdel());
                mm.put("comment", d.getComment());
                return mm;
            }).collect(Collectors.toList());
            data.put("details", detailsOut);
            data.put("item_name", (projectMapper.selectById(main.getProjectId()) != null) ? projectMapper.selectById(main.getProjectId()).getName() : "");
            data.put("total", null);
            data.put("status", "paid");
            data.put("comment", main.getInfo());
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "ok");
            resp.put("data", data);
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "服务器错误：" + ex.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
    public static class SaveOrderRequest {
        public Integer mainId;
        public String info;
        public List<DetailUpdate> details;
        public static class DetailUpdate {
            public Integer id;
            public Integer itemId;
            public String comment;
        }
    }
    @PostMapping("/{id}/save")
    @Transactional
    public Map<String, Object> saveOrder(@PathVariable("id") Integer id, @RequestBody SaveOrderRequest body) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        if (body == null || body.mainId == null) {
            resp.put("code", 1);
            resp.put("msg", "参数错误：缺少 mainId");
            return resp;
        }
        try {
            OrderMain main = orderMainMapper.selectById(body.mainId);
            if (main == null) {
                resp.put("code", 2);
                resp.put("msg", "主订单不存在");
                return resp;
            }
            if (body.info != null) {
                main.setInfo(body.info);
                orderMainMapper.updateById(main);
            }
            if (body.details != null && !body.details.isEmpty()) {
                List<OrderDetails> existList = orderDetailsMapper.selectList(new QueryWrapper<OrderDetails>().eq("main_id", main.getId()).eq("isdel", 0));
                Map<Integer, OrderDetails> existById = existList.stream().filter(od -> od.getId() != null).collect(Collectors.toMap(OrderDetails::getId, od -> od));
                Map<Integer, OrderDetails> existByItem = existList.stream().filter(od -> od.getItemId() != null).collect(Collectors.toMap(OrderDetails::getItemId, od -> od, (a,b)->a));
                for (SaveOrderRequest.DetailUpdate du : body.details) {
                    if (du == null) continue;
                    String comment = du.comment;
                    if (du.id != null) {
                        OrderDetails od = existById.get(du.id);
                        if (od != null) {
                            od.setComment(comment);
                            orderDetailsMapper.updateById(od);
                            continue;
                        }
                    }
                    if (du.itemId != null) {
                        OrderDetails odByItem = existByItem.get(du.itemId);
                        if (odByItem != null) {
                            odByItem.setComment(comment);
                            orderDetailsMapper.updateById(odByItem);
                        } else {
                            OrderDetails newOd = new OrderDetails();
                            newOd.setMainId(main.getId());
                            newOd.setItemId(du.itemId);
                            newOd.setComment(comment);
                            newOd.setDate(new java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss").format(new Date()));
                            newOd.setIsdel(0);
                            orderDetailsMapper.insert(newOd);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "保存成功");
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "保存失败：" + ex.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
    @PostMapping("/{id}/status")
    @Transactional
    public Map<String, Object> changeStatus(@PathVariable("id") Integer id, @RequestBody Map<String, Object> body) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        try {
            if (body == null || !body.containsKey("status")) {
                resp.put("code", 1);
                resp.put("msg", "缺少 status");
                return resp;
            }
            Integer statusVal;
            try {
                statusVal = Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(body.get("status")));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                resp.put("code", 2);
                resp.put("msg", "status 必须为整数（0..3）");
                return resp;
            }
            if (statusVal < 0 || statusVal > 3) {
                resp.put("code", 3);
                resp.put("msg", "status 值超出范围（0..3）");
                return resp;
            }
            OrderMain main = orderMainMapper.selectById(id);
            if (main == null) {
                resp.put("code", 4);
                resp.put("msg", "订单不存在");
                return resp;
            }
            Integer clientId = main.getClientId();
            if (clientId == null) {
                resp.put("code", 5);
                resp.put("msg", "订单未绑定用户（client_id 为空）");
                return resp;
            }
            Client client = clientMapper.selectById(clientId);
            if (client == null) {
                resp.put("code", 6);
                resp.put("msg", "对应用户不存在");
                return resp;
            }
            client.setStatus(statusVal);
            clientMapper.updateById(client);
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "修改用户状态成功");
            resp.put("data", Collections.singletonMap("clientStatus", statusVal));
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "修改失败：" + ex.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public Map<String, Object> delete(@PathVariable("id") Integer id) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        try {
            OrderMain main = orderMainMapper.selectById(id);
            if (main == null) {
                resp.put("code", 1);
                resp.put("msg", "订单不存在");
                return resp;
            }
            main.setIsdel(1);
            orderMainMapper.updateById(main);
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "删除成功");
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "删除失败：" + ex.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
    @PostMapping("/export")
    public Map<String, Object> export(@RequestParam Map<String, String> params) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        resp.put("code", 0);
        resp.put("msg", "导出任务已创建（后端需实现实际导出逻辑）");
        resp.put("data", null);
        return resp;
    }
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ClientMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.OrderDetailsMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.OrderMainMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ProjectItemMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.ProjectMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Client;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.OrderDetails;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.OrderMain;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Project;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.ProjectItem;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/report")
public class ReportController {
    @Autowired
    private OrderMainMapper orderMainMapper;
    @Autowired
    private OrderDetailsMapper orderDetailsMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ClientMapper clientMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectMapper projectMapper;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectItemMapper projectItemMapper;
    @GetMapping("/order-detail")
    public Map<String, Object> orderDetailList(@RequestParam("userId") Integer userId,
                                               @RequestParam(value = "page", required = false) Integer page,
                                               @RequestParam(value = "size", required = false) Integer size) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        try {
            if (userId == null) {
                resp.put("code", 400);
                resp.put("msg", "缺少 userId 参数");
                resp.put("data", Collections.emptyMap());
                return resp;
            }
            QueryWrapper<OrderMain> countQw = new QueryWrapper<>();
            countQw.eq("client_id", userId).eq("isdel", 0);
            int totalCount = Math.toIntExact(orderMainMapper.selectCount(countQw));
            QueryWrapper<OrderMain> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
            qw.eq("client_id", userId).eq("isdel", 0).orderByDesc("id");
            List<OrderMain> mains;
            int respPage = 1;
            int respSize;
            if (page != null && size != null && page > 0 && size > 0) {
                respPage = page;
                respSize = size;
                int offset = (page - 1) * size;
                if (totalCount == 0) {
                    Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
                    data.put("total", 0);
                    data.put("page", respPage);
                    data.put("size", respSize);
                    data.put("orders", Collections.emptyList());
                    resp.put("code", 0);
                    resp.put("msg", "ok");
                    resp.put("data", data);
                    return resp;
                }
                qw.last(" LIMIT " + offset + "," + size);
                mains = orderMainMapper.selectList(qw);
            } else {
                mains = orderMainMapper.selectList(qw);
                respSize = mains == null ? 0 : mains.size();
            }
            if (mains == null || mains.isEmpty()) {
                Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
                data.put("total", totalCount);
                data.put("page", respPage);
                data.put("size", respSize);
                data.put("orders", Collections.emptyList());
                resp.put("code", 0);
                resp.put("msg", "ok");
                resp.put("data", data);
                return resp;
            }
            List<Integer> mainIds = mains.stream().map(OrderMain::getId).collect(Collectors.toList());
            Set<Integer> projectIds = mains.stream().map(OrderMain::getProjectId).filter(Objects::nonNull).collect(Collectors.toSet());
            Set<Integer> clientIds = mains.stream().map(OrderMain::getClientId).filter(Objects::nonNull).collect(Collectors.toSet());
            List<OrderDetails> allDetails = new ArrayList<>();
            if (!mainIds.isEmpty()) {
                allDetails = orderDetailsMapper.selectList(new QueryWrapper<OrderDetails>()
                        .in("main_id", mainIds)
                        .eq("isdel", 0));
            }
            Map<Integer, List<OrderDetails>> detailsByMain = new HashMap<>();
            if (allDetails != null) {
                detailsByMain = allDetails.stream().collect(Collectors.groupingBy(OrderDetails::getMainId));
            }
            Set<Integer> needItemIds = new LinkedHashSet<>();
            Map<Integer, Project> projectById = new HashMap<>();
            if (!projectIds.isEmpty()) {
                for (Integer pid : projectIds) {
                    if (pid == null) continue;
                    Project p = projectMapper.selectById(pid);
                    if (p != null) {
                        projectById.put(pid, p);
                        String itemIds = p.getItemIds();
                        if (itemIds != null && !itemIds.trim().isEmpty()) {
                            Arrays.stream(itemIds.split(","))
                                    .map(String::trim)
                                    .filter(s -> !s.isEmpty())
                                    .map(Integer::valueOf)
                                    .forEach(needItemIds::add);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            List<ProjectItem> allItems = new ArrayList<>();
            Map<Integer, ProjectItem> itemById = new HashMap<>();
            if (!needItemIds.isEmpty()) {
                allItems = projectItemMapper.selectBatchIds(new ArrayList<>(needItemIds));
                if (allItems != null) {
                    itemById = allItems.stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(ProjectItem::getId, it -> it));
                }
            }
            Map<Integer, Client> clientById = new HashMap<>();
            if (!clientIds.isEmpty()) {
                for (Integer cid : clientIds) {
                    if (cid == null) continue;
                    Client cl = clientMapper.selectById(cid);
                    if (cl != null) clientById.put(cid, cl);
                }
            }
            List<Map<String, Object>> ordersOut = new ArrayList<>();
            for (OrderMain m : mains) {
                Map<String, Object> o = new HashMap<>();
                o.put("id", m.getId());
                o.put("main_id", m.getId());
                o.put("date", m.getDate());
                o.put("info", m.getInfo());
                o.put("project_id", m.getProjectId());
                o.put("client_id", m.getClientId());
                String clientName = "";
                if (m.getClientId() != null && clientById.containsKey(m.getClientId())) {
                    Client c = clientById.get(m.getClientId());
                    clientName = (c != null) ? c.getNike() : "";
                }
                o.put("clientName", clientName);
                String projectName = null;
                List<Map<String, Object>> itemOut = new ArrayList<>();
                if (m.getProjectId() != null && projectById.containsKey(m.getProjectId())) {
                    Project p = projectById.get(m.getProjectId());
                    projectName = p != null ? p.getName() : null;
                    String itemIds = p.getItemIds();
                    if (itemIds != null && !itemIds.trim().isEmpty()) {
                        List<Integer> ids = Arrays.stream(itemIds.split(","))
                                .map(String::trim)
                                .filter(s -> !s.isEmpty())
                                .map(Integer::valueOf)
                                .collect(Collectors.toList());
                        for (Integer iid : ids) {
                            ProjectItem pit = itemById.get(iid);
                            if (pit != null) {
                                Map<String, Object> mm = new HashMap<>();
                                mm.put("id", pit.getId());
                                mm.put("name", pit.getName());
                                mm.put("info", pit.getInfo());
                                mm.put("pic", pit.getPic());
                                itemOut.add(mm);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                o.put("project_name", projectName);
                o.put("items", itemOut);
                List<OrderDetails> ds = detailsByMain.getOrDefault(m.getId(), Collections.emptyList());
                List<Map<String, Object>> detailOut = ds.stream().map(d -> {
                    Map<String, Object> mm = new HashMap<>();
                    mm.put("id", d.getId());
                    mm.put("mainId", d.getMainId());
                    mm.put("date", d.getDate());
                    mm.put("info", d.getInfo());
                    mm.put("itemId", d.getItemId());
                    mm.put("status", d.getStatus());
                    mm.put("comment", d.getComment());
                    return mm;
                }).collect(Collectors.toList());
                o.put("details", detailOut);
                ordersOut.add(o);
            }
            Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("total", totalCount);
            data.put("page", respPage);
            data.put("size", respSize);
            data.put("orders", ordersOut);
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "ok");
            resp.put("data", data);
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "服务器错误：" + e.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
    @GetMapping("/order-detail/{mainId}")
    public Map<String, Object> orderDetailByMain(@PathVariable("mainId") Integer mainId) {
        Map<String, Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
        try {
            if (mainId == null) {
                resp.put("code", 400);
                resp.put("msg", "缺少 mainId");
                resp.put("data", Collections.emptyMap());
                return resp;
            }
            OrderMain main = orderMainMapper.selectById(mainId);
            if (main == null) {
                resp.put("code", 0);
                resp.put("msg", "未找到主订单");
                resp.put("data", Collections.emptyMap());
                return resp;
            }
            Client client = null;
            if (main.getClientId() != null) client = clientMapper.selectById(main.getClientId());
            String clientName = (client != null) ? client.getNike() : "";
            String projectName = null;
            List<ProjectItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
            if (main.getProjectId() != null) {
                Project p = projectMapper.selectById(main.getProjectId());
                if (p != null) {
                    projectName = p.getName();
                    String itemIds = p.getItemIds();
                    if (itemIds != null && !itemIds.trim().isEmpty()) {
                        List<Integer> ids = Arrays.stream(itemIds.split(","))
                                .map(String::trim)
                                .filter(s -> !s.isEmpty())
                                .map(Integer::valueOf)
                                .collect(Collectors.toList());
                        if (!ids.isEmpty()) {
                            items = projectItemMapper.selectBatchIds(ids);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            List<OrderDetails> details = orderDetailsMapper.selectList(new QueryWrapper<OrderDetails>()
                    .eq("main_id", main.getId())
                    .eq("isdel", 0));
            List<Map<String, Object>> detailOut = details.stream().map(d -> {
                Map<String, Object> m = new HashMap<>();
                m.put("id", d.getId());
                m.put("mainId", d.getMainId());
                m.put("date", d.getDate());
                m.put("info", d.getInfo());
                m.put("itemId", d.getItemId());
                m.put("status", d.getStatus());
                m.put("comment", d.getComment());
                return m;
            }).collect(Collectors.toList());
            List<Map<String, Object>> itemOut = items.stream().map(it -> {
                Map<String, Object> mm = new HashMap<>();
                mm.put("id", it.getId());
                mm.put("name", it.getName());
                mm.put("info", it.getInfo());
                mm.put("pic", it.getPic());
                return mm;
            }).collect(Collectors.toList());
            Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("id", main.getId());
            data.put("main_id", main.getId());
            data.put("date", main.getDate());
            data.put("info", main.getInfo());
            data.put("project_id", main.getProjectId());
            data.put("clientName", clientName);
            data.put("project_name", projectName);
            data.put("items", itemOut);
            data.put("details", detailOut);
            data.put("total", null);
            data.put("comment", main.getInfo());
            resp.put("code", 0);
            resp.put("msg", "ok");
            resp.put("data", data);
            return resp;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.put("code", 500);
            resp.put("msg", "服务器错误：" + e.getMessage());
            return resp;
        }
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.utils;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class StringUtil {
	public static boolean isEmpty(String str){
		if(str==null||"".equals(str.trim())){
			return true;
		}else{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public static boolean isNotEmpty(String str){
		if((str!=null)&&!"".equals(str.trim())){
			return true;
		}else{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public static String formatLike(String str){
		if(isNotEmpty(str)){
			return "%"+str+"%";
		}else{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public static List<String> filterWhite(List<String> list){
		List<String> resultList=new ArrayList<String>();
		for(String l:list){
			if(isNotEmpty(l)){
				resultList.add(l);
			}
		}
		return resultList;
	}
	public static String stripHtml(String content) { 
	    content = content.replaceAll("<p .*?>", "\r\n"); 
	    content = content.replaceAll("<br\\s*/?>", "\r\n"); 
	    content = content.replaceAll("\\<.*?>", ""); 
	    content = content.replaceAll(" ", ""); 
	    return content;   
	}
	public static String genSixRandomNum(){
		Random random = new Random();
		String result="";
		for (int i=0;i<6;i++)
		{
			result+=random.nextInt(10);
		}
		return result;
	}
	public static String getRandomString(int length){
		Random random = new Random();
		StringBuffer sb = new StringBuffer();
		for(int i = 0; i < length; ++i){
			int number = random.nextInt(2);
			long result = 0;
			switch(number){
				case 0:
					result = Math.round(Math.random() * 25 + 65);
					sb.append(String.valueOf((char)result));
					break;
				case 1:
					sb.append(String.valueOf(new Random().nextInt(10)));
					break;
			}
		}
		return sb.toString();
	}
}
package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.metadata.IPage;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.dto.ResultInfo;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.mapper.UserMapper;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.User;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/user")
public class UserController {
    @Autowired
    UserMapper mapper;
    @PostMapping("login")
    public ResultInfo login(@RequestParam(value = "name", required = false) String name,
                            @RequestParam(value = "pass", required = false) String pass) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("用户名不能为空");
            info.setData(null);
            return info;
        }
        if (pass == null || pass.trim().isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("密码不能为空");
            info.setData(null);
            return info;
        }
        QueryWrapper<User> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
        qw.eq("name", name.trim());
        User user = mapper.selectOne(qw);
        if (user == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("用户名或密码错误");
            info.setData(null);
            return info;
        }
        String dbPass = user.getPass() == null ? "" : user.getPass().trim();
        String md5Pass = md5Hex(pass); 
        if (!dbPass.equalsIgnoreCase(md5Pass)) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("用户名或密码错误");
            info.setData(null);
            return info;
        }
        if (user.getIsdel() != null && user.getIsdel() == 1) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("账户已被删除");
            info.setData(null);
            return info;
        }
        if (user.getStatus() != null && user.getStatus() == 0) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("账户已被禁用");
            info.setData(null);
            return info;
        }
        boolean isAdmin = (user.getPower() != null && user.getPower() == 1);
        java.util.Map<String, Object> userInfo = new java.util.HashMap<>();
        userInfo.put("id", user.getId());
        userInfo.put("name", user.getName());
        userInfo.put("power", user.getPower());
        userInfo.put("departmentId", user.getDepartmentId());
        java.util.Map<String, Object> payload = new java.util.HashMap<>();
        payload.put("is_admin", isAdmin);
        payload.put("user", userInfo);
        payload.put("status", 1);
        info.setCode(0);
        info.setMsg("登录成功");
        info.setCount(0);
        java.util.List<Object> dataList = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        dataList.add(payload);
        info.setData(dataList);
        return info;
    }
    private String md5Hex(String input) {
        if (input == null) return null;
        try {
            java.security.MessageDigest md = java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5");
            byte[] digest = md.digest(input.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(digest.length * 2);
            for (byte b : digest) {
                sb.append(String.format("%02x", b & 0xff));
            }
            return sb.toString();
        } catch (java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
    @GetMapping("index")
    public ResultInfo index(
            @RequestParam(value = "name", required = false) String name,
            @RequestParam(value = "page", required = false, defaultValue = "1") long page,
            @RequestParam(value = "limit", required = false, defaultValue = "10") long limit) {
        QueryWrapper<User> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
        if (name != null && !name.trim().isEmpty()) {
            qw.like("name", name.trim());
        }
        Page<User> pager = new Page<>(page, limit);
        IPage<User> resultPage = mapper.selectPage(pager, qw);
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        info.setCode(0);
        info.setMsg("success");
        info.setCount(resultPage.getTotal());
        info.setData(resultPage.getRecords());
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("insert")
    public ResultInfo insert(User u){
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (u == null || u.getName() == null || u.getName().trim().isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("姓名不能为空");
            return info;
        }
        int rows = mapper.insert(u);
        if (rows > 0) {
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("添加成功");
            info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(u));
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("添加失败");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @GetMapping("delete")
    public ResultInfo delete(@RequestParam("id") int id){
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        int rows = mapper.deleteById(id);
        if (rows > 0) {
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("删除成功");
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("删除失败，ID不存在");
        }
        return info;
    }
    @PostMapping("update")
    public ResultInfo update(@RequestParam("employeeId") Integer employeeId,
                             @RequestParam("name") String name) {
        ResultInfo info = new ResultInfo();
        if (employeeId == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("缺少 employeeId");
            return info;
        }
        if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("姓名不能为空");
            return info;
        }
        User u = mapper.selectById(employeeId);
        if (u == null) {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("未找到该用户");
            return info;
        }
        u.setName(name.trim());
        int rows = mapper.updateById(u);
        if (rows > 0) {
            info.setCode(0);
            info.setMsg("更新成功");
            info.setData(java.util.Arrays.asList(u));
        } else {
            info.setCode(1);
            info.setMsg("更新失败");
        }
        return info;
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.controller;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.*;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.service.*;
import lombok.Data;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/reservation")
public class ReservtaionController {
    @Autowired
    private PayinfoService payinfoService;
    @Autowired
    private ReserverService reserverService;
    private static final DateTimeFormatter DT_FMT = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    @Autowired
    private CardService cardService;
    @Autowired
    private ClientService clientService;
    @Autowired
    private ProjectService projectService;
    @Autowired
    private OrderMainService orderMainService;
    @GetMapping("/reserverList")
    public ApiResponse reserverList(
            @RequestParam(value = "clientId", required = false) Integer clientId,
            @RequestParam(value = "page", defaultValue = "1") Long page,
            @RequestParam(value = "size", defaultValue = "10") Long size,
            @RequestParam(value = "q", required = false) String q,
            @RequestParam(value = "status", required = false) Integer status
    ) {
        Page<Reserver> pg = new Page<>(page, size);
        QueryWrapper<Reserver> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
        qw.eq("isdel", 0);
        if (clientId != null) {
            qw.eq("client_id", clientId);
        }
        if (status != null) {
            qw.eq("status", status);
        }
        if (StringUtils.hasText(q)) {
            qw.and(w -> w.like("todate", q).or().like("date", q).or().like("remark", q));
        }
        Page<Reserver> res = reserverService.page(pg, qw);
        Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("total", res.getTotal());
        data.put("records", res.getRecords());
        data.put("page", res.getCurrent());
        data.put("size", res.getSize());
        return ApiResponse.ok(data);
    }
    @GetMapping("/reserverDetail")
    public ApiResponse reserverDetail(@RequestParam("id") Integer id) {
        if (id == null) return ApiResponse.fail("id 不能为空");
        Reserver r = reserverService.getById(id);
        if (r == null || (r.getIsdel() != null && r.getIsdel() == 1)) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("预约不存在");
        }
        Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("reserver", r);
        try {
            if (r.getClientId() != null) {
                Object clientObj = clientService.getById(r.getClientId());
                data.put("client", clientObj);
            } else {
                data.put("client", null);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            data.put("client", null);
        }
        try {
            if (r.getProjectId() != null && r.getProjectId() > 0) {
                Object projectObj = projectService.getById(r.getProjectId());
                data.put("project", projectObj);
            } else {
                data.put("project", null);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            data.put("project", null);
        }
        return ApiResponse.ok(data);
    }
    @PostMapping("/reserverUpdate")
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public ApiResponse reserverUpdate(@RequestBody Map<String,Object> payload) {
        if (payload == null) return ApiResponse.fail("参数为空");
        Object idObj = payload.get("id");
        if (idObj == null) return ApiResponse.fail("缺少 id");
        Integer id;
        try {
            id = Integer.valueOf(String.valueOf(idObj));
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("id 格式错误");
        }
        Reserver exist = reserverService.getById(id);
        if (exist == null || (exist.getIsdel() != null && exist.getIsdel() == 1)) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("预约不存在或已删除");
        }
        boolean modified = false;
        if (payload.containsKey("todate")) {
            Object v = payload.get("todate");
            if (v != null && StringUtils.hasText(String.valueOf(v))) {
                exist.setTodate(String.valueOf(v));
                modified = true;
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("totime")) {
            Object v = payload.get("totime");
            exist.setTotime(v == null ? null : String.valueOf(v));
            modified = true;
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("status")) {
            Object v = payload.get("status");
            if (v != null) {
                try {
                    exist.setStatus(Integer.valueOf(String.valueOf(v)));
                    if (exist.getStatus() != null) {
                        int s = exist.getStatus();
                        String now = LocalDateTime.now().format(DT_FMT);
                        if (s == 1) { 
                            try {
                                java.lang.reflect.Method m = exist.getClass().getMethod("setConfirmTime", String.class);
                                if (m != null) m.invoke(exist, now);
                            } catch (Exception ignore) {}
                        } else if (s == 2) { 
                            try {
                                java.lang.reflect.Method m = exist.getClass().getMethod("setCancelTime", String.class);
                                if (m != null) m.invoke(exist, now);
                            } catch (Exception ignore) {}
                        }
                    }
                    modified = true;
                } catch (Exception ignore) {}
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("projectId") || payload.containsKey("project_id")) {
            Object v = payload.containsKey("projectId") ? payload.get("projectId") : payload.get("project_id");
            if (v != null) {
                try {
                    exist.setProjectId(Integer.valueOf(String.valueOf(v)));
                    modified = true;
                } catch (Exception ignore) {}
            }
        }
        if (payload.containsKey("clientId") || payload.containsKey("client_id")) {
            Object v = payload.containsKey("clientId") ? payload.get("clientId") : payload.get("client_id");
            if (v != null) {
                try {
                    exist.setClientId(Integer.valueOf(String.valueOf(v)));
                    modified = true;
                } catch (Exception ignore) {}
            }
        }
        if (!modified) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("未提供可更新的字段");
        }
        try {
            reserverService.updateById(exist);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            return ApiResponse.fail("更新失败：" + ex.getMessage());
        }
        Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("reserver", exist);
        try {
            if (exist.getClientId() != null) data.put("client", clientService.getById(exist.getClientId()));
        } catch (Exception ignore) { data.put("client", null); }
        try {
            if (exist.getProjectId() != null && exist.getProjectId() > 0) data.put("project", projectService.getById(exist.getProjectId()));
        } catch (Exception ignore) { data.put("project", null); }
        return ApiResponse.ok(data);
    }
    @Data
    public static class RefundRequest {
        private Integer id;
        private Double amount;
        private String reason;
    }
    @PostMapping("/refund")
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public ApiResponse refund(@RequestBody RefundRequest req) {
        if (req == null || req.getId() == null) return ApiResponse.fail("缺少 id");
        Integer id = req.getId();
        Payinfo p = payinfoService.getById(id);
        if (p == null || (p.getIsdel() != null && p.getIsdel() == 1)) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("订单不存在");
        }
        if (p.getPaystatus() == null || p.getPaystatus() != 1) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("仅已支付订单可退款");
        }
        String payMode = null;
        try {
            java.lang.reflect.Method m = p.getClass().getMethod("getPaymode");
            if (m != null) {
                Object v = m.invoke(p);
                if (v != null) payMode = v.toString();
            }
        } catch (Exception ignore) {
        }
        if ("online".equalsIgnoreCase(payMode)) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("该订单为在线支付，请在支付平台/渠道发起退款或联系管理员");
        }
        double refundAmount = 0.0;
        if (req.getAmount() != null && req.getAmount() > 0) {
            refundAmount = req.getAmount();
        } else {
            refundAmount = (p.getPrice() == null ? 0.0 : p.getPrice());
        }
        if (refundAmount <= 0) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("退款金额无效");
        }
        Card card = null;
        QueryWrapper<Card> cw = new QueryWrapper<>();
        cw.eq("isdel", 0).eq("payinfo_id", p.getId());
        card = cardService.getOne(cw);
        if (card == null) {
            try {
                java.lang.reflect.Method gm = p.getClass().getMethod("getCardId");
                if (gm != null) {
                    Object cv = gm.invoke(p);
                    if (cv != null) {
                        Integer cardId = Integer.valueOf(cv.toString());
                        card = cardService.getById(cardId);
                        if (card != null && (card.getIsdel() != null && card.getIsdel() == 1)) {
                            card = null;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) { }
        }
        if (card == null) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("未找到关联的实卡信息，无法退回至卡");
        }
        if (p.getPaystatus() != null && p.getPaystatus() == 2) {
            Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("message", "订单已退款");
            return ApiResponse.ok(data);
        }
        double before = card.getCount() == null ? 0.0 : card.getCount();
        double after = before + refundAmount;
        card.setCount(after);
        try {
            cardService.updateById(card);
            p.setPaystatus(2);
            payinfoService.updateById(p);
            Map<String,Object> resp = new HashMap<>();
            resp.put("payinfoId", p.getId());
            resp.put("refundAmount", refundAmount);
            resp.put("cardId", card.getId());
            resp.put("cardBefore", before);
            resp.put("cardAfter", after);
            resp.put("message", "退款成功（已退回至实卡账户）");
            return ApiResponse.ok(resp);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ex.printStackTrace();
            return ApiResponse.fail("退款操作失败：" + ex.getMessage());
        }
    }
    @GetMapping("/list")
    public ApiResponse list(
            @RequestParam(value = "page", defaultValue = "1") Long page,
            @RequestParam(value = "size", defaultValue = "10") Long size,
            @RequestParam(value = "q", required = false) String q,
            @RequestParam(value = "paystatus", required = false) Integer paystatus,
            @RequestParam(value = "clientId", required = false) Integer clientId
    ) {
        Page<Payinfo> pg = new Page<>(page, size);
        QueryWrapper<Payinfo> qw = new QueryWrapper<>();
        qw.eq("isdel", 0);
        if (paystatus != null) {
            qw.eq("paystatus", paystatus);
        }
        if (clientId != null) {
            qw.eq("client_id", clientId);
        }
        if (StringUtils.hasText(q)) {
            qw.like("date", q);
        }
        Page<Payinfo> res = payinfoService.page(pg, qw);
        Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("total", res.getTotal());
        data.put("records", res.getRecords());
        data.put("page", res.getCurrent());
        data.put("size", res.getSize());
        return ApiResponse.ok(data);
    }
    @GetMapping("/detail")
    public ApiResponse detail(@RequestParam("id") Integer id) {
        if (id == null) return ApiResponse.fail("id 不能为空");
        Payinfo p = payinfoService.getById(id);
        if (p == null || (p.getIsdel() != null && p.getIsdel() == 1)) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("订单不存在");
        }
        Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
        result.put("payinfo", p);
        try {
            if (p.getClientId() != null) {
                Object clientObj = null;
                try {
                    clientObj = clientService.getById(p.getClientId());
                } catch (Exception ex) {
                    clientObj = null;
                }
                result.put("client", clientObj);
            } else {
                result.put("client", null);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            result.put("client", null);
        }
        try {
            Card card = null;
            QueryWrapper<Card> cw = new QueryWrapper<>();
            cw.eq("isdel", 0).eq("payinfo_id", p.getId());
            card = cardService.getOne(cw);
            if (card == null) {
                try {
                    java.lang.reflect.Method gm = p.getClass().getMethod("getCardId");
                    if (gm != null) {
                        Object cv = gm.invoke(p);
                        if (cv != null) {
                            Integer cardId = Integer.valueOf(cv.toString());
                            Card tmp = cardService.getById(cardId);
                            if (tmp != null && (tmp.getIsdel() == null || tmp.getIsdel() == 0)) {
                                card = tmp;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignore) { }
            }
            result.put("card", card);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            result.put("card", null);
        }
        try {
            Integer projectId = p.getProjectId();
            if (projectId != null && projectId > 0) {
                ProjectDetailVO projectDetail = null;
                ProjectItemsResult itemsResult = null;
                try {
                    projectDetail = projectService.getProjectDetailById(projectId);
                } catch (Exception ignore) { projectDetail = null; }
                try {
                    itemsResult = projectService.getProjectItemsByProjectId(projectId);
                } catch (Exception ignore) { itemsResult = null; }
                result.put("project", projectDetail);
                if (itemsResult != null) {
                    Map<String,Object> itemsData = new HashMap<>();
                    itemsData.put("selected_ids", itemsResult.getSelectedIds());
                    itemsData.put("selected", itemsResult.getSelectedItems());
                    result.put("projectItems", itemsData);
                } else {
                    result.put("projectItems", null);
                }
            } else {
                result.put("project", null);
                result.put("projectItems", null);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            result.put("project", null);
            result.put("projectItems", null);
        }
        return ApiResponse.ok(result);
    }
    @PostMapping("/createFromPay")
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public ApiResponse createFromPay(@RequestBody CreateReserverFromPayReq req) {
        if (req == null || req.getPayinfoId() == null) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("缺少 payinfoId");
        }
        Payinfo p = payinfoService.getById(req.getPayinfoId());
        if (p == null || (p.getIsdel() != null && p.getIsdel() == 1)) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("对应订单不存在");
        }
        Reserver r = new Reserver();
        OrderMain orderMain = new OrderMain();
        String now = LocalDateTime.now().format(DT_FMT);
        r.setDate(now);
        r.setTodate(req.getTodate());
        r.setTotime(req.getTotime());
        r.setClientId(p.getClientId());
        r.setProjectId(p.getProjectId());
        orderMain.setDate(req.getTodate());
        orderMain.setClientId(p.getClientId());
        orderMain.setProjectId(p.getProjectId());
        r.setStatus(req.getStatus() == null ? 0 : req.getStatus());
        r.setIsdel(0);
        reserverService.save(r);
        orderMainService.save(orderMain);
        Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("reserverId", r.getId());
        data.put("reserver", r);
        return ApiResponse.ok(data);
    }
    @PostMapping("/create")
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public ApiResponse create(@RequestBody CreateReserverReq req) {
        if (req == null) return ApiResponse.fail("参数为空");
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(req.getTodate())) {
            return ApiResponse.fail("请选择预约日期");
        }
        if (req.getClientId() == null) return ApiResponse.fail("clientId 必须提供");
        Reserver r = new Reserver();
        String now = LocalDateTime.now().format(DT_FMT);
        r.setDate(now);
        r.setTodate(req.getTodate());
        r.setTotime(req.getTotime());
        r.setClientId(req.getClientId());
        r.setProjectId(req.getProjectId() == null ? 0 : req.getProjectId());
        r.setStatus(req.getStatus() == null ? 0 : req.getStatus());
        r.setIsdel(0);
        reserverService.save(r);
        Map<String,Object> data = new HashMap<>();
        data.put("reserverId", r.getId());
        data.put("reserver", r);
        return ApiResponse.ok(data);
    }
    @PostMapping("/delete")
    public ApiResponse delete(@RequestParam("id") Integer id) {
        if (id == null) return ApiResponse.fail("id 不能为空");
        Reserver r = reserverService.getById(id);
        if (r == null) return ApiResponse.fail("预约不存在");
        r.setIsdel(1);
        reserverService.updateById(r);
        return ApiResponse.ok();
    }
    @Data
    public static class CreateReserverFromPayReq {
        private Integer payinfoId;
        private String todate;
        private String totime;
        private Integer status; 
    }
    @Data
    public static class CreateReserverReq {
        private String todate;
        private String totime;
        private Integer clientId;
        private Integer projectId;
        private Integer status;
    }
    @Data
    public static class ApiResponse {
        private int code; 
        private String message;
        private Object data;
        public static ApiResponse ok(Object data) {
            ApiResponse r = new ApiResponse();
            r.setCode(0);
            r.setMessage("ok");
            r.setData(data);
            return r;
        }
        public static ApiResponse ok() { return ok(null); }
        public static ApiResponse fail(String msg) {
            ApiResponse r = new ApiResponse();
            r.setCode(-1);
            r.setMessage(msg);
            r.setData(null);
            return r;
        }
    }
}
