<script type="text/javascript">
    var $;
    var layer;
    layui.use(["jquery","layer"], function(){
        $ = layui.jquery;
        layer = layui.layer;
        var $form = $("#loginForm");
        var $btn = $("#submitBtn");
        var $error = $("#error");
        function showError(msg){
            $error.text(msg).fadeIn(150);
            setTimeout(function(){ $error.fadeOut(300); }, 5000);
        }
        function disableBtn(yes){
            if(yes){
                $btn.prop("disabled", true).text("登录中...");
            }else{
                $btn.prop("disabled", false).text("登录 管理员");
            }
        }
        $form.on("keydown", function(e){
            if(e.key === "Enter"){
                e.preventDefault();
                $btn.trigger("click");
            }
        });
        $btn.on("click", function(){
            var name = $.trim($("[name=name]").val());
            var pass = $.trim($("[name=pass]").val());
            if(!name){
                showError("请输入用户名");
                $("[name=name]").focus();
                return;
            }
            if(!pass){
                showError("请输入密码");
                $("[name=pass]").focus();
                return;
            }
            disableBtn(true);
            ajax_post("/user/login", {name: name, pass: pass}, function(json){
                disableBtn(false);
                console.log("login resp:", json);
                if(!json){
                    showError("服务端未返回数据");
                    return;
                }
                // 兼容：真实信息可能在顶层，也可能在 data[0]
                var payload = null;
                if(Array.isArray(json.data) && json.data.length > 0){
                    payload = json.data[0];
                } else {
                    payload = json;
                }
                // 规范 status（判断登录是否成功）
                var statusRaw = (payload && (typeof payload.status !== "undefined" ? payload.status : json.status));
                var statusNum = parseInt(statusRaw, 10);
                if(isNaN(statusNum)){
                    // 如果没有数值型 status，尝试按常见字段判断（如 code 或 success）
                    if(typeof json.code !== "undefined"){
                        statusNum = parseInt(json.code, 10) || 0;
                    } else if(typeof json.success !== "undefined"){
                        statusNum = (json.success ? 1 : 0);
                    } else {
                        statusNum = 0; // 保守处理为失败
                    }
                }
                // 优先从 payload.user.power 读取，其次 payload.power，再退回到顶层 json.user.power / json.power
                var powerRaw;
                if(payload && payload.user && typeof payload.user.power !== "undefined"){
                    powerRaw = payload.user.power;
                } else if(payload && typeof payload.power !== "undefined"){
                    powerRaw = payload.power;
                } else if(json && json.user && typeof json.user.power !== "undefined"){
                    powerRaw = json.user.power;
                } else {
                    powerRaw = json.power;
                }
                // 规范 power 字段（后端可能为 "0"/"1" 或 0/1 或 true/false）
                var powerNum = null;
                if(typeof powerRaw !== "undefined" && powerRaw !== null){
                    if(typeof powerRaw === "boolean"){
                        powerNum = powerRaw ? 1 : 0;
                    } else {
                        var tmp = parseInt(powerRaw, 10);
                        if(!isNaN(tmp)){
                            powerNum = tmp;
                        } else {
                            var str = String(powerRaw).toLowerCase();
                            if(str === "true") powerNum = 1;
                            else if(str === "false") powerNum = 0;
                        }
                    }
                }
                console.log("parsed status:", statusNum, "parsed power:", powerNum, "raw power:", powerRaw);
                if(statusNum && statusNum > 0){
                    // 如果你希望登录成功后不论权限都跳转管理员页，直接用： location.href = "/Admin/admin.html";
                    // 这里我们采用常见策略：只有 power === 1 允许进入管理员页
                    if(powerNum === null){
                        // 当缺少权限字段时，优先提示并打印完整 payload 供调试
                        console.warn("缺少可解析的 power 字段，payload:", payload);
                        showError(payload && payload.msg ? payload.msg : json.msg || "没有权限字段，无法判断管理员权限。");
                        return;
                    }
                    if(powerNum === 1){
                        location.href = "/Admin/admin.html";
                    } else {
                        showError(payload && payload.msg ? payload.msg : json.msg || "该账号没有管理员权限。");
                    }
                } else {
                    showError(payload && payload.msg ? payload.msg : json.msg || "登录失败，请检查用户名和密码");
                }
            }, function(err){
                disableBtn(false);
                showError("请求失败，请稍后重试");
                console.error("ajax error:", err);
            });
        });
        $("#forgot").on("click", function(){
            layer.open({
                title: "找回密码",
                content: "请联系系统管理员或通过工单重置密码。"
            });
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>
package com.syd.medicalbutler.service;
import com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo.Project;

@Data
public class Client {

    private Integer id;

    private String code;
    @TableField(value= "power")
    private Integer power;

    private String nike;
    @TableField(value = "pass")
    private String pass;
    @TableField(value = "weixin")
    private String weixin;
    @TableField(value = "sex")
    private Integer sex;
    @TableField(value = "qq")
    private String qq;
    @TableField(value = "tel")
    private String tel;
    @TableField(value = "tel2")
    private String tel2;
    @TableField(value = "status")
    private Integer status;
    @TableField(value = "level_id")
    private Integer levelId;
    @TableField(value = "point")
    private Integer point;
    @TableField(value = "srcinfo")
    private String srcinfo;
    @TableField(value = "createdate")
    private String createdate;
    @TableField(value = "card_id")
    private Integer cardId;
    @TableField(value = "isdel")
    private Integer isdel;
    @TableField(value = "avatar")
    private String avatar;
    @TableField(value = "comment")
    private String comment;
    static String[]STATUS={"空闲","预约","签到","咨询"};
    public String getStatusname(){return STATUS[status];}
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.dto;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.QueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.metadata.IPage;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import lombok.Data;
@Data
public class SearchInfo {
    private String sea_val;
    private String sea_col;
    private String sea_relation;
    private  Long page;
    private  Long limit;
    public Long getLimit(){
        return limit==null?100000:limit;
    }
    public <T> IPage<T> getPageInfo(Class<T> cls){
        if(page==null) return null;
        IPage<T> po = new Page<T>(page, limit);
        return po;
    }
    public <T> QueryWrapper<T>  getQuery(QueryWrapper<T> ex,Class<T> cls){
        QueryWrapper<T> q =null;
        if(ex==null) q=new QueryWrapper<T>();
        else q=ex;
        if(sea_val!=null) {
            if(sea_relation==null) sea_relation="=";
            if(sea_relation.equals("==")) q.eq(sea_col,sea_val);
            else if(sea_relation.equals("like")) q.like(sea_col,sea_val);
        }
        return q;
    }
}

package com.syd.medicalbutler.pojo;
import lombok.Data;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
@Data
public class R {
    private Integer code;
    private String msg;
    private Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
    public static R ok() {
        R r = new R();
        r.setCode(200);
        r.setMsg("成功");
        return r;
    }
    public static R ok(String msg) {
        R r = new R();
        r.setCode(200);
        r.setMsg(msg);
        return r;
    }
    public static R error() {
        R r = new R();
        r.setCode(500);
        r.setMsg("失败");
        return r;
    }
    public static R error(String msg) {
        R r = new R();
        r.setCode(500);
        r.setMsg(msg);
        return r;
    }
    public R data(String key, Object value) {
        this.data.put(key, value);
        return this;
    }
    public static R notFound() {
        R r = new R();
        r.setCode(404);
        r.setMsg("资源未找到");
        return r;
    }
    public static R unauthorized() {
        R r = new R();
        r.setCode(401);
        r.setMsg("未授权");
        return r;
    }
}

        /* 将关闭按钮居中对齐文本 */
        .close-btn { display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; text-align:center; }

            display: flex;

        }

<script src="../../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    // 页面状态
    let state = {
        page: 1,
        pageSize: 10,
        keyword: '',
        status: '',
        hasMore: true,
        loading: false
    };
    // 简单工具函数
    function escapeHtml(s){ if(!s) return ''; return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;'); }
    function statusText(s){
        if(s === 1) return '已确认';
        if(s === 2) return '已取消';
        return '未处理';
    }
    function badgeClass(s){
        if(s === 1) return 'confirm';
        if(s === 2) return 'cancel';
        return 'pending';
    }
    /* ---------- AJAX 通用封装（XMLHttpRequest） ---------- */
    function ajaxRequest(url, method = 'GET', data = null) {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            try {
                const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
                const isGet = method.toUpperCase() === 'GET';
                let sendUrl = url;
                if (isGet && data && Object.keys(data).length > 0) {
                    const params = Object.keys(data).map(k => encodeURIComponent(k) + '=' + encodeURIComponent(data[k])).join('&');
                    sendUrl = url + (url.indexOf('?') === -1 ? '?' : '&') + params;
                }
                xhr.open(method, sendUrl, true);
                if (!isGet) {
                    xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json;charset=UTF-8');
                }
                xhr.onreadystatechange = function() {
                    if (xhr.readyState !== 4) return;
                    const status = xhr.status;
                    if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
                        try {
                            const json = xhr.responseText ? JSON.parse(xhr.responseText) : {};
                            resolve(json);
                        } catch (err) {
                            reject(new Error('返回结果非 JSON'));
                        }
                    } else {
                        reject(new Error('HTTP status ' + status));
                    }
                };
                xhr.onerror = function() {
                    reject(new Error('网络错误'));
                };
                if (isGet) {
                    xhr.send();
                } else {
                    xhr.send(data ? JSON.stringify(data) : null);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                reject(err);
            }
        });
    }
    /* ---------- 用 AJAX 实现的 API 函数（接口不变） ---------- */
    async function fetchReservations(page = 1, pageSize = 10, keyword = '', status = '') {
        try {
            state.loading = true;
            showLoading();
            let url = '/reservation/reserverList';
            const params = { page: page, size: pageSize };
            if (keyword) params.q = keyword;
            if (status !== '') params.status = status;
            const result = await ajaxRequest(url, 'GET', params);
            if (result && result.code === 0) {
                return result.data;
            } else {
                const msg = result ? result.message : '未知错误';
                alertFallback('获取数据失败: ' + msg);
                return null;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            alertFallback('网络错误，请稍后重试');
            return null;
        } finally {
            state.loading = false;
            hideLoading();
        }
    }
    async function fetchReservationDetail(id) {
        try {
            const url = '/reservation/reserverDetail';
            const result = await ajaxRequest(url, 'GET', { id: id });
            if (result && result.code === 0) {
                return result.data;
            } else {
                const msg = result ? result.message : '未知错误';
                alertFallback('获取详情失败: ' + msg);
                return null;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            alertFallback('网络错误，请稍后重试');
            return null;
        }
    }
    async function updateReservation(data) {
        try {
            const url = '/reservation/reserverUpdate';
            const result = await ajaxRequest(url, 'POST', data);
            return result;
        } catch (error) {
            return { code: -1, message: '网络错误' };
        }
    }
    async function createReservation(data) {
        try {
            const url = '/reservation/create';
            const result = await ajaxRequest(url, 'POST', data);
            return result;
        } catch (error) {
            return { code: -1, message: '网络错误' };
        }
    }
    async function deleteReservation(id) {
        try {
            const url = '/reservation/delete';
            const result = await ajaxRequest(url + '?id=' + encodeURIComponent(id), 'POST', null);
            return result;
        } catch (error) {
            return { code: -1, message: '网络错误' };
        }
    }
    /* ---------- 渲染函数（保持不变或轻度增强） ---------- */
    function renderCards(data) {
        const wrap = document.getElementById('cardList');
        if (!data || !Array.isArray(data.records) || data.records.length === 0) {
            if (state.page === 1) {
                document.getElementById('empty').style.display = 'block';
                wrap.innerHTML = '';
            }
            document.getElementById('moreWrap').style.display = 'none';
            return;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('empty').style.display = 'none';
        }
        if (state.page === 1) {
            wrap.innerHTML = '';
        }
        data.records.forEach(item => {
            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'card';
            const av = document.createElement('div');
            av.className = 'avatar';
            av.textContent = 'P' + (item.projectId || '0');
            const body = document.createElement('div');
            body.className = 'card-body';
            const right = (item.todate || '') + (item.totime ? (' · ' + item.totime) : '');
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="meta">
                    <div class="left">#${item.id} · ${escapeHtml(item.date)}</div>
                    <div class="right">${escapeHtml(right)}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="title">项目 ${escapeHtml(item.projectId || '无')} · 客户 ${escapeHtml(item.clientId)}</div>
                <div class="desc">${escapeHtml(item.remark||'无备注')}</div>
                <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:6px;">
                    <div class="status">
                        <span class="badge ${badgeClass(item.status)}">${statusText(item.status)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <button class="action" data-id="${item.id}" data-action="view"><i class="bi bi-eye"></i> 查看</button>
                        <button class="action" data-id="${item.id}" data-action="edit"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i> 编辑</button>
                        <button class="action danger" data-id="${item.id}" data-action="delete"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i> 删除</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            card.appendChild(av);
            card.appendChild(body);
            wrap.appendChild(card);
        });
        // 显示或隐藏"加载更多"按钮
        state.hasMore = (state.page * state.pageSize) < data.total;
        document.getElementById('moreWrap').style.display = state.hasMore ? 'block' : 'none';
    }
    function renderTable(data) {
        const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
        if (state.page === 1) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '';
        }
        if (!data || !Array.isArray(data.records)) return;
        data.records.forEach(item => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td>${item.id}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(item.date)}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(item.todate)}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(item.totime || '无')}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(item.clientId)}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(item.projectId || '无')}</td>
                <td><span class="badge ${badgeClass(item.status)}">${statusText(item.status)}</span></td>
                <td>
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-xs" data-id="${item.id}" data-action="view">查看</button>
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-xs layui-btn-normal" data-id="${item.id}" data-action="edit">编辑</button>
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-xs layui-btn-danger" data-id="${item.id}" data-action="delete">删除</button>
                </td>
            `;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
    }
    function showLoading() {
        const cards = document.getElementById('cardList');
        const table = document.getElementById('tableBody');
        if (state.page === 1) {
            if (window.innerWidth < 801) {
                cards.innerHTML = '<div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton"></div>';
            } else {
                table.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center;">加载中...</td></tr>';
            }
        }
    }
    function hideLoading() {
        // no-op，渲染函数会覆盖
    }
    function showDetailModal(data) {
        document.getElementById('detailId').textContent = data.reserver.id;
        document.getElementById('detailDate').textContent = data.reserver.date || '无';
        document.getElementById('detailTodate').textContent = data.reserver.todate || '无';
        document.getElementById('detailTotime').textContent = data.reserver.totime || '无';
        document.getElementById('detailClientId').textContent = data.reserver.clientId;
        document.getElementById('detailProjectId').textContent = data.reserver.projectId || '无';
        document.getElementById('detailStatus').innerHTML = `<span class="badge ${badgeClass(data.reserver.status)}">${statusText(data.reserver.status)}</span>`;
        document.getElementById('detailRemark').textContent = data.reserver.remark || '无';
        openOverlay('detailOverlay');
    }
    function showEditModal(data) {
        document.getElementById('editId').value = data.reserver.id;
        document.getElementById('editTodate').value = data.reserver.todate || '';
        document.getElementById('editTotime').value = data.reserver.totime || '';
        document.getElementById('editClientId').value = data.reserver.clientId;
        document.getElementById('editProjectId').value = data.reserver.projectId || '';
        document.getElementById('editStatus').value = data.reserver.status;
        document.getElementById('editRemark').value = data.reserver.remark || '';
        openOverlay('editOverlay');
        if (window.layui && layui.form) {
            layui.form.render();
        }
    }
    function showAddModal() {
        const addForm = document.getElementById('addForm');
        if (addForm) addForm.reset();
        openOverlay('addOverlay');
        if (window.layui && layui.form) {
            layui.form.render();
        }
    }
    function openOverlay(id) {
        document.getElementById(id).style.display = 'flex';
    }
    function closeOverlay(id) {
        document.getElementById(id).style.display = 'none';
    }
    /* ---------- layui 初始化与事件绑定 ---------- */
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
        // 尝试初始化 layui 模块（如果加载成功）
        if (window.layui) {
            try {
                layui.use(['layer', 'form', 'laydate'], function(){
                    const layer = layui.layer;
                    const form = layui.form;
                    const laydate = layui.laydate;
                    // 初始化日期选择器
                    laydate.render({
                        elem: '#editTodate',
                        type: 'date'
                    });
                    laydate.render({
                        elem: '[name="todate"]',
                        type: 'date'
                    });
                    // 编辑表单提交
                    form.on('submit(editSubmit)', function(data){
                        const formData = data.field;
                        const payload = {
                            id: parseInt(formData.id),
                            todate: formData.todate,
                            totime: formData.totime || null,
                            clientId: parseInt(formData.clientId),
                            projectId: formData.projectId ? parseInt(formData.projectId) : null,
                            status: parseInt(formData.status),
                            remark: formData.remark
                        };
                        updateReservation(payload).then(result => {
                            if (result && result.code === 0) {
                                layer.msg('更新成功', {icon: 1});
                                layer.closeAll();
                                loadReservations(1);
                            } else {
                                layer.msg('更新失败: ' + (result ? result.message : '未知错误'), {icon: 2});
                            }
                        });
                        return false; // 阻止表单跳转
                    });
                    // 新增表单提交
                    form.on('submit(addSubmit)', function(data){
                        const formData = data.field;
                        const payload = {
                            todate: formData.todate,
                            totime: formData.totime || null,
                            clientId: parseInt(formData.clientId),
                            projectId: formData.projectId ? parseInt(formData.projectId) : null,
                            status: 0, // 默认未处理
                            remark: formData.remark
                        };
                        createReservation(payload).then(result => {
                            if (result && result.code === 0) {
                                layer.msg('创建成功', {icon: 1});
                                layer.closeAll();
                                loadReservations(1);
                            } else {
                                layer.msg('创建失败: ' + (result ? result.message : '未知错误'), {icon: 2});
                            }
                        });
                        return false; // 阻止表单跳转
                    });
                });
            } catch (e) {
                // layui init failed，保持为空实现
            }
        }
        // 加载预约数据
        loadReservations();
        // 事件监听
        const byId = id => document.getElementById(id);
        byId('btnRefresh').addEventListener('click', function(){
            loadReservations(1);
        });
        byId('btnSearch').addEventListener('click', function(){
            state.keyword = document.getElementById('searchInput').value;
            state.status = document.getElementById('statusFilter').value;
            loadReservations(1);
        });
        byId('btnMore').addEventListener('click', function(){
            if (!state.loading && state.hasMore) {
                loadReservations(state.page + 1);
            }
        });
        byId('btnAdd').addEventListener('click', function(){
            showAddModal();
        });
        byId('goSubmit').addEventListener('click', function(){
            showAddModal();
        });
        byId('btnCloseDetailOverlay').addEventListener('click', function(){
            closeOverlay('detailOverlay');
        });
        byId('closeDetailOverlay').addEventListener('click', function(){
            closeOverlay('detailOverlay');
        });
        byId('btnCancelEditOverlay').addEventListener('click', function(){
            closeOverlay('editOverlay');
        });
        byId('closeEditOverlay').addEventListener('click', function(){
            closeOverlay('editOverlay');
        });
        byId('btnCancelAddOverlay').addEventListener('click', function(){
            closeOverlay('addOverlay');
        });
        byId('closeAddOverlay').addEventListener('click', function(){
            closeOverlay('addOverlay');
        });
        byId('btnEditSubmit').addEventListener('click', function(){
            const form = document.getElementById('editForm');
            const formData = {
                id: document.getElementById('editId').value,
                todate: document.getElementById('editTodate').value,
                totime: document.getElementById('editTotime').value,
                clientId: document.getElementById('editClientId').value,
                projectId: document.getElementById('editProjectId').value,
                status: document.getElementById('editStatus').value,
                remark: document.getElementById('editRemark').value
            };
            const payload = {
                id: parseInt(formData.id),
                todate: formData.todate,
                totime: formData.totime || null,
                clientId: parseInt(formData.clientId),
                projectId: formData.projectId ? parseInt(formData.projectId) : null,
                status: parseInt(formData.status),
                remark: formData.remark
            };
            updateReservation(payload).then(result => {
                if (result && result.code === 0) {
                    alert('更新成功');
                    closeOverlay('editOverlay');
                    loadReservations(1);
                } else {
                    alert('更新失败: ' + (result ? result.message : '未知错误'));
                }
            });
        });
        // 委托事件处理（查看/编辑/删除）
        document.body.addEventListener('click', function(e){
            const btn = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
            if(!btn) return;
            const action = btn.getAttribute('data-action');
            const id = btn.getAttribute('data-id');
            if (action === 'view') {
                fetchReservationDetail(id).then(data => {
                    if (data) {
                        showDetailModal(data);
                    }
                });
            } else if (action === 'edit') {
                fetchReservationDetail(id).then(data => {
                    if (data) {
                        showEditModal(data);
                    }
                });
            } else if (action === 'delete') {
                confirmFallback('确定要删除这条预约记录吗？').then(confirmed => {
                    if (!confirmed) return;
                    deleteReservation(id).then(result => {
                        if (result && result.code === 0) {
                            alertFallback('删除成功');
                            loadReservations(1);
                        } else {
                            alertFallback('删除失败: ' + (result ? result.message : '未知错误'));
                        }
                    });
                });
            }
        });
    });
    // 加载预约数据
    async function loadReservations(page = 1) {
        if (page === 1) {
            state.page = 1;
        } else {
            state.page = page;
        }
        const data = await fetchReservations(state.page, state.pageSize, state.keyword, state.status);
        if (data) {
            renderCards(data);
            renderTable(data);
        }
    }
    /* ---------- 辅助的回退函数（layui 不可用时） ---------- */
    function confirmFallback(msg) {
        if (window.layui && layui.layer && layui.layer.confirm) {
            return new Promise((resolve) => {
                layui.layer.confirm(msg, function(index){
                    layui.layer.close(index);
                    resolve(true);
                }, function(){
                    resolve(false);
                });
            });
        } else {
            return Promise.resolve(confirm(msg));
        }
    }
    function alertFallback(msg) {
        if (window.layui && layui.layer && layui.layer.msg) {
            layui.layer.msg(msg);
        } else {
            alert(msg);
        }
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    // API endpoints
    const API = {
        list: '/report/order-detail',        // GET userId,page,size -> data.total,data.page,data.size,data.orders
        byMain: '/report/order-detail/'      // GET /{mainId}
    };
    // state
    let state = {
        userId: localStorage.getItem('id') || null,
        page: 1,
        size: 10,
        total: 0,
        orders: [],   // array of orders returned by API
        currentMain: null
    };
    function escapeHtml(s){ if (s==null) return ''; return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;'); }
    // tiny ajax
    function ajax(url, method='GET', data=null) {
        return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
            const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
            let sendUrl = url;
            if (method.toUpperCase()==='GET' && data) {
                const params = Object.keys(data).map(k=>encodeURIComponent(k)+'='+encodeURIComponent(data[k])).join('&');
                sendUrl = url + (url.indexOf('?')===-1 ? '?' : '&') + params;
            }
            xhr.open(method, sendUrl, true);
            if (method.toUpperCase()!=='GET') xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/json;charset=UTF-8');
            xhr.timeout = 10000;
            xhr.onreadystatechange = ()=> {
                if (xhr.readyState !== 4) return;
                if (xhr.status>=200 && xhr.status<300) {
                    try { resolve(xhr.responseText ? JSON.parse(xhr.responseText) : {}); } catch(e){ reject(e); }
                } else reject(new Error('HTTP '+xhr.status));
            };
            xhr.onerror = ()=>reject(new Error('网络错误'));
            xhr.ontimeout = ()=>reject(new Error('超时'));
            xhr.send(method.toUpperCase()==='GET' ? null : JSON.stringify(data));
        });
    }
    // render list of orders (cards) - 修复：确保每个主订单都会渲染为单张卡片
    function renderOrders() {
        const listWrap = document.getElementById('cardList');
        listWrap.innerHTML = '';
        // 防御：确保 state.orders 是数组
        const orders = Array.isArray(state.orders) ? state.orders : [];
        if (orders.length === 0) {
            document.getElementById('empty').style.display = 'block';
        } else {
            document.getElementById('empty').style.display = 'none';
            orders.forEach(o=>{
                // 保证字段名容错（后端你已经返回 clientName/project_name/items/details）
                const client = o.clientName || o.client_name || '';
                const mainDate = o.date || '';
                const project = o.project_name || o.projectName || '--';
                const info = o.info || '';
                const id = o.id || o.main_id || '';
                const card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'card';
                card.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="min-width:56px;height:56px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0f2fe,#eef2ff);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;color:var(--primary);font-size:14px">
                        ${escapeHtml((client||'').slice(0,1) || '体')}
                    </div>
                    <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
                      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px">
                        <div style="color:#6b7280;font-size:13px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">
                          ${escapeHtml(mainDate)} · ${escapeHtml(project)}
                        </div>
                        <div style="color:#6b7280;font-size:12px">订单ID:${escapeHtml(String(id))}</div>
                      </div>
                      <div style="font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;margin-bottom:6px">${escapeHtml(client)}</div>
                      <div style="color:#6b7280;margin-bottom:8px">${escapeHtml(info)}</div>
                      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
                        <button class="pg-btn" data-mainid="${escapeHtml(String(id))}" data-act="view">查看详情</button>
                        <button class="pg-btn" data-mainid="${escapeHtml(String(id))}" data-act="open">打开弹窗</button>
                        <div style="margin-left:auto;color:#6b7280;font-size:12px">子项目: ${Array.isArray(o.items) ? o.items.length : 0}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                `;
                listWrap.appendChild(card);
            });
            // 绑定按钮事件（统一处理 view 与 open）
            listWrap.querySelectorAll('button[data-mainid]').forEach(b=>{
                b.addEventListener('click', function(){
                    const mid = this.getAttribute('data-mainid');
                    openMainDetail(mid);
                });
            });
        }
        // update totals & pagination
        document.getElementById('totalCount').textContent = state.total || 0;
        const pg = document.getElementById('pagination');
        const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((state.total || 0) / state.size));
        if ((state.total || 0) > state.size) {
            pg.style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('pgNum').textContent = state.page;
            document.getElementById('pgTotal').textContent = totalPages;
            document.getElementById('prevPage').disabled = state.page <= 1;
            document.getElementById('nextPage').disabled = state.page >= totalPages;
        } else {
            pg.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }
    // open main order detail in overlay using data already loaded in state.orders
    function openMainDetail(mainId) {
        // try find in current state.orders first
        const orders = Array.isArray(state.orders) ? state.orders : [];
        let main = orders.find(x => String(x.id) === String(mainId) || String(x.main_id) === String(mainId));
        if (!main) {
            // fallback: fetch single main from backend
            ajax(API.byMain + encodeURIComponent(mainId), 'GET').then(res=>{
                if (!res || res.code !== 0) { alert(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '获取失败'); return; }
                // 后端返回的 res.data 属于单个主订单对象（按你后端实现）
                fillDetailOverlay(res.data);
                showOverlay();
            }).catch(err=>{ alert('请求失败：' + (err.message||err)); });
            return;
        }
        fillDetailOverlay(main);
        showOverlay();
    }
    // fill overlay content: leftList and detailRight
    function fillDetailOverlay(main) {
        state.currentMain = main;
        const clientName = main.clientName || main.client_name || '';
        const mainDate = main.date || '';
        document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = (clientName ? (clientName + ' 的检查') : '检查详情');
        document.getElementById('modalSubtitle').textContent = mainDate ? ('检查时间：' + mainDate) : '';
        const leftList = document.getElementById('leftList');
        leftList.innerHTML = '';
        // items 可能是 [] 或 null
        const items = Array.isArray(main.items) ? main.items : [];
        // details 可能是 [] 或 null
        const details = Array.isArray(main.details) ? main.details : [];
        // 建立 itemId -> details[] 的映射（一个 item 可能有多条 detail）
        const detailsMap = {};
        details.forEach(d => {
            const key = String(d.itemId || d.item_id || d.itemID || '');
            if (!detailsMap[key]) detailsMap[key] = [];
            detailsMap[key].push(d);
        });
        // 如果 items 为空，但 details 有（后端可能只返回 details），尝试从 details 中推导 items 列表
        let derivedItems = items;
        if (derivedItems.length === 0 && details.length > 0) {
            // 按 itemId 分组并创建简略 item
            const itemIds = Array.from(new Set(details.map(d => String(d.itemId || d.item_id || d.itemID || '')))).filter(x=>x);
            derivedItems = itemIds.map(id => ({ id: id, name: '子项 ' + id, info: '' }));
        }
        if (derivedItems.length === 0) {
            leftList.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:8px;color:#6b7280">该订单没有子项目</div>';
            document.getElementById('detailRight').innerHTML = '<div style="color:#6b7280">无子项可显示</div>';
            return;
        }
        // 构建左侧的每一项
        derivedItems.forEach((it, idx) => {
            const iid = it.id || it.itemId || it.item_id || String(idx);
            const itsName = it.name || it.title || ('子项 ' + iid);
            const itsInfo = it.info || '';
            const dsForItem = detailsMap[String(iid)] || [];
            // 取第一个 detail 的 comment/info/status 作为预览
            const preview = dsForItem.length > 0 ? (dsForItem[0].comment || dsForItem[0].info || dsForItem[0].status || '') : '';
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.style.minWidth = '160px';
            div.style.padding = '10px';
            div.style.borderRadius = '10px';
            div.style.background = '#fff';
            div.style.border = '1px solid rgba(16,24,40,0.04)';
            div.style.marginRight = '8px';
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(String(itsName))}</div>
                <div style="color:#6b7280;margin-top:6px">${escapeHtml(String(itsInfo))}</div>
                <div style="margin-top:8px">${ preview ? ('<div style="font-weight:700;color:#111">'+escapeHtml(String(preview))+'</div>') : '<div style="color:#6b7280">无结果</div>'}</div>
                <div style="margin-top:8px"><button class="pg-btn" data-itemid="${escapeHtml(String(iid))}">查看</button></div>
            `;
            leftList.appendChild(div);
        });
        // 绑定查看按钮
        leftList.querySelectorAll('button[data-itemid]').forEach(b=>{
            b.addEventListener('click', function(){
                const itemId = this.getAttribute('data-itemid');
                showItemDetail(itemId);
            });
        });
        // 自动展示第一项
        const firstId = (derivedItems[0] && (derivedItems[0].id || derivedItems[0].itemId || derivedItems[0].item_id)) || null;
        if (firstId !== null) {
            showItemDetail(firstId);
        } else {
            document.getElementById('detailRight').innerHTML = '<div style="color:#6b7280">无子项</div>';
        }
    }
    function showItemDetail(itemId) {
        const main = state.currentMain;
        if (!main) return;
        const items = Array.isArray(main.items) ? main.items : [];
        const details = Array.isArray(main.details) ? main.details : [];
        // 找 item（优先按 items 数组查找，否则用占位）
        let item = items.find(it => String(it.id) === String(itemId) || String(it.itemId) === String(itemId));
        if (!item) {
            // 如果 items 不存在，则从 details 中查找信息作为备选
            const d0 = details.find(d => String(d.itemId) === String(itemId) || String(d.item_id) === String(itemId));
            item = { id: itemId, name: d0 && (d0.name || ('子项 ' + itemId)) || ('子项 ' + itemId), info: '' };
        }
        // 找出该 item 对应的所有 detail（可能多条）
        const itemDetails = details.filter(d => String(d.itemId) === String(itemId) || String(d.item_id) === String(itemId));
        const right = document.getElementById('detailRight');
        right.innerHTML = `<div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(item.name || ('子项 ' + itemId))}</div><div style="color:#6b7280;margin-top:6px">${escapeHtml(item.info || '')}</div><div style="margin-top:10px" id="detailContent"></div>`;
        const container = document.getElementById('detailContent');
        container.innerHTML = `<div style="color:#6b7280;margin-bottom:8px">检查时间： ${escapeHtml(main.date || '')} · 项目编号：${escapeHtml(String(itemId))}</div>`;
        if (itemDetails.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML += `<div style="padding:8px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,24,40,0.04);background:#fff;color:#6b7280">暂无检测结果</div>`;
            return;
        }
        // 展示每一条 detail（若 info 为 JSON，则解析并逐键显示）
        itemDetails.forEach(d => {
            // 友好化字段名
            const info = d.info;
            const comment = d.comment;
            const status = d.status;
            let parsed = null;
            if (info && typeof info === 'string') {
                try {
                    const s = info.trim();
                    if (s.startsWith('{') || s.startsWith('[')) parsed = JSON.parse(s);
                } catch(e){
                    parsed = null;
                }
            } else if (info && typeof info === 'object') {
                parsed = info;
            }
            if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
                // 展示对象内的每个键值
                const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                wrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:8px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,24,40,0.04);background:#fff;margin-bottom:8px';
                Object.keys(parsed).forEach(k=>{
                    const v = parsed[k];
                    const val = (typeof v === 'object') ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v);
                    const row = document.createElement('div');
                    row.style.display = 'flex';
                    row.style.justifyContent = 'space-between';
                    row.style.marginBottom = '6px';
                    row.innerHTML = `<div style="color:#6b7280">${escapeHtml(k)}</div><div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(val)}</div>`;
                    wrapper.appendChild(row);
                });
                container.appendChild(wrapper);
            } else {
                // 直接展示 info / comment / status
                if (info) {
                    container.innerHTML += `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,24,40,0.04);background:#fff;margin-bottom:8px"><div style="color:#6b7280">信息</div><div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(info)}</div></div>`;
                }
                if (comment) {
                    container.innerHTML += `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,24,40,0.04);background:#fff;margin-bottom:8px"><div style="color:#6b7280">备注/结论</div><div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(comment)}</div></div>`;
                }
                if (status != null) {
                    container.innerHTML += `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,24,40,0.04);background:#fff;margin-bottom:8px"><div style="color:#6b7280">状态</div><div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(String(status))}</div></div>`;
                }
            }
        });
    }
    function showOverlay(){ document.getElementById('detailOverlay').style.display='flex'; document.body.style.overflow='hidden'; }
    function hideOverlay(){ document.getElementById('detailOverlay').style.display='none'; document.body.style.overflow=''; }
    document.getElementById('closeDetailOverlay').addEventListener('click', hideOverlay);
    document.getElementById('btnCloseDetail').addEventListener('click', hideOverlay);
    // fetch list from backend (with pagination)
    function fetchList(page = 1, size = 10) {
        if (!state.userId) { console.warn('请先在 localStorage 中设置 id'); return; }
        state.page = page;
        state.size = size;
        const params = { userId: state.userId, page: state.page, size: state.size };
        ajax(API.list, 'GET', params).then(res=>{
            if (!res || res.code !== 0) { alert(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '请求失败'); return; }
            const data = res.data || {};
            state.total = data.total || 0;
            // orders: list of orders with fields including items and details
            // 防御：确保 orders 是数组
            state.orders = Array.isArray(data.orders) ? data.orders : [];
            renderOrders();
        }).catch(err=>{ console.error(err); alert('网络或服务器错误：' + (err.message || err)); });
    }
    // pagination handlers
    document.getElementById('prevPage').addEventListener('click', function(){
        if (state.page > 1) fetchList(state.page - 1, state.size);
    });
    document.getElementById('nextPage').addEventListener('click', function(){
        const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((state.total || 0) / state.size));
        if (state.page < totalPages) fetchList(state.page + 1, state.size);
    });
    // refresh & search
    document.getElementById('refresh').addEventListener('click', function(){ document.getElementById('kw').value=''; fetchList(1, state.size); });
    document.getElementById('kw').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if (e.key === 'Enter') { /* optionally implement client-side filtering */ fetchList(1, state.size); }});
    // initial load
    fetchList(1, 10);
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    layui.use(['layer', 'element'], function(){
        var layer = layui.layer;
        var element = layui.element;
        // 模拟加载进度
        element.progress('demo', '50%');
        // 卡片点击效果
        var cards = document.querySelectorAll('.health-card');
        cards.forEach(function(card) {
            card.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var title = this.querySelector('.card-title span').textContent;
                layer.msg('您点击了: ' + title, {icon: 1});
            });
        });
        // 统计数据动画
        function animateValue(obj, start, end, duration) {
            let startTimestamp = null;
            const step = (timestamp) => {
                if (!startTimestamp) startTimestamp = timestamp;
                const progress = Math.min((timestamp - startTimestamp) / duration, 1);
                obj.innerHTML = Math.floor(progress * (end - start) + start);
                if (progress < 1) {
                    window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
                }
            };
            window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
        }
        // 页面加载完成后启动数字动画
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const valueElements = document.querySelectorAll('.stat-value');
            animateValue(valueElements[0], 0, 8542, 2000);
            animateValue(valueElements[1], 0, 420, 2000);
            animateValue(valueElements[2], 0, 72, 2000);
            animateValue(valueElements[3], 0, 7.5, 2000);
        });
    });
</script>

        .panel { background:#fff; border-radius:8px; box-shadow:var(--card-shadow); overflow:hidden; }
        #demoAdmin { width:100%; border:0; display:block; min-height:600px; border-radius:8px; }
        .layui-footer { text-align:center; color:var(--text-light); background:transparent; padding:15px 0; font-size:13px; border-top:1px solid var(--border-color); }
        /* 用户下拉：独立绝对定位 */
        .user-dropdown {
            position:absolute; right:16px; top:64px; background:#fff; border-radius:6px; box-shadow:0 8px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.12);
            min-width:180px; z-index:1200; overflow:visible; display:none;
        }
        .user-dropdown a { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; padding:10px 12px; color:#333; text-decoration:none; }
        .user-dropdown a:hover { background: rgba(30,159,255,0.04); color: var(--primary-color); }
        /* 响应 */
        @media (max-width: 992px) {
            .top-search { width:200px; }
            .user-name { display:none; }
        }
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
            .layui-side { position:fixed; transform:translateX(-100%); height:100%; box-shadow:5px 0 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); }
            .layui-side.layui-side-show { transform:translateX(0); }
            .layui-body { padding:15px; }
            .page-header { flex-direction:column; align-items:flex-start; gap:12px; }
            .header-actions { width:100%; justify-content:flex-end; }
            .top-search { display:none; }
            .user-dropdown { right:8px; top:56px; }
        }
        /* 动画 */
        @keyframes fadeIn { from { opacity:0; transform:translateY(10px);} to { opacity:1; transform:translateY(0);} }
        .fade-in { animation: fadeIn 0.45s ease forwards; }
        /* 滚动条 */
        ::-webkit-scrollbar { width:6px; height:6px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background:#f1f1f1; border-radius:10px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background:#c1c1c1; border-radius:10px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background:var(--primary-color); }
    </style>

                        <i class="layui-icon">&#xe615;</i>

                        <i class="layui-icon top-icon" id="btnNotifications" title="通知" aria-label="notifications">&#xe63a;</i>
                        <span class="notification-badge">3</span>
                    </div>

                    <div id="userArea" style="position:relative;">

<script src="../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['element','layer','util','laypage','form'], function(){
        var element = layui.element;
        var layer = layui.layer;
        var util = layui.util;
        var $ = layui.$;
        // 退出登录
        function logout(){
            layer.confirm('确定要退出登录吗？', {
                title: '退出确认',
                icon: 3,
                btn: ['确定', '取消']
            }, function(index){
                layer.close(index);
                localStorage.removeItem('token');
                window.location.href = 'login.html';
            });
        }
        // 绑定 logout btn（兼容在下拉内）
        $('#logoutBtn').on('click', logout);
        // 初始化：让侧栏子菜单默认收起
        $('.layui-side .layui-nav-child').hide();
        $('.layui-side .layui-nav-item').removeClass('layui-nav-itemed open');
        // 侧栏折叠 / 展开（桌面）
        var sideCollapsed = false;
        function onSideToggle(collapsed){
            if(collapsed){
                // 折叠：隐藏文本（由 CSS 控制），同时确保所有子菜单被隐藏并移除 open 标记
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-child').hide();
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-item').removeClass('open');
            } else {
                // 展开：保持默认收起（用户点击父项再展开）
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-child').hide();
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-item').removeClass('open');
            }
        }
        $('#btnToggleSide').on('click', function(){
            sideCollapsed = !sideCollapsed;
            if(sideCollapsed){
                $('.layui-side').addClass('layui-side-collapsed');
                onSideToggle(true);
            } else {
                $('.layui-side').removeClass('layui-side-collapsed');
                onSideToggle(false);
            }
        });
        // 侧栏父项点击：展开/收起子菜单（仅在未折叠时生效）
        $('.layui-side .layui-nav > .layui-nav-item > a').on('click', function(e){
            var $parent = $(this).parent();
            var $child = $parent.children('.layui-nav-child').first();
            if($child.length){
                e.preventDefault();
                if($('.layui-side').hasClass('layui-side-collapsed')) return; // 折叠时不展开
                // 切换 open
                if($parent.hasClass('open')){
                    $parent.removeClass('open');
                    $child.stop(true,true).slideUp(180);
                } else {
                    // 可选择先关闭其他 open（若需要 accordion 行为）
                    // $('.layui-side .layui-nav-item.open').removeClass('open').children('.layui-nav-child').slideUp(120);
                    $parent.addClass('open');
                    $child.stop(true,true).slideDown(180);
                }
            }
        });
        // 移动端侧栏显示或快速新增弹窗
        $('#btnQuickAdd').on('click', function(){
            if(window.innerWidth < 768){
                $('#sideBar').toggleClass('layui-side-show');
            } else {
                layer.open({
                    title: '快速新增',
                    area: ['420px', '340px'],
                    content: '<div style="padding:18px;">' +
                        '<div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:14px; text-align:center;">' +
                        '<div class="quick-action-btn" data-type="project" style="cursor:pointer; padding:12px; border-radius:6px; background:#f7fbff;"><i class="layui-icon" style="font-size:22px;">&#xe654;</i><div style="margin-top:6px;">新增套餐</div></div>' +
                        '<div class="quick-action-btn" data-type="item" style="cursor:pointer; padding:12px; border-radius:6px; background:#f7fbff;"><i class="layui-icon" style="font-size:22px;">&#xe654;</i><div style="margin-top:6px;">新增检查项</div></div>' +
                        '<div class="quick-action-btn" data-type="order" style="cursor:pointer; padding:12px; border-radius:6px; background:#f7fbff;"><i class="layui-icon" style="font-size:22px;">&#xe654;</i><div style="margin-top:6px;">新增订单</div></div>' +
                        '<div class="quick-action-btn" data-type="user" style="cursor:pointer; padding:12px; border-radius:6px; background:#f7fbff;"><i class="layui-icon" style="font-size:22px;">&#xe654;</i><div style="margin-top:6px;">新增用户</div></div>' +
                        '</div></div>'
                });
            }
        });
        // 顶部搜索回车
        $('#globalSearch').on('keydown', function(e){
            if(e.key === 'Enter'){
                var q = $(this).val().trim();
                if(!q){ layer.msg('请输入搜索关键词'); return; }
                $('#demoAdmin').attr('src', './search.html?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q));
                $('#breadcrumb').text('搜索 / ' + q);
            }
        });
        // 侧栏子菜单链接点击：改变 iframe src 并更新面包屑
        $('.layui-side-scroll .layui-nav-tree a[data-url]').on('click', function(e){
            var url = $(this).data('url');
            var text = $(this).text().trim();
            if(url){
                $('#demoAdmin').attr('src', url);
                $('#breadcrumb').text('管理 / ' + text);
                if(window.innerWidth < 768){
                    $('#sideBar').removeClass('layui-side-show');
                }
                $('.page-title').html('<i class="fas fa-chart-line"></i> ' + text);
            }
        });
        // 默认加载第一个子菜单项（如果你需要默认打开第一个内部页面）
        var firstSub = $('.layui-side-scroll .layui-nav-tree .layui-nav-child a[data-url]').first();
        if(firstSub.length>0){
            $('#demoAdmin').attr('src', firstSub.data('url'));
            $('#breadcrumb').text('管理 / ' + firstSub.text().trim());
        }
        // 刷新 iframe
        $('#btnRefresh').on('click', function(){
            var iframe = document.getElementById('demoAdmin');
            try {
                iframe.contentWindow.location.reload();
            } catch (e) {
                var src = $('#demoAdmin').attr('src');
                $('#demoAdmin').attr('src', src);
            }
            layer.msg('已刷新', {icon:1, time:800});
        });
        // 全屏 iframe
        $('#btnFull').on('click', function(){
            var el = document.getElementById('demoAdmin');
            if (el.requestFullscreen) { el.requestFullscreen(); }
            else if (el.mozRequestFullScreen) { el.mozRequestFullScreen(); }
            else if (el.webkitRequestFullscreen) { el.webkitRequestFullscreen(); }
            else if (el.msRequestFullscreen) { el.msRequestFullscreen(); }
        });
        // 打开个人中心（iframe）
        $('#openProfile').on('click', function(e){
            e.preventDefault();
            $('#demoAdmin').attr('src','./user-profile.html');
            $('#breadcrumb').text('个人中心');
            $('.page-title').html('<i class="fas fa-user-cog"></i> 个人中心');
            hideUserDropdown();
        });
        // 通知按钮
        $('#btnNotifications').on('click', function(){
            layer.open({
                title:'系统通知',
                area: ['420px', '360px'],
                content: '<div style="padding:14px;">' +
                    '<div style="margin-bottom:12px;"><strong>系统更新通知</strong><div style="font-size:12px;color:#888;margin-top:6px;">2023-06-15 10:30</div><div style="margin-top:8px;color:#555;">系统将于今晚凌晨2点进行维护更新，预计耗时2小时。</div></div>' +
                    '<div><strong>新订单提醒</strong><div style="font-size:12px;color:#888;margin-top:6px;">2023-06-15 09:15</div><div style="margin-top:8px;color:#555;">您有3个新订单需要处理。</div></div>' +
                    '</div>'
            });
        });
        // iframe 自适应高度
        function adjustIframeHeight(){
            var headerH = $('.layui-header').outerHeight() || 64;
            var footerH = $('.layui-footer').outerHeight() || 40;
            var winH = $(window).height();
            var avail = winH - headerH - footerH - 80;
            $('#demoAdmin').css('min-height', Math.max(avail, 360) + 'px');
        }
        adjustIframeHeight();
        $(window).on('resize', adjustIframeHeight);
        // 用户下拉逻辑（独立容器）
        var $userTrigger = $('#userTrigger');
        var $userDropdown = $('#userDropdown');
        function showUserDropdown(){
            var winW = $(window).width();
            if(winW < 768){
                $userDropdown.css({ right: 8, top: $('.layui-header').outerHeight() - 8 });
            } else {
                $userDropdown.css({ right: 16, top: $('.layui-header').outerHeight() });
            }
            $userDropdown.show();
            $userTrigger.attr('aria-expanded','true');
            $userDropdown.attr('aria-hidden','false');
        }
        function hideUserDropdown(){
            $userDropdown.hide();
            $userTrigger.attr('aria-expanded','false');
            $userDropdown.attr('aria-hidden','true');
        }
        $userTrigger.on('click', function(e){
            e.stopPropagation();
            if($userDropdown.is(':visible')) hideUserDropdown();
            else showUserDropdown();
        });
        $userDropdown.on('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
        $(document).on('click', function(){ hideUserDropdown(); });
        // 点击快速新增中弹窗内按钮的代理
        $(document).on('click', '.quick-action-btn', function(){
            var type = $(this).data('type');
            layer.msg('快速新增：' + type);
            layer.closeAll('page');
        });
        // 窗口 resize 事件确保侧栏折叠/展开时子菜单状态一致
        $(window).on('resize', function(){
            if($('.layui-side').hasClass('layui-side-collapsed')){
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-child').hide();
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-item').removeClass('open');
            } else {
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-child').hide();
                $('.layui-side .layui-nav-item').removeClass('open');
            }
        }).trigger('resize');
        // iframe 错误处理（可选）
        $('#demoAdmin').on('error', function(){
            $(this).attr('src','about:blank');
        });
    });
</script>
</body>

<body>
<div class="wrap">
    <div class="head">
        <div>
            <h2>购买历史</h2>
            <div class="muted" id="userHint">移动优先视图，支持卡片和表格两种展示</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
            <button class="layui-btn btn-export" id="btnExport">导出</button>
            <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-danger" id="btnBatchDel">批量删除</button>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel">
        <div class="filter-toggle">
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
                <span class="badge" id="resultCount">0</span>
                <div class="small-muted" id="descCount">条记录</div>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
                <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="btnToggleFilters">筛选</button>
                <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-warm" id="btnSwitchView">切换表格</button>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="filters" id="filters">
            <div class="row" style="gap:8px">
                <div class="layui-input-inline" style="flex:1">
                    <input type="text" id="q" placeholder="客户/手机号/套餐" class="layui-input">
                </div>
                <div class="layui-input-inline" style="width:240px">
                    <input type="text" id="dateRange" placeholder="时间范围" class="layui-input">
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="row" style="gap:8px;align-items:center">
                <div class="layui-input-inline" style="width:160px">
                    <select id="filterPay" class="layui-select">
                        <option value="">支付方式（全部）</option>
                        <option value="online">在线支付</option>
                        <option value="card">实卡转入</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div class="layui-input-inline" style="width:160px">
                    <select id="filterStatus" class="layui-select">
                        <option value="">状态（全部）</option>
                        <option value="1">已支付</option>
                        <option value="0">未支付</option>
                        <option value="2">已退款</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div style="margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:8px">
                    <button class="layui-btn" id="btnSearch">查询</button>
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="btnReset">重置</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="list-cards" id="cardList"></div>
        <div id="loadMoreWrap" style="text-align:center"><button id="loadMore" class="load-more">加载更多</button></div>
        <div class="table-wrap" id="tableWrap">
            <table class="layui-table" lay-size="sm">
                <colgroup>
                    <col width="40"><col width="160"><col width="160"><col><col width="80"><col width="90"><col width="90"><col width="110"><col width="120">
                </colgroup>
                <thead>
                <tr>
                    <th><input type="checkbox" id="chkAll"></th>
                    <th>时间</th>
                    <th>客户</th>
                    <th>套餐</th>
                    <th>数量</th>
                    <th>单价</th>
                    <th>总价</th>
                    <th>支付方式</th>
                    <th>操作</th>
                </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody id="tbodyTable"></tbody>
            </table>
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:12px">
                <div class="muted">共 <span id="totalCount">0</span> 条记录</div>
                <div id="pager"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div id="reservePanel" class="panel" style="margin-top:12px; display:none">
            <div class="reserve-header">
                <div><strong>发起预约</strong></div>
                <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="closeReserve">关闭</button>
                </div>
            </div>
            <form class="layui-form" id="clientForm" style="margin-top:6px;">
                <input type="hidden" name="payinfo_id" value="0" />
                <div class="reserve-grid">
                    <div class="reserve-field">
                        <label class="small-muted">联系人</label>
                        <input type="text" name="client_name" placeholder="联系人姓名或用户名" class="layui-input">
                    </div>
                    <div class="reserve-field">
                        <label class="small-muted">联系电话</label>
                        <input type="text" name="phone" placeholder="手机号（不强制校验）" class="layui-input">
                    </div>
                    <div class="reserve-field">
                        <label class="small-muted">项目ID</label>
                        <input type="number" name="project_id" min="0" value="0" class="layui-input">
                    </div>
                    <div class="reserve-field">
                        <label class="small-muted">预约日期</label>
                        <input type="text" id="todate" name="todate" placeholder="选择预约日期" class="layui-input" readonly>
                    </div>
                    <div class="reserve-field">
                        <label class="small-muted">预约时间</label>
                        <input type="text" id="totime" name="totime" placeholder="选择预约时间" class="layui-input" readonly>
                    </div>
                    <div class="reserve-field">
                        <label class="small-muted">备注</label>
                        <input type="text" name="remark" placeholder="可选：填写具体需求或备注" class="layui-input">
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="reserve-actions">
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-normal" id="btnSubmit" type="submit" style="width:200px;">提交预约</button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>
