<script src="../../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script src="../../layui/layui.all.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['layer','laydate','form','jquery'], function(){
        var layer = layui.layer, laydate = layui.laydate, form = layui.form, $ = layui.$;
        // API base，按后端部署调整
        var API_BASE = '/';
        // 工具
        function escapeHtml(s){ return String(s||'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(m){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m];}); }
        function apiFetch(path, opts){
            opts = opts || {};
            opts.headers = opts.headers || {};
            if(!(opts.body instanceof FormData)) {
                opts.headers['Content-Type'] = opts.headers['Content-Type'] || 'application/json;charset=utf-8';
            }
            opts.credentials = opts.credentials || 'same-origin';
            if(opts.body && typeof opts.body === 'object' && !(opts.body instanceof FormData)) {
                opts.body = JSON.stringify(opts.body);
            }
            var url = path.indexOf('http')===0 ? path : (API_BASE + path.replace(/^\//,''));
            return fetch(url, opts)
                .then(function(resp){
                    if(!resp.ok) throw new Error('Network response not ok: ' + resp.status);
                    return resp.json().catch(function(){ return null; });
                });
        }
        var state = { q:'', startDate:'', endDate:'', payMode:'', status:'', page:1, limit:8, view:'card', filtered:[], total:0 };
        // 初始化日期/时间选择器
        laydate.render({ elem: '#dateRange', range: ' 到 ', trigger: 'click' });
        laydate.render({ elem: '#todate', type: 'date', min: 0, trigger: 'click' });
        laydate.render({ elem: '#totime', type: 'time', trigger: 'click' });
        // 加载列表（带筛选）
        function loadList(page, size, q, payMode, status, startDate, endDate){
            page = page || 1; size = size || 100;
            var params = '?page='+encodeURIComponent(page) + '&size=' + encodeURIComponent(size);
            if(q) params += '&q=' + encodeURIComponent(q);
            if(payMode) params += '&paymode=' + encodeURIComponent(payMode);
            if(status !== undefined && status !== '') params += '&paystatus=' + encodeURIComponent(status);
            if(startDate) params += '&start=' + encodeURIComponent(startDate);
            if(endDate) params += '&end=' + encodeURIComponent(endDate);
            return apiFetch('/reservation/list' + params, { method: 'GET' })
                .then(function(json){
                    var payload = json && (json.data || json) || {};
                    var total = payload.total || payload.totalCount || 0;
                    var records = payload.records || payload || [];
                    if(!Array.isArray(records)) records = [];
                    state.filtered = records;
                    state.total = total;
                    $('#resultCount').text(total);
                    $('#totalCount').text(total);
                    renderCards(true);
                    renderTable();
                    return payload;
                })
                .catch(function(err){
                    console.warn('加载列表失败', err);
                    layer.msg('加载失败');
                });
        }
        // 与详情同等的套餐名称解析（尽量复用 openDetail 的策略）
        function getPackageNameFromRecord(rec, project){
            if(!rec) return '';
            project = project || rec.project || rec.projectObj || rec.projectItem || rec.projectInfo || rec.projectData || null;
            // 优先简单字段
            var first = rec.projectName || rec.project_name || rec.packageName || rec.package_name || rec.title || rec.name || rec.product_name || rec.goodsName;
            if(first && typeof first === 'string' && first.trim()) return first.trim();
            // project 可能为字符串或对象
            if(typeof rec.project === 'string' && rec.project.trim()) return rec.project.trim();
            if(project && typeof project === 'string' && project.trim()) return project.trim();
            // project 对象尝试常见字段
            if(project && typeof project === 'object'){
                var n = project.name || project.title || project.projectName || project.project_name || project.getName && (typeof project.getName === 'function' ? project.getName() : undefined);
                if(n && typeof n === 'string' && n.trim()) return n.trim();
                // 深层尝试
                if(project.data && typeof project.data === 'object'){
                    var dn = project.data.name || project.data.title;
                    if(dn && typeof dn === 'string' && dn.trim()) return dn.trim();
                }
                if(project.attributes && typeof project.attributes === 'object'){
                    var an = project.attributes.name || project.attributes.title;
                    if(an && typeof an === 'string' && an.trim()) return an.trim();
                }
            }
            // 有时记录内包含 payload/project 的不同命名
            var alt = rec.projectObj || rec.project_item || rec.projectInfo || rec.payload || rec.data;
            if(alt){
                if(typeof alt === 'string' && alt.trim()) return alt.trim();
                if(typeof alt === 'object'){
                    var mn = alt.name || alt.title || alt.projectName || alt.packageName;
                    if(mn && typeof mn === 'string' && mn.trim()) return mn.trim();
                    if(alt.data && typeof alt.data === 'object'){
                        var md = alt.data.name || alt.data.title;
                        if(md && typeof md === 'string' && md.trim()) return md.trim();
                    }
                }
            }
            // 最后尝试 rec.project（不同大小写）和 rec.title/rec.name
            var fallback = rec.title || rec.name || rec.planName || rec.product_name || rec.package;
            if(fallback && typeof fallback === 'string' && fallback.trim()) return fallback.trim();
            // 未找到则返回空字符串（调用端决定回退显示）
            return '';
        }
        // renderCards：列表处使用与详情相同的解析逻辑，只有当解析不到名称时才回退到显示“记录ID ...”
        function renderCards(reset){
            var list = state.filtered || [];
            if(reset) state.page = 1;
            var end = state.page * state.limit;
            var slice = list.slice(0, end);
            var wrap = document.getElementById('cardList');
            wrap.innerHTML = '';
            if(slice.length === 0){
                wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="empty" style="margin-top:12px;">暂无记录</div>';
                document.getElementById('loadMore').style.display = 'none';
                return;
            }
            slice.forEach(function(item){
                var projectCandidate = item.project || item.projectObj || item.projectInfo || item.project_item || item.projectItem || null;
                var packageName = getPackageNameFromRecord(item, projectCandidate) || (item.title || item.name || '');
                var displayTitle = packageName && packageName.trim() ? packageName : ('记录ID ' + (item.id||''));
                var payLabel = (item.payMode || item.paymode || (item.paystatus==1 ? 'online' : 'card')) === 'card'? '实卡转入' : '在线支付';
                var statusLabel = (item.paystatus===1)? '<span style="color:green">已支付</span>' : '<span style="color:#ff9900">未支付</span>';
                var client = item.clientName || item.client || item.client_name || item.clientTel || item.client_tel || '-';
                var tel = item.clientTel || item.client_tel || item.phone || item.mobile || '';
                var card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'card-item';
                card.setAttribute('data-id', item.id);
                card.innerHTML = `
          <div class="card-left" style="flex:1;">
            <div class="card-title">${escapeHtml(displayTitle)}</div>
            <div class="card-meta">${escapeHtml(client)} <span class="small-muted" style="margin-left:6px;">${escapeHtml(tel)}</span></div>
            <div class="card-meta">${escapeHtml(item.date || item.createdAt || item.time || '')} · ${payLabel} · ${statusLabel}</div>
            <div class="card-foot" style="margin-top:6px;">
              <span class="badge" style="background:#eef2ff;color:#3b82f6;border-radius:999px;padding:4px 8px;font-size:12px;">记录ID ${item.id}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
                const btns = document.createElement('div');
                btns.style.display = 'flex';
                btns.style.alignItems = 'center';
                btns.style.gap = '8px';
                btns.style.marginLeft = '8px';
                var viewBtn = document.createElement('button');
                viewBtn.className = 'layui-btn layui-btn-xs';
                viewBtn.textContent = '查看';
                viewBtn.setAttribute('data-id', item.id);
                viewBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){ openDetail(item.id); });
                var reserveBtn = document.createElement('button');
                reserveBtn.className = 'btn btn-reserve layui-btn-xs';
                reserveBtn.textContent = '预约';
                reserveBtn.setAttribute('data-id', item.id);
                reserveBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){ openReserveForRecord(item); });
                var delBtn = document.createElement('button');
                delBtn.className = 'layui-btn layui-btn-danger layui-btn-xs';
                delBtn.textContent = '删除';
                delBtn.setAttribute('data-id', item.id);
                delBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){ deletePayinfo(item.id); });
                btns.appendChild(viewBtn);
                btns.appendChild(reserveBtn);
                btns.appendChild(delBtn);
                card.appendChild(btns);
                wrap.appendChild(card);
            });
            if(end < list.length) document.getElementById('loadMore').style.display = 'block';
            else document.getElementById('loadMore').style.display = 'none';
        }
        // renderTable：同样使用相同解析逻辑
        function renderTable(){
            var list = state.filtered || [];
            var end = state.page * 10;
            var slice = list.slice(0, end);
            var tbody = document.getElementById('tbodyTable');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            slice.forEach(function(r){
                var projectCandidate = r.project || r.projectObj || r.projectInfo || r.project_item || null;
                var packageName = getPackageNameFromRecord(r, projectCandidate) || (r.title || r.name || '');
                var displayTitle = packageName && packageName.trim() ? packageName : ('记录ID ' + (r.id||''));
                var payLabel = (r.payMode || r.paymode || (r.paystatus==1 ? 'online' : 'card'))==='card'? '实卡转入' : '在线支付';
                var clientName = r.clientName || r.client || r.client_name || '';
                var clientTel = r.clientTel || r.client_tel || r.phone || r.mobile || '';
                var tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.innerHTML = `
          <td><input type="checkbox" class="chk" data-id="${r.id}"></td>
          <td>${escapeHtml(r.date || r.createdAt || '')}</td>
          <td>${escapeHtml(clientName)}<div class="small-muted" style="font-size:12px">${escapeHtml(clientTel)}</div></td>
          <td>${escapeHtml(displayTitle)}</td>
          <td>${r.qty || 1}</td>
          <td>${(Number(r.price||0)).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td>${(Number(r.total||r.price||0)).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td>${payLabel}</td>
          <td>
            <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-xs viewBtn" data-id="${r.id}">查看</button>
            <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-danger layui-btn-xs delBtn" data-id="${r.id}">删除</button>
          </td>
        `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
            $(document).off('click', '.viewBtn').on('click', '.viewBtn', function(){ openDetail($(this).data('id')); });
            $(document).off('click', '.delBtn').on('click', '.delBtn', function(){ deletePayinfo($(this).data('id')); });
        }
        // --------------- 新版详情与检查项渲染（兼容后端增强 /reservation/detail 返回结构） -------------------
        function openDetail(id){
            layer.load(2);
            apiFetch('/reservation/detail?id=' + encodeURIComponent(id), { method:'GET' })
                .then(function(json){
                    layer.closeAll('loading');
                    var payload = json && (json.data || json) || null;
                    if(!payload){
                        layer.msg('未找到记录');
                        return;
                    }
                    // 支持 payload.payinfo 或 payload 本身为 payinfo
                    var rec = payload.payinfo ? payload.payinfo : payload;
                    if(!rec && Array.isArray(payload) && payload.length>0) rec = payload[0];
                    if(!rec){
                        layer.msg('未找到记录数据');
                        return;
                    }
                    var client = payload.client || payload.clientObj || null;
                    var card = payload.card || null;
                    var project = payload.project || null;
                    var projectItems = payload.projectItems || null;
                    var payModeLabel = (rec.payMode || rec.paymode || (rec.paystatus==1 ? 'online' : 'card')) === 'card' ? '实卡转入' : '在线支付';
                    var statusLabel = rec.paystatus === 1 ? '<span style="color:#67c23a">已支付</span>' : (rec.paystatus === 2 ? '<span style="color:#999">已退款</span>' : '<span style="color:#909399">未支付</span>');
                    var price = (Number(rec.total || rec.price || 0)).toFixed(2);
                    var html = '<div style="position:relative;padding:18px;max-width:900px;box-sizing:border-box;">';
                    html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:18px;flex-wrap:wrap;">';
                    html += '<div style="flex:1;min-width:280px;">';
                    var title = rec.projectName || rec.project || (project && (project.name || project.getName && project.getName())) || '未命名套餐';
                    html += '<h3 style="margin:0 0 8px 0;color:#333;">' + escapeHtml(title) + '</h3>';
                    html += '<div style="margin-bottom:10px;color:#666;">' +
                        '<div>价格：<span style="color:#f56c6c;font-weight:600">¥' + price + '</span></div>' +
                        '<div>数量：' + (rec.qty || 1) + '</div>' +
                        '<div>支付方式：' + payModeLabel + '</div>' +
                        '<div>状态：' + statusLabel + '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    var pic = (project && (project.pic || project.image)) || rec.pic || rec.image || null;
                    if(pic){
                        html += '<div style="margin-bottom:10px;">封面：<br/><img src="' + escapeHtml(pic) + '" style="max-width:220px;max-height:140px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer" onclick="layer.photos({photos:{data:[{src:\'' + escapeHtml(pic) + '\'}]},anim:5})"></div>';
                    }
                    if(rec.remark){
                        html += '<div style="color:#333;margin-bottom:10px;"><strong>备注：</strong><div style="color:#444;line-height:1.6;margin-top:6px;">' + escapeHtml(rec.remark) + '</div></div>';
                    }
                    if(client){
                        var cname = client.nike || client.name || client.nick || client.nickname || '';
                        var tel = client.tel || client.tel2 || client.phone || client.mobile || '';
                        html += '<div style="color:#666;margin-bottom:8px;">客户：' + escapeHtml(cname || '') + ' <span class="small-muted">' + escapeHtml(tel || '') + '</span></div>';
                    }
                    html += '</div>';
                    html += '<div style="width:240px;flex-shrink:0;">';
                    html += '<div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #eef2f8;padding:12px;border-radius:6px;">';
                    html += '<div style="margin-bottom:8px;color:#666;">实付金额</div>';
                    html += '<div style="font-size:20px;color:#f56c6c;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;">¥' + price + '</div>';
                    var refundBtnText = (rec.payMode || rec.paymode || '') === 'online' ? '在线支付 - 请用支付渠道退款' : '退回到卡（实卡转入）';
                    html += '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;">';
                    html += '<button id="btnRefundNow" class="layui-btn layui-btn-warm" style="width:100%">' + escapeHtml(refundBtnText) + '</button>';
                    // 保留右侧区域内不显示关闭按钮（顶部已有）
                    html += '</div>';
                    html += '</div></div>';
                    html += '</div>';
                    html += '<div style="margin-top:16px;">';
                    html += '<h4 style="margin:0 0 8px 0;border-bottom:2px solid #409eff;padding-bottom:6px;display:inline-block;">包含的检查项</h4>';
                    html += '<div id="detailItemsArea" style="min-height:120px;padding-top:12px;color:#999">加载中...</div>';
                    html += '</div>';
                    html += '</div>';
                    var layerIndex = layer.open({
                        type: 1,
                        title: '购买详情（记录ID ' + (rec.id || '') + '）',
                        area: ['90%', '74%'],
                        content: html,
                        success: function(dom){
                            // 顶部关闭按钮
                            $(dom).find('#btnCloseTop').on('click', function(){ layer.close(layerIndex); });
                            $(dom).find('#btnRefundNow').on('click', function(){
                                var $btn = $(this);
                                if($btn.data('loading')) return;
                                $btn.data('loading', true);
                                var payMode = (rec.payMode || rec.paymode || '');
                                if(!payMode){
                                    payMode = card ? 'card' : 'online';
                                }
                                if(payMode === 'online'){
                                    layer.alert('该订单为在线支付，如需退款请在支付渠道/平台发起退款或联系管理员处理。', {title:'无法直接退回'});
                                    $btn.data('loading', false);
                                    return;
                                }
                                layer.confirm('确认将 ¥' + price + ' 退回至该实卡账户吗？（记录ID ' + rec.id + '）', function(confirmIdx){
                                    layer.close(confirmIdx);
                                    layer.load(2);
                                    apiFetch('/reservation/refund', {
                                        method: 'POST',
                                        body: { id: rec.id, amount: Number(price) }
                                    })
                                        .then(function(res){
                                            layer.closeAll('loading');
                                            $btn.data('loading', false);
                                            if(res && (res.code === 0 || res.success === true)){
                                                layer.msg('退款成功', {icon:1});
                                                layer.close(layerIndex);
                                                loadList(1, 100, state.q, state.payMode, state.status, state.startDate, state.endDate);
                                            } else {
                                                var msg = (res && (res.msg || res.message)) || '退款失败';
                                                layer.alert('退款失败：' + msg);
                                            }
                                        })
                                        .catch(function(err){
                                            console.error('refund error', err);
                                            layer.closeAll('loading');
                                            $btn.data('loading', false);
                                            layer.alert('退款请求失败，请稍后重试或联系管理员');
                                        });
                                });
                            });
                            // 渲染检查项：优先使用 projectItems.selected，如果没有则发起回退请求
                            if(projectItems && (Array.isArray(projectItems.selected) && projectItems.selected.length > 0)){
                                renderDetailItemsList(projectItems.selected, $(dom).find('#detailItemsArea'));
                            } else if(payload && Array.isArray(payload.selected)){
                                renderDetailItemsList(payload.selected, $(dom).find('#detailItemsArea'));
                            } else {
                                fetchDetailItemsForRecord(rec, dom, project);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                })
                .catch(function(err){
                    layer.closeAll('loading');
                    console.warn('获取购买详情失败', err);
                    layer.msg('获取详情失败');
                });
        }
        // 辅助：请求并渲染包含检查项（多路径尝试，兼容多种返回格式）
        function fetchDetailItemsForRecord(rec, dom, project){
            var target = $(dom).find('#detailItemsArea');
            target.html('<div style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:30px">加载中...</div>');
            var tryPaths = [
                '/reservation/' + encodeURIComponent(rec.id) + '/items',
                '/reservation/items?id=' + encodeURIComponent(rec.id)
            ];
            var pid = (rec.projectId || (project && (project.id || project.getId && project.getId())) || '');
            if(pid) {
                tryPaths.push('/project/' + encodeURIComponent(pid) + '/items');
                tryPaths.push('/project/items?projectId=' + encodeURIComponent(pid));
            }
            tryPaths.push('/project/' + encodeURIComponent(rec.projectId || '') + '/items');
            var tried = 0;
            function tryNext(){
                if(tried >= tryPaths.length){
                    target.html('<div style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:30px">暂无检查项数据</div>');
                    return;
                }
                var p = tryPaths[tried++];
                apiFetch(p, { method:'GET' })
                    .then(function(json){
                        var body = json && (json.data || json) || null;
                        if(Array.isArray(body) && body.length===1 && typeof body[0] === 'object') body = body[0];
                        var items = [];
                        if(!body){
                            if(json && json.selected && Array.isArray(json.selected)) items = json.selected;
                            else { tryNext(); return; }
                        } else if(Array.isArray(body)){
                            items = body;
                        } else if(Array.isArray(body.records)){
                            items = body.records;
                        } else if(Array.isArray(body.selected)){
                            items = body.selected;
                        } else if(Array.isArray(body.items)){
                            items = body.items;
                        } else {
                            tryNext(); return;
                        }
                        if(items && items.length > 0){
                            renderDetailItemsList(items, target);
                        } else {
                            tryNext();
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(function(err){
                        console.warn('try path failed', p, err);
                        tryNext();
                    });
            }
            tryNext();
        }
        function renderDetailItemsList(items, $target){
            if(!items || items.length === 0){
                $target.html('<div style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:30px">暂无检查项</div>');
                return;
            }
            var html = '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(260px,1fr));gap:12px;">';
            items.forEach(function(it){
                var name = it.name || it.title || it.itemName || it.item_name || '';
                var pic = it.pic || it.image || it.avatar || it.thumb || it.cover || '';
                var desc = it.info || it.description || it.desc || it.remark || '';
                var point = (typeof it.point !== 'undefined') ? it.point : ((typeof it.score !== 'undefined') ? it.score : '');
                var thumb = pic ? '<img src="' + escapeHtml(pic) + '" style="width:60px;height:60px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer" onclick="layer.photos({photos:{data:[{src:\'' + escapeHtml(pic) + '\'}]},anim:5})">' :
                    '<div style="width:60px;height:60px;background:#f5f5f5;border-radius:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#999">无图</div>';
                html += '<div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #eef2f8;padding:12px;border-radius:8px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;">' +
                    thumb +
                    '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">' +
                    '<div style="font-weight:600;color:#333;margin-bottom:6px;">' + escapeHtml(name || '未命名') + '</div>' +
                    '<div style="color:#666;font-size:13px;line-height:1.4;margin-bottom:8px;">' + escapeHtml(desc || '暂无描述') + '</div>' +
                    (point!=='' ? ('<div style="color:#1890ff;font-weight:600;">' + escapeHtml(point) + ' 分</div>') : '') +
                    '</div></div>';
            });
            html += '</div>';
            $target.html(html);
        }
        function deletePayinfo(id){
            layer.confirm('确认删除该记录吗？', function(idx){
                apiFetch('/reservation/delete?id=' + encodeURIComponent(id), { method: 'POST' })
                    .then(function(json){
                        layer.msg('删除成功');
                        loadList(1, 100, state.q, state.payMode, state.status, state.startDate, state.endDate);
                    })
                    .catch(function(err){
                        console.warn(err);
                        layer.msg('删除失败或接口未实现');
                    });
                layer.close(idx);
            });
        }
        function openReserveForRecord(record){
            $('#clientForm [name="client_name"]').val(record.clientName || record.client || '');
            $('#clientForm [name="phone"]').val(record.clientTel || record.client_tel || '');
            $('#clientForm [name="project_id"]').val(record.projectId || 0);
            $('#clientForm [name="payinfo_id"]').val(record.id || 0);
            var prevRemark = record.remark || '';
            var remark = '预约来源：记录ID ' + record.id;
            $('#clientForm [name="remark"]').val(prevRemark ? prevRemark + '；' + remark : remark);
            $('#reservePanel').show();
            var today = new Date();
            $('#todate').val(today.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0'+(today.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0'+today.getDate()).slice(-2));
            $('#totime').val('');
            $('#todate').focus();
        }
        function submitReservation(data){
            if(data.payinfo_id && Number(data.payinfo_id) > 0){
                var payload = {
                    payinfoId: Number(data.payinfo_id),
                    todate: data.todate,
                    totime: data.totime || '',
                    status: 0
                };
                return apiFetch('/reservation/createFromPay', { method: 'POST', body: payload })
                    .then(function(json){
                        layer.msg('预约提交成功');
                        $('#reservePanel').hide();
                        $('#clientForm')[0].reset();
                        return json;
                    })
                    .catch(function(err){ console.warn(err); layer.msg('预约提交失败'); });
            } else {
                var payload2 = {
                    clientId: data.clientId || null,
                    projectId: Number(data.project_id || 0),
                    todate: data.todate,
                    totime: data.totime || '',
                    status: 0
                };
                return apiFetch('/reservation/create', { method: 'POST', body: payload2 })
                    .then(function(json){
                        layer.msg('预约提交成功');
                        $('#reservePanel').hide();
                        $('#clientForm')[0].reset();
                        return json;
                    })
                    .catch(function(err){ console.warn(err); layer.msg('预约提交失败'); });
            }
        }
        // 初始化交互
        function init(){
            loadList(1, 100, '');
            $('#btnToggleFilters').on('click', function(){ $('#filters').toggleClass('open'); });
            $('#btnSwitchView').on('click', function(){
                if($('.table-wrap').is(':visible')){
                    $('.table-wrap').hide();
                    $('.list-cards').show();
                    $('#loadMore').show();
                } else {
                    $('.table-wrap').show();
                    $('.list-cards').hide();
                    $('#loadMore').hide();
                }
            });
            $('#btnSearch').on('click', function(){
                state.q = $('#q').val();
                state.payMode = $('#filterPay').val();
                state.status = $('#filterStatus').val();
                var dr = $('#dateRange').val().split(' 到 ');
                state.startDate = dr[0] || '';
                state.endDate = dr[1] || '';
                loadList(1, 100, state.q, state.payMode, state.status, state.startDate, state.endDate);
            });
            $('#btnReset').on('click', function(){
                $('#q').val(''); $('#dateRange').val(''); $('#filterPay').val(''); $('#filterStatus').val('');
                state = { q:'', startDate:'', endDate:'', payMode:'', status:'', page:1, limit:8, view:'card', filtered:[], total:0 };
                loadList(1, 100, '');
            });
            $('#loadMore').on('click', function(){ state.page++; renderCards(false); });
            $('#clientForm').on('submit', function(e){
                e.preventDefault();
                var fd = new FormData(e.target);
                var vals = Object.fromEntries(fd.entries());
                if(!vals.todate || !vals.todate.trim()){ layer.msg('请选择预约日期'); return; }
                submitReservation(vals);
            });
            $('#closeReserve').on('click', function(){ $('#reservePanel').hide(); });
            var $backBtn = $('<button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="btnBack" style="margin-right:8px;">返回</button>');
            $('.head').prepend($backBtn);
            $('#btnBack').on('click', function(){
                try{ if(window.history && window.history.length > 1) window.history.back(); else window.location.href = '/'; } catch(e){ window.location.href = '/'; }
            });
            (function(){
                var t;
                $('#btnBack').on('mousedown touchstart', function(){ t = setTimeout(function(){ localStorage.removeItem('userInfo'); layer.msg('本地用户信息已清除'); }, 800); });
                $('#btnBack').on('mouseup mouseleave touchend', function(){ clearTimeout(t); });
            })();
        }
        init();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <link href="../layui/css/layui.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        body{ padding:18px; }
        .leftCard{ width:260px; display:inline-block; vertical-align:top; }
        .rightForm{ display:inline-block; width:calc(100% - 300px); margin-left:20px; vertical-align:top; }
        .avatar{ width:120px; height:120px; border-radius:50%; object-fit:cover; border:1px solid #eee; padding:4px; background:#fff; }
        /* 响应式：窄屏下纵向排列 */
        @media (max-width: 900px){
            .leftCard, .rightForm { display:block; width:auto; margin-left:0; }
            .rightForm { margin-top:16px; }
            .layui-form-label{ width:100%!important; }
            .layui-input-block{ margin-left:0!important; }
        }
        .meta-row{ margin-top:8px; color:#666; }
        .avatar-wrap{ display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; gap:8px; }

    <div class="leftCard layui-card">
        <div class="layui-card-body" style="text-align:center;">
            <div class="avatar-wrap">
                <img id="avatarImg" class="avatar" src="../static/default-avatar.png" alt="头像">
                <div>
                    <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-sm" id="btnUploadAvatar"><i class="layui-icon">&#xe67c;</i> 上传头像</button>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <h3 id="username" style="margin:0;"></h3>

                        <input type="text" name="email" class="layui-input" placeholder="example@domain.com">

                <div class="layui-form-item">

<script src="../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['layer','form','upload','jquery'], function(){
        var layer = layui.layer, form = layui.form, upload = layui.upload, $ = layui.$;
        // API 配置（根据实际后端路径调整）
        var API = {
            get: '/api/user/me',
            update: '/api/user/',        // + id 或 /api/user/{id}
            upload: '/api/upload',
            depts: '/api/departments',
            roles: '/api/roles',
            pwd: '/api/user/change-password',
            updateAvatar: '/api/user/avatar', // example: POST { avatar: url }
            toggleStatus: '/api/user/' // + {id}/status
        };
        // 简单缓存下拉数据，避免重复请求
        var cache = { depts: null, roles: null };
        // 扩展表单验证
        form.verify({
            // newpass: 必须至少 8 位
            newpass: function(value){
                if(value && value.length < 8){
                    return '新密码至少 8 位';
                }
            }
        });
        // 通用错误处理
        function ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status){
            layer.msg('网络错误：' + (status || '请求失败'));
        }
        // 加载下拉（若缓存中存在则直接使用）
        function loadSelects(callback){
            var doneCount = 0;
            function checkDone(){ doneCount++; if(doneCount === 2 && typeof callback === 'function') callback(); }
            // 部门
            if(cache.depts){
                renderDepts(cache.depts);
                checkDone();
            } else {
                $.ajax({ url: API.depts, method: 'GET', dataType: 'json',
                    success: function(res){
                        if(res && res.code === 0){
                            // 兼容多种 data 结构
                            var list = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : (Array.isArray(res.records) ? res.records : []);
                            cache.depts = list;
                            renderDepts(list);
                        } else {
                            $('#selectDept').html('<option value="">加载失败</option>');
                            layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '部门加载失败');
                        }
                        checkDone();
                    }, error: function(xhr, status){ $('#selectDept').html('<option value="">加载错误</option>'); ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status); checkDone(); }
                });
            }
            // 角色
            if(cache.roles){
                renderRoles(cache.roles);
                checkDone();
            } else {
                $.ajax({ url: API.roles, method: 'GET', dataType: 'json',
                    success: function(res){
                        if(res && res.code === 0){
                            var list = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : (Array.isArray(res.records) ? res.records : []);
                            cache.roles = list;
                            renderRoles(list);
                        } else {
                            $('#selectRole').html('<option value="">加载失败</option>');
                            layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '角色加载失败');
                        }
                        checkDone();
                    }, error: function(xhr, status){ $('#selectRole').html('<option value="">加载错误</option>'); ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status); checkDone(); }
                });
            }
        }
        function renderDepts(list){
            var html = '<option value="">请选择部门</option>';
            list.forEach(function(d){
                html += '<option value="'+ (d.id || d.departmentId || '') +'">'+ (d.name || d.title || '') +'</option>';
            });
            $('#selectDept').html(html);
            form.render('select');
        }
        function renderRoles(list){
            var html = '<option value="">请选择角色</option>';
            list.forEach(function(r){
                html += '<option value="'+ (r.id || r.roleId || '') +'">'+ (r.name || r.title || '') +'</option>';
            });
            $('#selectRole').html(html);
            form.render('select');
        }
        // 加载用户信息（与下拉并行）
        function loadUser(){
            var loading = layer.load(2);
            $.ajax({ url: API.get, method: 'GET', dataType: 'json',
                success: function(res){
                    layer.close(loading);
                    if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data){
                        var d = res.data;
                        form.val('profileForm', {
                            id: d.id,
                            name: d.name || '',
                            departmentId: d.departmentId || '',
                            power: d.power || '',
                            email: d.email || '',
                            phone: d.phone || ''
                        });
                        $('#username').text(d.name || '');
                        $('#role').text(d.roleName || '');
                        $('#avatarImg').attr('src', d.avatar || '../static/default-avatar.png');
                    } else {
                        layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '获取用户信息失败');
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status){ layer.close(loading); ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status); }
            });
        }
        // 初始化：并行加载下拉与用户信息
        loadSelects(loadUser);
        // 上传头像
        upload.render({
            elem: '#btnUploadAvatar',
            url: API.upload,
            accept: 'images',
            size: 2048, // KB
            before: function(obj){
                layer.load(2);
            },
            done: function(res){
                layer.closeAll('loading');
                if(res && res.code === 0){
                    // 上传成功：取得图片地址（兼容不同后端返回）
                    var url = res.url || (res.data && (res.data.url || res.data.path)) || '';
                    if(!url){
                        layer.msg('上传成功，但未返回文件地址');
                        return;
                    }
                    // 立即回显
                    $('#avatarImg').attr('src', url);
                    // 保存到后端（可用单独接口）
                    $.ajax({
                        url: API.updateAvatar,
                        method: 'POST',
                        contentType: 'application/json',
                        data: JSON.stringify({ avatar: url }),
                        success: function(r){
                            if(r && r.code === 0){
                                layer.msg('头像已更新');
                            } else {
                                layer.msg(r && r.msg ? r.msg : '头像保存失败');
                            }
                        },
                        error: function(xhr, status){
                            ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status);
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '上传失败');
                }
            },
            error: function(){
                layer.closeAll('loading');
                layer.msg('上传出错，请重试');
            }
        });
        // 保存个人信息
        form.on('submit(submitProfile)', function(fd){
            var data = fd.field;
            if(!data.name || data.name.trim() === ''){
                layer.msg('用户名不能为空');
                return false;
            }
            var id = data.id;
            if(!id){
                layer.msg('用户ID缺失，无法保存');
                return false;
            }
            var loading = layer.load(2);
            $.ajax({
                url: API.update + id,
                method: 'PUT',
                contentType: 'application/json',
                data: JSON.stringify({
                    name: data.name,
                    departmentId: data.departmentId || null,
                    power: data.power || null,
                    email: data.email || '',
                    phone: data.phone || ''
                }),
                success: function(res){
                    layer.close(loading);
                    if(res && res.code === 0){
                        layer.msg('保存成功');
                        // 重新加载用户信息（确保头像/role 等同步）
                        loadUser();
                    } else {
                        layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '保存失败');
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status){
                    layer.close(loading);
                    ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status);
                }
            });
            return false;
        });
        // 修改密码
        form.on('submit(submitPwd)', function(fd){
            var d = fd.field;
            if(!d.oldpass || !d.newpass){
                layer.msg('请完整填写密码字段');
                return false;
            }
            if(d.newpass.length < 8){
                layer.msg('新密码至少 8 位');
                return false;
            }
            var loading = layer.load(2);
            $.ajax({
                url: API.pwd,
                method: 'POST',
                contentType: 'application/json',
                data: JSON.stringify({ old_password: d.oldpass, new_password: d.newpass }),
                success: function(res){
                    layer.close(loading);
                    if(res && res.code === 0){
                        layer.msg('修改成功，请重新登录');
                        // TODO: 这里可以触发退出登录跳转
                    } else {
                        layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '修改失败');
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status){
                    layer.close(loading);
                    ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status);
                }
            });
            return false;
        });
        // 切换启用/禁用（示例：需要后端支持 /api/user/{id}/status 或类似接口）
        $('#btnToggleStatus').on('click', function(){
            var loading = layer.load(2);
            // 先获取当前用户信息（或者从页面隐藏域读取）
            $.ajax({
                url: API.get,
                method: 'GET',
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function(res){
                    layer.close(loading);
                    if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data){
                        var d = res.data;
                        var to = (d.status === 1 || d.status === '1') ? 0 : 1;
                        layer.confirm((to === 1 ? '确认启用该账户？' : '确认禁用该账户？'), function(idx){
                            layer.close(idx);
                            var l2 = layer.load(2);
                            // 假设后端支持 POST /api/user/{id}/status { status: 0/1 }
                            $.ajax({
                                url: API.toggleStatus + d.id + '/status',
                                method: 'POST',
                                contentType: 'application/json',
                                data: JSON.stringify({ status: to }),
                                success: function(r){
                                    layer.close(l2);
                                    if(r && r.code === 0){
                                        layer.msg('操作成功');
                                        loadUser();
                                    } else {
                                        layer.msg(r && r.msg ? r.msg : '操作失败');
                                    }
                                },
                                error: function(xhr, status){
                                    layer.close(l2);
                                    ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status);
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    } else {
                        layer.msg(res && res.msg ? res.msg : '获取用户失败');
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status){ layer.close(loading); ajaxErrorHandler(xhr, status); }
            });
        });
        // 可选：当某些后端返回字段不一致时，请根据后端实际字段调整 mapping 逻辑
        // 可选：增加“退出登录/刷新用户信息”等按钮并绑定实际逻辑
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>套餐商城（悬浮购物球 · 我的卡悬浮窗）</title>
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, user-scalable=no" />
    <link href="../../layui/css/layui.css" rel="stylesheet" />
    <style>
        :root {
            --primary: #ff6b00;
            --primary-2: #ff8a33;
            --muted: #8a8f98;

<script src="../../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['layer','form','laypage','jquery'], function(){
        var layer = layui.layer, form = layui.form, laypage = layui.laypage, $ = layui.$;
        var api = {
            list: '/project/list',
            purchase: '/pay/purchase',
            cardInfo: '/card/info'
        };
        var projects = [], cart = [];
        var userCards = [];
        var currentCard = null;
        var clientId = localStorage.getItem('id') || localStorage.getItem('userId') || null;
        function normalizeListResponse(res){
            if(!res) return {items:[], total:0};
            if(Array.isArray(res)) return {items:res, total:res.length};
            if(res.code!==undefined){
                if(res.code===0||res.code===200){
                    if(Array.isArray(res.data)) return {items:res.data, total: res.count||res.total||res.data.length};
                    if(res.data && res.data.list) return {items:res.data.list, total: res.data.count||res.data.total||res.data.list.length};
                }
            }
            if(res.data && Array.isArray(res.data.list)) return {items: res.data.list, total: res.data.total||res.data.count||res.data.list.length};
            if(Array.isArray(res.items)) return {items:res.items, total: res.total||res.count||res.items.length};
            return {items:[], total:0};
        }
        function loadProjectList(params){
            params = params || {};
            $('#cardsContainer').html('<div class="loading"><i class="layui-icon layui-anim layui-anim-rotate layui-anim-loop" style="font-size:24px;color:var(--primary)">&#xe63e;</i><div style="margin-top:8px">正在加载套餐...</div></div>');
            $.ajax({
                url: api.list, method: 'GET', data: params, dataType:'json', xhrFields:{withCredentials:true},
                success: function(res){
                    var norm = normalizeListResponse(res);
                    projects = norm.items||[];
                    renderCards();
                    renderPagination(norm.total || projects.length, params.page||1, params.size||10);
                },
                error: function(){ $('#cardsContainer').html('<div class="empty">网络错误，请稍后重试</div>'); layer.msg('获取套餐数据失败'); }
            });
        }
        function renderCards(){
            var $wrap = $('#cardsContainer').empty();
            if(!projects || projects.length===0){ $wrap.append('<div class="empty">暂无套餐数据</div>'); return; }
            projects.forEach(function(p){
                var price = parseFloat(p.price||p.amount||0);
                var $card = $('<div class="card" tabindex="0"></div>');
                var img = p.pic||p.img||p.cover||p.image||'';
                var $cover = $('<div class="cover"></div>');
                if(img) $cover.append('<img loading="lazy" src="'+img+'" alt="'+(p.name||'套餐')+'">'); else $cover.append('<i class="layui-icon icon" style="font-size:28px;color:var(--primary)">&#xe62d;</i>');
                $card.append($cover);
                $card.append('<h3>'+(p.name||'套餐')+'</h3>');
                $card.append('<div class="meta">'+(p.info||p.desc||p.description||'')+'</div>');
                var $priceRow = $('<div class="priceRow"></div>');
                var $actions = $('<div class="actions"></div>');
                $actions.append('<button class="buy-btn btnBuyNow" data-id="'+p.id+'"><i class="layui-icon" style="font-size:16px">&#xe654;</i> 购买</button>');
                $actions.append('<button class="add-btn btnAddCart" data-id="'+p.id+'">加入购物车</button>');
                $priceRow.append($actions);
                $priceRow.append('<div class="price">¥'+price.toFixed(2)+'</div>');
                $card.append($priceRow);
                $wrap.append($card);
            });
        }
        function renderPagination(total,page,size){
            $('#pagerContainer').empty();
            laypage.render({ elem:'pagerContainer', count: total||0, limit:size||10, curr: page||1, layout:['count','prev','page','next','limit','skip'], jump: function(obj, first){
                    if(!first) loadProjectList({page:obj.curr, size: obj.limit, name: $('#searchName').val(), status: $('#statusFilter').val()});
                }});
        }
        function refreshFab(){
            var $fabCount = $('#fabCount'), $fabAmount = $('#fabAmount'), $fabList = $('#fabList'), $fabTotal = $('#fabTotal'), $fabItemCount = $('#fabItemCount');
            $fabList.empty();
            if(cart.length===0){ $fabList.html('<div class="muted">购物车为空</div>'); $fabCount.text(0); $fabAmount.text('¥0.00'); $fabTotal && $fabTotal.text && $fabTotal.text('¥0.00'); $fabItemCount.text(''); return; }
            cart.forEach(function(item,idx){
                var $it = $('<div class="fab-item"></div>');
                var img = (projects.find(p=>p.id===item.projectId)||{}).pic || '';
                var $thumb = $('<div class="thumb"></div>');
                if(img) $thumb.append('<img src="'+img+'" alt="">'); else $thumb.text('套餐');
                $it.append($thumb);
                var subtotal = (item.price*item.qty)||0;
                var $info = $('<div class="info" style="flex:1;margin-left:8px;"></div>');
                $info.append('<div class="name" style="font-weight:700">'+(item.name||'套餐')+'</div>');
                $info.append('<div class="price" style="font-size:13px;color:'+getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--muted')+'">¥'+item.price.toFixed(2)+' × <input type="number" min="1" value="'+item.qty+'" data-idx="'+idx+'" class="fab-qty" style="width:56px;border:none;background:transparent;text-align:center;font-weight:700"> = <span style="color:var(--primary);font-weight:800">¥'+subtotal.toFixed(2)+'</span></div>');
                $it.append($info);
                $it.append('<div><button class="layui-btn layui-btn-danger layui-btn-xs fab-remove" data-idx="'+idx+'">移除</button></div>');
                $fabList.append($it);
            });
            var totalQty = cart.reduce(function(s,it){return s+it.qty},0);
            var total = cart.reduce(function(s,it){return s+it.price*it.qty},0);
            $fabCount.text(totalQty); $fabAmount.text('¥'+total.toFixed(2)); $fabTotal && $fabTotal.text && $fabTotal.text('¥'+total.toFixed(2)); $fabItemCount.text('（'+totalQty+' 件）');
        }
        function addToCart(id, qty){
            qty = qty||1; var p = projects.find(x=>x.id==id); if(!p) return layer.msg('套餐不存在');
            var ex = cart.find(c=>c.projectId==id); if(ex) ex.qty += qty; else cart.push({projectId:id, name:p.name, price:parseFloat(p.price||p.amount||0), qty:qty});
            refreshFab(); layer.msg('已加入购物车',{icon:1,time:1000});
        }
        function normalizeCardInfoResponse(res){
            if(!res) return {};
            if(res.code!==undefined){
                if(res.code===0) return res.data||{};
                return res;
            }
            if(res.status!==undefined){
                if(res.status===0||res.status==='ok'||res.status==='success') return res.data||{};
            }
            return res.data||res;
        }
        function fetchCards(clientIdParam, cardCodeParam, cb){
            var data = {};
            if(clientIdParam) data.clientId = clientIdParam;
            // 手动 cardCode 查询逻辑已移除；后端仍可接受 clientId 以返回该用户卡
            $('#mycardList').html('<div class="muted">加载中...</div>');
            $.ajax({
                url: api.cardInfo, method:'GET', data: data, dataType:'json', xhrFields:{withCredentials:true},
                success: function(res){
                    var d = normalizeCardInfoResponse(res);
                    var cards = [];
                    if(Array.isArray(d.cards)) cards = d.cards;
                    else if(Array.isArray(d.list)) cards = d.list;
                    else if(d.card){ cards = Array.isArray(d.card) ? d.card : [d.card]; }
                    else if(Array.isArray(d)) cards = d;
                    userCards = cards||[];
                    // 选中第一张作为默认卡（如果没有已选卡）
                    if(userCards.length>0 && !currentCard){
                        var c = userCards[0];
                        var id = c.id||c.cardId||c.code||Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);
                        var code = c.code||c.card_no||c.cardNo||id;
                        var balance = c.count!==undefined?c.count:(c.balance!==undefined?c.balance:(c.amount||0));
                        var disabled = (c.isdel===1 || (c.status!==undefined && Number(c.status)!==1));
                        if(!disabled){
                            currentCard = { id: id, code: code, count: parseFloat(balance||0) };
                        } else {
                            // 如果第一张不可用则尝试找可用卡
                            for(var i=0;i<userCards.length;i++){
                                var cc = userCards[i];
                                var dstatus = (cc.isdel===1 || (cc.status!==undefined && Number(cc.status)!==1));
                                if(!dstatus){
                                    var iid = cc.id||cc.cardId||cc.code||Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);
                                    var ccode = cc.code||cc.card_no||cc.cardNo||iid;
                                    var cbalance = cc.count!==undefined?cc.count:(cc.balance!==undefined?cc.balance:(cc.amount||0));
                                    currentCard = { id: iid, code: ccode, count: parseFloat(cbalance||0) };
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    renderMycardList();
                    if(cb) cb(userCards);
                },
                error: function(){ $('#mycardList').html('<div class="muted">加载失败</div>'); layer.msg('获取卡片失败'); if(cb) cb([]); }
            });
        }
        function renderMycardList(){
            var $wrap = $('#mycardList').empty();
            if(!userCards || userCards.length===0){
                $wrap.html('<div class="muted">您暂无会员卡，请充值或办卡后再使用卡支付</div>');
                $('#mycardCount').text('');
                currentCard = null;
                updateMycardFabLabel();
                return;
            }
            userCards.forEach(function(c){
                var id = c.id||c.cardId||c.code||Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);
                var code = c.code||c.card_no||c.cardNo||id;
                var balance = c.count!==undefined?c.count:(c.balance!==undefined?c.balance:(c.amount||0));
                var disabled = (c.isdel===1 || (c.status!==undefined && Number(c.status)!==1)) ? 'disabled' : '';
                var sel = (currentCard && (String(currentCard.id)===String(c.id) || String(currentCard.code)===String(code))) ? 'selected' : '';
                var $item = $('<div class="card-list-item '+disabled+' '+sel+'" data-id="'+id+'" data-code="'+code+'" data-balance="'+balance+'"></div>');
                $item.append('<div><div style="font-weight:700">'+(c.title||c.name||('卡#'+code))+'</div><div class="muted" style="font-size:12px">卡号：'+code+'</div></div>');
                $item.append('<div style="text-align:right"><div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--primary)">¥'+parseFloat(balance||0).toFixed(2)+'</div><div class="muted" style="font-size:12px">余额</div></div>');
                $item.on('click keypress', function(e){
                    if(e.type==='keypress' && e.which!==13) return;
                    if($(this).hasClass('disabled')){ layer.msg('该卡不可用'); return; }
                    $('.card-list-item').removeClass('selected');
                    $(this).addClass('selected');
                    var cid = $(this).data('id'), ccode = $(this).data('code'), cbalance = parseFloat($(this).data('balance')||0);
                    currentCard = { id: cid, code: ccode, count: cbalance };
                    updateMycardFabLabel();
                    layer.msg('已选中卡 '+(ccode||cid), {icon:1, time:800});
                });
                $wrap.append($item);
            });
            $('#mycardCount').text('（'+userCards.length+'）');
            updateMycardFabLabel();
        }
        function updateMycardFabLabel(){
            if(currentCard){
                var short = (currentCard.code || ('卡#'+currentCard.id));
                $('#mycardFabLabel').text(short + ' ¥' + (parseFloat(currentCard.count||0).toFixed(0)));
            } else {
                $('#mycardFabLabel').text('我的卡');
            }
        }
        $(function(){
            loadProjectList({page:1,size:10});
            refreshFab();
            fetchCards(clientId, null, function(){});
            // 初始放在右中偏上
            var startLeft = Math.max(window.innerWidth - 90, window.innerWidth - 100);
            $('#mycardFab').css({ left: (startLeft) + 'px', top: (window.innerHeight * 0.25) + 'px' });
        });
        // 拖拽实现（保留之前逻辑但修复 isDragging 范围）
        (function() {
            var mycardFab = document.getElementById('mycardFab');
            var isDragging = false;
            var startX = 0, startY = 0, initialLeft = 0, initialTop = 0;
            var dragThreshold = 5;
            var moved = false;
            function getStyleNumber(el, prop, fallback){
                var v = window.getComputedStyle(el)[prop];
                return parseFloat(v) || fallback || 0;
            }
            mycardFab.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
                // only left button
                if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;
                isDragging = true;
                moved = false;
                startX = e.clientX;
                startY = e.clientY;
                initialLeft = getStyleNumber(mycardFab, 'left', mycardFab.offsetLeft);
                initialTop = getStyleNumber(mycardFab, 'top', mycardFab.offsetTop);
                mycardFab.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
                mycardFab.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
                e.preventDefault();
            }, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e) {
                if (!isDragging) return;
                var dx = e.clientX - startX;
                var dy = e.clientY - startY;
                if (!moved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) >= dragThreshold) moved = true;
                mycardFab.style.left = (initialLeft + dx) + 'px';
                mycardFab.style.top = (initialTop + dy) + 'px';
            }, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e) {
                if (!isDragging) return;
                isDragging = false;
                mycardFab.style.cursor = 'grab';
                // 限制在可视窗口内
                var rect = mycardFab.getBoundingClientRect();
                var w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
                var left = rect.left, top = rect.top;
                if (left < 8) mycardFab.style.left = '8px';
                if (top < 8) mycardFab.style.top = '8px';
                if (rect.right > w - 8) mycardFab.style.left = (w - rect.width - 8) + 'px';
                if (rect.bottom > h - 8) mycardFab.style.top = (h - rect.height - 8) + 'px';
                // small snap to edges (x axis)
                var finalRect = mycardFab.getBoundingClientRect();
                var midX = finalRect.left + finalRect.width / 2;
                if (midX < w / 2) {
                    mycardFab.style.left = '8px';
                } else {
                    mycardFab.style.left = (w - finalRect.width - 8) + 'px';
                }
                // store position in localStorage
                try {
                    localStorage.setItem('mycardFabLeft', mycardFab.style.left);
                    localStorage.setItem('mycardFabTop', mycardFab.style.top);
                } catch (e) {}
            });
            // restore saved position
            try {
                var savedLeft = localStorage.getItem('mycardFabLeft');
                var savedTop = localStorage.getItem('mycardFabTop');
                if (savedLeft && savedTop) {
                    $('#mycardFab').css({ left: savedLeft, top: savedTop });
                }
            } catch (e) {}
            // expose moved flag to other handlers via property
            Object.defineProperty(window, '__mycard_moved_flag__', {
                get: function(){ return moved; }
            });
        })();
        // 我的卡 悬浮开关（区分拖动与点击）
        $('#mycardFab').on('click', function(e){
            // If pointer dragged, ignore click activation
            if (window.__mycard_moved_flag__) {
                return;
            }
            e.stopPropagation();
            var $panel = $('#mycardPanel');
            if($panel.is(':visible')){ $panel.hide(); $(this).attr('aria-expanded','false'); }
            else { $panel.show(); $(this).attr('aria-expanded','true'); }
        });
        // 点击页面空白区域关闭面板
        $(document).on('click', function(e){
            if(!$(e.target).closest('#mycardPanel').length && !$(e.target).closest('#mycardFab').length){
                $('#mycardPanel').hide(); $('#mycardFab').attr('aria-expanded','false');
            }
            if(!$(e.target).closest('#fabPanel').length && !$(e.target).closest('#cartFab').length){
                $('#fabPanel').hide(); $('#cartFab').attr('aria-expanded','false');
            }
        });
        // mycardPanel 操作
        $('#btnRefreshCards').on('click', function(){ fetchCards(clientId, null, function(){ layer.msg('已刷新'); }); });
        $('#btnCloseCardPanel').on('click', function(){ $('#mycardPanel').hide(); $('#mycardFab').attr('aria-expanded','false'); });
        // 购物球与面板
        $('#cartFab').on('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); var $panel = $('#fabPanel'); if($panel.is(':visible')){ $panel.hide(); $(this).attr('aria-expanded','false'); } else { $panel.show(); $(this).attr('aria-expanded','true'); } });
        $('#fabPanel').on('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
        // 购物车操作
        $(document).on('click','.btnAddCart',function(){ addToCart($(this).data('id'),1); });
        $(document).on('click','.btnBuyNow',function(){
            var id=$(this).data('id'); var p=projects.find(x=>x.id==id);
            if(!p) return layer.msg('套餐不存在');
            cart=[{projectId:p.id,name:p.name,price:parseFloat(p.price||p.amount||0),qty:1}]; refreshFab(); layer.msg('已选中 '+p.name);
            openFabPanel();
            if(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width:720px)').matches) $('html,body').animate({scrollTop:0},200);
        });
        $('#fabClear').on('click', function(){ layer.confirm('确定清空购物车？',function(i){ cart=[]; refreshFab(); layer.close(i); }); });
        $('#btnApplyFilters').on('click', function(){ loadProjectList({page:1,size:10,name:$('#searchName').val(),status:$('#statusFilter').val()}); });
        $('#searchName').on('keypress', function(e){ if(e.which===13) $('#btnApplyFilters').click(); });
        $(document).on('change','.fab-qty', function(){ var idx=+$(this).data('idx'); var v=parseInt($(this).val())||1; if(v<1) v=1; cart[idx].qty=v; refreshFab(); });
        $(document).on('click','.fab-remove', function(){ var idx=+$(this).data('idx'); cart.splice(idx,1); refreshFab(); });
        function openFabPanel(){ $('#fabPanel').show(); $('#cartFab').attr('aria-expanded','true'); }
        function closeFabPanel(){ $('#fabPanel').hide(); $('#cartFab').attr('aria-expanded','false'); }
        // 结算
        $('#fabCheckout').on('click', function(){
            if(cart.length===0) return layer.msg('购物车为空');
            var client = clientId;
            if(!client) return layer.msg('请先登录');
            var total = cart.reduce(function(s,it){return s+it.price*it.qty},0).toFixed(2);
            var cardHtml = '';
            if(!userCards || userCards.length===0){
                // 不提供手动输入，直接提示办卡
                cardHtml = '<div class="muted">您暂无可用会员卡，请充值或办卡后使用卡支付。</div>';
            } else {
                userCards.forEach(function(c){
                    var balance = c.count!==undefined?parseFloat(c.count).toFixed(2):(c.balance!==undefined?parseFloat(c.balance).toFixed(2):(c.amount?parseFloat(c.amount).toFixed(2):'0.00'));
                    var selected = currentCard && (String(currentCard.id)===String(c.id) || String(currentCard.code)===String(c.code)) ? 'selected' : '';
                    var disabled = (c.isdel===1 || (c.status!==undefined && Number(c.status)!==1))? 'disabled':'';
                    cardHtml += '<div class="card-option '+selected+' '+disabled+'" data-id="'+(c.id||'')+'" data-code="'+(c.code||'')+'" data-balance="'+balance+'"><div class="card-code">'+(c.code||('卡#'+(c.id||'')))+'</div><div class="card-balance-info">余额: ¥'+balance+'</div></div>';
                });
            }
            var formHtml = '<div style="padding:12px 18px;"><form class="layui-form" id="fabBuyForm"><input type="hidden" name="buyType" id="fabBuyType" value="self"><div class="layui-form-item"><label class="layui-form-label" style="width:90px">支付方式</label><div class="layui-input-inline" style="width:180px"><select name="payMode" id="fabPayMode"><option value="online">在线支付</option><option value="card">使用会员卡</option></select></div></div><div id="cardArea" style="display:none;margin-bottom:6px"><label style="display:block;margin-bottom:6px;color:#666">选择会员卡（默认使用您已选卡）</label><div class="card-selector">'+cardHtml+'</div><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-top:6px"><input type="hidden" id="fabCardCode" value=""/><input type="hidden" id="fabCardId" value=""/></div></div><div class="layui-form-item"><label class="layui-form-label" style="width:90px">备注</label><div class="layui-input-inline" style="width:180px"><input type="text" id="fabComments" class="layui-input"></div></div><div style="margin-top:8px;color:#333">合计：<strong style="color:#ff6b00">¥'+total+'</strong></div></form></div>';
            var layerIdx = layer.open({
                type:1,
                title:'确认购买',
                content: formHtml,
                area: (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width:480px)').matches) ? ['92%','auto'] : ['640px','auto'],
                btn: ['确认支付','取消'],
                success: function(layero, idx){
                    form.render(null,null);
                    // 如果已有 currentCard，填充隐藏字段
                    if(currentCard){
                        $(layero).find('#fabCardCode').val(currentCard.code||'');
                        $(layero).find('#fabCardId').val(currentCard.id||'');
                        $(layero).find('.card-option').removeClass('selected');
                        $(layero).find('.card-option[data-id="'+currentCard.id+'"]').addClass('selected');
                    }
                    $(layero).find('#fabPayMode').on('change', function(){
                        if(this.value==='card') $(layero).find('#cardArea').show(); else $(layero).find('#cardArea').hide();
                    });
                    $(layero).find('#fabPayMode').trigger('change');
                    // 允许在弹窗中选择卡（会更新 currentCard）
                    $(layero).find('.card-option:not(.disabled)').on('click', function(){
                        $(layero).find('.card-option').removeClass('selected');
                        $(this).addClass('selected');
                        var code = $(this).attr('data-code')||'', cid = $(this).attr('data-id')||'', balance = parseFloat($(this).attr('data-balance')||0);
                        $(layero).find('#fabCardCode').val(code);
                        $(layero).find('#fabCardId').val(cid);
                        currentCard = { id: cid, code: code, count: balance };
                        $('.card-list-item').removeClass('selected');
                        $('.card-list-item[data-id="'+cid+'"]').addClass('selected');
                        updateMycardFabLabel();
                    });
                },
                yes: function(idx, layero){
                    var buyType = $(layero).find('#fabBuyType').val();
                    var payMode = $(layero).find('#fabPayMode').val();
                    var cardCode = $(layero).find('#fabCardCode').val().trim();
                    var cardId = $(layero).find('#fabCardId').val();
                    var comments = $(layero).find('#fabComments').val().trim();
                    if(payMode==='card'){
                        // 现在必须使用 currentCard（或弹窗内选中的卡）
                        if(!currentCard || !currentCard.id){
                            layer.msg('您暂无可用会员卡，请充值或办卡'); return;
                        }
                        var available = parseFloat(currentCard.count || 0);
                        if(available < parseFloat(total)){ layer.msg('卡余额不足，当前余额: ¥'+available.toFixed(2)); return; }
                    }
                    var payload = {
                        cart: cart.map(function(it){ return { projectId: it.projectId, name: it.name, price: it.price, qty: it.qty }; }),
                        buyType: buyType,
                        clientId: clientId,
                        payMode: payMode,
                        cardCode: (payMode==='card' ? (currentCard ? currentCard.code : '') : ''),
                        cardId: (payMode==='card' ? (currentCard ? currentCard.id : null) : null),
                        comments: comments
                    };
                    layer.confirm('确认购买，合计：¥'+total+' 元？', {btn:['确认支付','取消']}, function(cidx){
                        layer.close(cidx);
                        var loader = layer.load(1);
                        $.ajax({ url: api.purchase, method:'POST', contentType:'application/json', data: JSON.stringify(payload), dataType:'json', xhrFields:{withCredentials:true},
                            success: function(res){
                                layer.close(loader);
                                var ok=false;
                                if(res){
                                    if(res.code!==undefined) ok=(res.code===0||res.code===200);
                                    else if(res.success!==undefined) ok=(res.success===true);
                                    else if(res.status!==undefined) ok=(res.status===0||res.status==='ok'||res.status==='success');
                                    else ok=true;
                                }
                                if(ok){
                                    layer.msg('购买成功');
                                    cart=[]; refreshFab(); layer.close(layerIdx); $('#fabPanel').hide();
                                    fetchCards(clientId, null, function(){});
                                } else {
                                    layer.msg(res && (res.msg||res.message) || '购买失败');
                                }
                            },
                            error: function(){ layer.close(loader); layer.msg('购买失败'); }
                        });
                    });
                }
            });
        });
    });
</script>
</body>

<script src="../../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['layer','form','jquery'], function(){
        var layer = layui.layer;
        var form = layui.form;
        var $ = layui.jquery;
        var API_BASE = '/card/';
        var RECHARGE_PATH = '/card/recharge';
        var levels = [
            { id: 1, name: '普通会员', threshold: 1000, discount: 0.99 },
            { id: 2, name: '银卡会员', threshold: 2000, discount: 0.95 },
            { id: 3, name: '金卡会员', threshold: 5000, discount: 0.88 },
            { id: 4, name: '钻石会员', threshold: 10000, discount: 0.85 },
            { id: 5, name: '尊享会员', threshold: 20000, discount: 0.60 }
        ];
        function renderLevels(){
            var $c = $('#levelsList').empty();
            levels.forEach(function(l){
                var $item = $('<div>').css({display:'flex',alignItems:'center',padding:'10px',borderRadius:'10px',background:'#fafafa',marginBottom:'8px'});
                $item.append('<div style="width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:8px;background:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-right:12px;"><i class="fas fa-certificate" style="color:#ffb84d"></i></div>');
                $item.append('<div style="flex:1"><div style="font-weight:600;">'+ l.name +'</div><div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">累计充值满 '+ l.threshold +' 元</div></div>');
                $item.append('<div style="font-weight:600;color:var(--primary)">'+ (l.discount*10).toFixed(1) +'折</div>');
                $c.append($item);
            });
        }
        function fetchMemberInfo() {
            var clientId = localStorage.getItem('id') || localStorage.getItem('userId') || null;
            if (!clientId) {
                layer.msg('未找到用户ID，请先登录', {icon: 2});
                return;
            }
            var idx = layer.load(1, {shade: 0.3});
            $.ajax({
                url: API_BASE + 'info',
                method: 'GET',
                data: { clientId: clientId },
                success: function(resp) {
                    layer.close(idx);
                    if (resp.code === 0) {
                        applyServerDataToUI(resp.data);
                    } else {
                        layer.msg(resp.msg || '获取会员信息失败', {icon: 2});
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status, err) {
                    layer.close(idx);
                    console.error('获取会员信息失败', status, err);
                    layer.msg('获取会员信息失败，请检查网络连接', {icon: 2});
                }
            });
        }
        function applyServerDataToUI(data) {
            if (!data) return;
            var client = data.client || data;
            var card = data.card || data;
            // 累计金额 - 优先使用client.point，如果没有则使用card.tocount
            var accumulated = client.point || (card ? card.tocount : 0) || 0;
            // 账户余额 - 优先使用card.count
            var balance = (card ? card.count : 0) || 0;
            // 会员卡号
            var cardNo = (card ? card.code : '--') || '--';
            $('#accumulatedAmount').text(accumulated + ' 元');
            $('#accountBalance').text(balance + ' 元');
            $('#memberCardNumber').text(cardNo);
            // 确定会员等级
            var levelName = '普通会员';
            if (client.levelId) {
                var found = levels.find(function(it) { return it.id == client.levelId; });
                if (found) levelName = found.name;
            } else {
                // 如果没有levelId，根据累计金额计算
                for (var i = levels.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                    if (Number(accumulated) >= levels[i].threshold) {
                        levelName = levels[i].name;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            $('#currentLevelBadge').text(levelName);
        }
        function setupEvents(){
            // 快捷金额
            $(document).on('click', '.amount-option', function(){
                $('.amount-option').removeClass('active');
                $(this).addClass('active');
                $('#amountInput').val($(this).data('amount'));
            });
            $(document).on('focus', '#amountInput', function(){ $('.amount-option').removeClass('active'); });
            // 帮助
            $('#infoBtn').off('click').on('click', function(){ layer.msg('请确认充值金额，充值完成后以后台返回为准'); });
            // 确认充值
            $('#confirmBtn').off('click').on('click', function(e){
                if(e && e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
                console.log('CLICK-RECHARGE-START', new Date().toISOString());
                if(typeof $ === 'undefined' || !$.ajax){ layer.msg('内部错误：jQuery 未就绪', {icon:2}); return; }
                var raw = $('#amountInput').val();
                var amount = parseFloat(raw);
                console.log('ABOUT-TO-SEND payload prepare, raw amount=', raw, ' parsed=', amount);
                if(isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0){ layer.msg('请输入有效的充值金额', {icon:2}); return; }
                var clientId = localStorage.getItem('id') || localStorage.getItem('userId') || null;
                var payload = clientId ? { clientId: Number(clientId), amount: amount } : { amount: amount };
                console.log('send ajax', RECHARGE_PATH, payload);
                var idx = layer.load(1, {shade:0.3});
                $.ajax({
                    url: RECHARGE_PATH,
                    method: 'POST',
                    contentType: 'application/json',
                    data: JSON.stringify(payload),
                    success: function(resp){
                        layer.close(idx);
                        if(!resp){ layer.msg('服务端无返回', {icon:0}); return; }
                        // 兼容 code / status 两种结构
                        if(resp.code !== undefined && resp.code !== 0){
                            layer.msg(resp.msg || '充值未通过', {icon:2}); return;
                        }
                        if(resp.status !== undefined && (resp.status !== 0 && resp.success !== true)){
                            layer.msg(resp.msg || '充值未通过', {icon:2}); return;
                        }
                        var data = resp.data || resp;
                        if(data && data.isNewCard) layer.msg('新卡创建成功并充值完成', {icon:1});
                        else layer.msg('充值成功', {icon:1});
                        applyServerDataToUI(data);
                    },
                    error: function(xhr, status, err){
                        layer.close(idx);
                        console.error('ajax error', status, err, 'responseText=', xhr && xhr.responseText, 'statusCode=', xhr && xhr.status);
                        if(xhr && xhr.status === 0){
                            layer.msg('网络异常或请求被拦截（请检查控制台 Network 与扩展）', {icon:2, time:4000});
                        } else {
                            layer.msg('充值请求失败，请检查后端或网络（' + (xhr && xhr.status) + '）', {icon:2});
                        }
                    }
                });
            });
        }
        // 初始化
        $(function(){
            renderLevels();
            setupEvents();
            fetchMemberInfo(); // 加载会员信息
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>
