<script src="../../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['table','layer','form','upload','jquery','laytpl'], function(){
        var table = layui.table, layer = layui.layer, form = layui.form, upload = layui.upload, $ = layui.$, laytpl = layui.laytpl;
        // API 统一配置
        var API = {
            list: '/project/list',
            get: '/project/',
            create: '/project',
            update: '/project/',
            remove: '/project/',
            batchDelete: '/project/batch-delete',
            batchStatus: '/project/batch-status',
            items: '/api/items',
            upload: '/project/upload',
            updateStatus: '/project/'  // 保留原路径，实际请求体模式通过修改后端实现的约定
        };
        // 通用请求封装
        function request(options){
            var opts = $.extend(true, {
                method: 'GET',
                contentType: 'application/json',
                dataType: 'json'
            }, options);
            var ajaxOpt = {
                url: opts.url,
                type: opts.method,
                dataType: opts.dataType,
                contentType: opts.contentType,
                success: function(res){ opts.success && opts.success(res); },
                error: function(xhr){ opts.error && opts.error(xhr); }
            };
            if(opts.contentType === 'application/json' && opts.data && typeof opts.data !== 'string'){
                ajaxOpt.data = JSON.stringify(opts.data);
            } else {
                ajaxOpt.data = opts.data;
            }
            return $.ajax(ajaxOpt);
        }
        // 防抖
        function debounce(fn, wait){
            var t;
            return function(){
                var ctx = this, args = arguments;
                clearTimeout(t);
                t = setTimeout(function(){ fn.apply(ctx, args); }, wait);
            };
        }
        // 全局变量存储检查项数据
        var allItemsData = [];
        var selectedItemsData = [];
        // 表格渲染
        var tableId = 'projectTable';
        var tableIns = table.render({
            elem: '#'+tableId,
            cols: [[
                {type:'checkbox', fixed:'left'},
                {field:'id', title:'ID', width:70, sort:true},
                {field:'name', title:'套餐名称', minWidth:180, templet:function(d){ return '<span class="ellipsis" title="'+ (d.name||'') +'">' + (d.name||'') + '</span>'; }},
                {field:'info', title:'描述', minWidth:200, templet:function(d){ var txt = d.info||''; return '<span class="ellipsis" title="'+ txt +'">' + txt + '</span>'; }},
                {field:'price', title:'价格(元)', width:110, align:'right', templet:function(d){ return (d.price!=null? Number(d.price).toFixed(2):'-'); }},
                {field:'count', title:'库存', width:100, align:'center'},
                // {field:'itemCount', title:'检查项数', width:110, align:'center', templet:function(d){ return (d.item_ids? d.item_ids.split(',').filter(Boolean).length : 0); }},
                {field:'pic', title:'图片', width:100, align:'center', templet:function(d){
                        if(d.pic){
                            return '<img class="thumbnail" data-src="'+ d.pic +'" src="'+ d.pic +'" alt="pic" role="button" tabindex="0">';
                        }
                        return '-';
                    }},
                {field:'status', title:'状态', width:100, align:'center', templet:function(d){ if(d.status==1) return '<span style="color:#009688">上架</span>'; return '<span style="color:#999">下架</span>'; }},
                {title:'操作', toolbar:'#toolbarTpl', width:240, align:'center'}
            ]],
            page: true,
            limit: 10,
            height: 'full-160',
            url: API.list,
            method: 'get',
            parseData: function(res){
                if(res && res.code === 0 && Array.isArray(res.data)){
                    return { "code": 0, "msg": res.msg||'', "count": res.count||res.data.length||0, "data": res.data };
                }
                return { "code": res && res.code || 1, "msg": res && res.msg || '加载失败', "count": 0, "data": [] };
            },
            request: { pageName: 'page', limitName: 'size' },
            where: {}
        });
        // 图片预览
        $('body').on('click', '.thumbnail', function(){
            var src = $(this).attr('data-src') || $(this).attr('src');
            layer.photos({
                photos: { title: '图片预览', data: [{src: src}] },
                shadeClose: true, anim: 5
            });
        });
        // 搜索
        var doSearch = debounce(function(){
            table.reload(tableId, { where: { name: $('#searchName').val(), status: $('#filterStatus').val() }, page: { curr: 1 } });
        }, 300);
        $('#btnSearch').on('click', doSearch);
        $('#searchName').on('keyup', function(e){ if(e.key === 'Enter' || e.keyCode === 13) doSearch(); });
        // 操作按钮
        $('#btnAdd').on('click', function(){ openForm(); });
        $('#btnMore').on('click', function(){
            layer.open({ type:1, title:'更多操作', area:['320px','240px'], content: '<div style="padding:12px;">' +
                    '<button class="layui-btn layui-btn-sm" id="exportBtn">导出CSV</button> ' +
                    '<button class="layui-btn layui-btn-sm layui-btn-primary" id="refreshBtn">刷新缓存</button>' +
                    '</div>' });
        });
        $('#btnBatchEnable').on('click', function(){
            var data = table.checkStatus(tableId).data;
            if(!data || data.length===0){ layer.msg('请选择要操作的记录'); return; }
            var ids = data.map(function(i){ return i.id; });
            var shown = ids.slice(0,8).join(',') + (ids.length>8? ' ...共'+ids.length+'项' : '');
            layer.confirm('确认将选中套餐上架？<div style="margin-top:8px;color:#666;">'+ shown +'</div>', function(idx){
                request({ url: API.batchStatus, method: 'POST', contentType:'application/json', data: { ids: ids, status:1 } })
                    .done(function(res){ if(res.code===0){ layer.msg('操作成功'); table.reload(tableId); } else { layer.msg(res.msg||'失败'); } })
                    .fail(function(){ layer.msg('网络错误'); });
                layer.close(idx);
            });
        });
        $('#btnBatchDelete').on('click', function(){
            var data = table.checkStatus(tableId).data;
            if(!data || data.length===0){ layer.msg('请选择要删除的记录'); return; }
            var ids = data.map(function(i){ return i.id; });
            var shown = ids.slice(0,8).join(',') + (ids.length>8? ' ...共'+ids.length+'项' : '');
            layer.confirm('确认删除选中套餐？（软删）<div style="margin-top:8px;color:#666;">'+ shown +'</div>', function(idx){
                request({ url: API.batchDelete, method: 'POST', contentType:'application/json', data: { ids: ids } })
                    .done(function(res){ if(res.code===0){ layer.msg('删除成功'); table.reload(tableId); } else { layer.msg(res.msg||'失败'); } })
                    .fail(function(){ layer.msg('网络错误'); });
                layer.close(idx);
            });
        });
        // 检查项渲染函数
        function renderItemEntry(item, isSelected) {
            var className = isSelected ? 'item-entry selected in-use' : 'item-entry';
            var thumbHtml = item.pic ?
                '<img src="' + item.pic + '" class="item-thumb" alt="图片">' :
                '<div class="item-thumb" style="background:#f5f5f5;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#999;font-size:12px;">无图</div>';
            return '<li class="' + className + '" data-id="' + item.id + '">' +
                thumbHtml +
                '<div class="item-info">' +
                '<div class="item-name">' + (item.name || '未命名') + '</div>' +
                '<div class="item-desc">' + (item.info || '暂无描述') + '</div>' +
                '</div>' +
                '<div class="item-point">' + (item.point || 0) + '分</div>' +
                '</li>';
        }
        // 渲染可选检查项
        function renderAvailableItems(searchText) {
            var filteredItems = allItemsData.filter(function(item) {
                var isNotSelected = !selectedItemsData.some(function(sel) { return String(sel.id) === String(item.id); });
                var matchSearch = !searchText || (item.name && item.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchText.toLowerCase()));
                return isNotSelected && matchSearch;
            });
            var html = '';
            if (filteredItems.length === 0) {
                html = '<li style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:40px;">暂无可选项目</li>';
            } else {
                filteredItems.forEach(function(item) {
                    html += renderItemEntry(item, false);
                });
            }
            $('#availableItems').html(html);
            $('#availableCount').text(filteredItems.length);
        }
        // 渲染已选检查项
        function renderSelectedItems() {
            var html = '';
            if (selectedItemsData.length === 0) {
                html = '<li style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:40px;">暂无已选项目</li>';
            } else {
                selectedItemsData.forEach(function(item) {
                    html += renderItemEntry(item, true);
                });
            }
            $('#selectedItems').html(html);
            $('#selectedCount').text(selectedItemsData.length);
        }
        // 加载所有检查项
        function loadAllItems() {
            return request({ url: API.items + '?page=1&size=1000', method: 'GET' })
                .done(function(res) {
                    allItemsData = [];
                    if(res && res.code === 0) {
                        if(Array.isArray(res.data)) {
                            allItemsData = res.data;
                        } else if(res.data && Array.isArray(res.data.records)) {
                            allItemsData = res.data.records;
                        } else if(res.data && Array.isArray(res.data.data)) {
                            allItemsData = res.data.data;
                        }
                    }
                    console.log('加载的所有检查项:', allItemsData);
                })
                .fail(function() {
                    layer.msg('加载检查项失败');
                    console.error('加载检查项失败');
                });
        }
        // 检查项点击事件
        $(document).on('click', '#availableItems .item-entry', function() {
            var id = $(this).data('id');
            var item = allItemsData.find(function(item) { return String(item.id) === String(id); });
            if (item && !selectedItemsData.some(function(sel) { return String(sel.id) === String(id); })) {
                selectedItemsData.push(item);
                console.log('添加检查项:', item, '当前已选:', selectedItemsData.length);
                renderAvailableItems($('#searchAvailable').val());
                renderSelectedItems();
            }
        });
        $(document).on('click', '#selectedItems .item-entry', function() {
            var id = $(this).data('id');
            selectedItemsData = selectedItemsData.filter(function(item) { return String(item.id) !== String(id); });
            console.log('移除检查项 ID:', id, '当前已选:', selectedItemsData.length);
            renderAvailableItems($('#searchAvailable').val());
            renderSelectedItems();
        });
        // 搜索可选项
        $(document).on('input', '#searchAvailable', debounce(function() {
            renderAvailableItems($(this).val());
        }, 300));
        // 表格操作事件
        table.on('tool(projectTableFilter)', function(obj){
            var data = obj.data, event = obj.event;
            if(event === 'edit'){ openForm(data.id); }
            else if(event === 'detail'){ openDetail(data); }
            else if(event === 'del'){
                layer.confirm('确认删除该套餐？', function(idx){
                    request({ url: API.remove + data.id, method: 'DELETE' })
                        .done(function(res){ if(res.code===0){ layer.msg('删除成功'); table.reload(tableId); } else layer.msg(res.msg||'删除失败'); })
                        .fail(function(){ layer.msg('网络错误'); });
                    layer.close(idx);
                });
            } else if(event === 'toggle'){
                // 根据前端约定：1 上架，0 下架
                var toStatus = data.status==1 ? 0 : 1;
                // 直接以 JSON 体方式发送
                layer.confirm('确认更改状态？', function(idx){
                    // 使用 JSON 请求体的方式发送状态，URL 仍为 /project/{id}/status
                    request({
                        url: API.updateStatus + data.id + '/status',
                        method: 'POST',
                        contentType:'application/json',
                        data: { status: toStatus }
                    })
                        .done(function(res){
                            if(res && res.code===0){
                                layer.msg('操作成功');
                                table.reload(tableId);
                            } else {
                                layer.msg((res && res.msg) || '失败');
                            }
                        })
                        .fail(function(){ layer.msg('网络错误'); });
                    layer.close(idx);
                });
            }
        });
        // 打开详情窗口
        function openDetail(data) {
            var detailTpl = $('#detailTpl').html();
            var compiled = laytpl(detailTpl);
            var content = compiled.render(data);
            var detailIndex = layer.open({
                type: 1,
                title: '套餐详情',
                area: ['90%', '80%'],
                maxWidth: '800px',
                content: content,
                success: function() {
                    // 加载包含的检查项详情
                    loadDetailItems(data.id);
                }
            });
        }
        // 加载详情页面的检查项
        function loadDetailItems(projectId) {
            request({ url: API.get + projectId + '/items', method: 'GET' })
                .done(function(res) {
                    console.log('详情页检查项API返回:', res);
                    if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data) {
                        var items = [];
                        // 根据实际返回的数据结构解析
                        var dataArray = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [res.data];
                        if(dataArray.length > 0 && dataArray[0].selected) {
                            items = dataArray[0].selected;
                        }
                        renderDetailItemsList(items);
                    } else {
                        $('#detailItemsList').html('<div style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:40px;">加载失败</div>');
                    }
                })
                .fail(function() {
                    $('#detailItemsList').html('<div style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:40px;">加载失败</div>');
                });
        }
        // 渲染详情页检查项列表
        function renderDetailItemsList(items) {
            var html = '';
            if(items.length === 0) {
                html = '<div style="text-align:center;color:#999;padding:40px;">暂无检查项</div>';
            } else {
                html = '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr));gap:16px;">';
                items.forEach(function(item) {
                    var thumbHtml = item.pic ?
                        '<img src="' + item.pic + '" style="width:60px;height:60px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;" onclick="layer.photos({photos: {data: [{src: \'' + item.pic + '\'}]}, anim: 5});">' :
                        '<div style="width:60px;height:60px;background:#f5f5f5;border-radius:4px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#999;font-size:12px;">无图</div>';
                    html += '<div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #e6e6e6;border-radius:6px;padding:16px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;">' +
                        thumbHtml +
                        '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">' +
                        '<h4 style="margin:0 0 8px 0;color:#333;font-size:14px;font-weight:500;">' + (item.name || '未命名') + '</h4>' +
                        '<div style="color:#666;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:8px;line-height:1.4;">' + (item.info || '暂无描述') + '</div>' +
                        '<div style="color:#1890ff;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;">' + (item.point || 0) + ' 分</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                });
                html += '</div>';
            }
            $('#detailItemsList').html(html);
        }
        // 打开表单
        var formIndex;
        function openForm(id) {
            // 重置表单和全局变量
            form.val('projectForm', { id:'', name:'', price:'', count:'', status:1, info:'', pic:'' });
            $('#imgPreview').empty();
            $('#btnSaveForm').removeClass('layui-btn-disabled').prop('disabled', false);
            selectedItemsData = [];
            formIndex = layer.open({
                type: 1,
                title: id ? '编辑套餐' : '新增套餐',
                area: ['95%', '90%'],
                maxWidth: '960px',
                content: $('#projectFormBox'),
                success: function(){
                    // 先加载所有检查项
                    loadAllItems().done(function() {
                        console.log('所有检查项加载完成:', allItemsData.length);
                        // 渲染初始状态
                        renderAvailableItems();
                        renderSelectedItems();
                        // 如果是编辑模式，再加载套餐详情
                        if(id) {
                            // 延迟一下确保DOM渲染完成
                            setTimeout(function() {
                                loadProjectDetails(id);
                            }, 100);
                        }
                    }).fail(function() {
                        layer.msg('加载检查项数据失败');
                    });
                },
                end: function() {
                    selectedItemsData = [];
                }
            });
        }
        // 加载套餐详情
        function loadProjectDetails(id) {
            // 加载基础信息
            request({ url: API.get + id, method: 'GET' })
                .done(function(res) {
                    if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data) {
                        var payload = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data[0] : res.data;
                        form.val('projectForm', {
                            id: payload.id,
                            name: payload.name,
                            price: payload.price,
                            count: payload.count,
                            status: payload.status,
                            info: payload.info,
                            pic: payload.pic
                        });
                        if(payload.pic) {
                            $('input[name=pic]').val(payload.pic);
                            $('#imgPreview').html('<img src="' + payload.pic + '">');
                        }
                    }
                })
                .fail(function() {
                    console.warn('加载套餐基础信息失败');
                });
            // 加载已选检查项 - 修复数据解析逻辑
            request({ url: API.get + id + '/items', method: 'GET' })
                .done(function(res) {
                    if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data) {
                        var items = [];
                        // 修复：正确解析数据结构 - data是数组，取第一个元素的selected字段
                        if(Array.isArray(res.data) && res.data.length > 0) {
                            var dataItem = res.data[0];
                            if(dataItem.selected && Array.isArray(dataItem.selected)) {
                                items = dataItem.selected;
                            }
                        }
                        // 备用解析方式
                        else if(res.data.selected && Array.isArray(res.data.selected)) {
                            items = res.data.selected;
                        }
                        else if(Array.isArray(res.data)) {
                            items = res.data;
                        }
                        // 如果仍然没有获取到已选项，尝试从基础信息的item_ids字段解析
                        if(items.length === 0) {
                            tryLoadItemsFromBasicInfo(id);
                        } else {
                            selectedItemsData = items;
                            console.log('设置已选检查项数据:', selectedItemsData.length);
                            renderAvailableItems();
                            renderSelectedItems();
                        }
                    } else {
                        console.warn('加载已选检查项失败:', res && res.msg);
                        tryLoadItemsFromBasicInfo(id);
                    }
                })
                .fail(function() {
                    console.warn('请求已选检查项失败');
                    tryLoadItemsFromBasicInfo(id);
                });
        }
        // 备用方法：从基础信息的item_ids解析已选项
        function tryLoadItemsFromBasicInfo(id) {
            request({ url: API.get + id, method: 'GET' })
                .done(function(res) {
                    if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data) {
                        var payload = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data[0] : res.data;
                        if(payload.item_ids) {
                            var itemIds = payload.item_ids.split(',').filter(Boolean);
                            var items = allItemsData.filter(function(item) {
                                return itemIds.includes(String(item.id));
                            });
                            selectedItemsData = items;
                            renderAvailableItems();
                            renderSelectedItems();
                        }
                    }
                });
        }
        // 上传图片
        var uploadInst = upload.render({
            elem: '#btnUpload', url: API.upload, accept:'images', size:2048,
            before: function(obj){
                obj.preview(function(index, file, result){
                    $('#imgPreview').html('<img src="'+ result +'">');
                });
            },
            done: function(res){
                if(res && res.code===0){
                    var url = res.url || (res.data && (res.data.url || res.data.path)) || '';
                    $('input[name=pic]').val(url);
                    $('#imgPreview').html('<img src="'+ url +'">');
                    layer.msg('上传成功',{icon:1});
                } else {
                    layer.msg((res && res.msg) || '上传失败',{icon:2});
                }
            },
            error: function(){ layer.msg('上传错误',{icon:2}); }
        });
        $('#btnRemovePic').on('click', function(){
            $('input[name=pic]').val('');
            $('#imgPreview').empty();
        });
        // 表单校验
        form.verify({
            len100: function(v){ if(v && v.length>100) return '长度不得超过100字符'; },
            price: function(v){ if(v === '' || isNaN(v) || Number(v) < 0) return '价格格式不正确'; },
            int0: function(v){ if(!/^\d+$/.test(v)) return '请输入非负整数'; }
        });
        // 表单提交 - 添加详细调试信息
        form.on('submit(submitProject)', function(fd){
            var $save = $('#btnSaveForm');
            var data = fd.field;
            var isEdit = data.id && data.id.trim() !== '';
            // 处理检查项ID
            if(selectedItemsData.length > 0) {
                data.itemIds = selectedItemsData.map(function(item) { return item.id; }).join(',');
            } else {
                data.itemIds = '';
            }
            var url = isEdit ? (API.update + data.id) : API.create;
            var method = isEdit ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
            $save.addClass('layui-btn-disabled').prop('disabled', true);
            request({
                url: url,
                method: method,
                contentType:'application/json',
                data: data
            })
                .done(function(res){
                    if(res && res.code===0){
                        layer.msg('保存成功',{icon:1});
                        layer.close(formIndex);
                        table.reload(tableId);
                        console.log('✓ 保存成功');
                    } else {
                        layer.msg((res && res.msg) || '保存失败',{icon:2});
                    }
                })
                .fail(function(xhr, status, error){
                    layer.msg('网络错误',{icon:2});
                })
                .always(function(){
                    $save.removeClass('layui-btn-disabled').prop('disabled', false);
                });
            return false;
        });
        $('#btnCancelForm').on('click', function(){ layer.close(formIndex); });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="text/html" id="orderToolbar">
    <a class="layui-btn layui-btn-xs" lay-event="detail">详情</a>
    <a class="layui-btn layui-btn-normal layui-btn-xs" lay-event="status">修改状态</a>
    <a class="layui-btn layui-btn-danger layui-btn-xs" lay-event="del">删除</a>
</script>

<script src="../../layui/layui.js"></script>
<script>
    layui.use(['table','layer','form','laydate','jquery'], function(){
        var table = layui.table, layer = layui.layer, laydate = layui.laydate, $ = layui.$;
        // 固定四个状态文本
        const STATIC_STATUS = ["空闲","预约","签到","咨询"];
        var API = {
            list: '/order',
            get: '/order/',
            save: '/order/',
            updateStatus: '/order/',
            remove: '/order/',
            export: '/order/export'
        };
        // 简单请求封装
        function request(opts){
            var settings = $.extend({
                method: 'GET',
                dataType: 'json',
                contentType: 'application/json'
            }, opts || {});
            if(settings.contentType === 'application/json' && settings.data && typeof settings.data !== 'string'){
                settings.data = JSON.stringify(settings.data);
            }
            return $.ajax(settings);
        }
        // 时间范围选择
        laydate.render({ elem: '#dateRange', type: 'date', range: true, theme: '#1E9FFF' });
        // 防抖工具
        function debounce(fn, wait){
            var t;
            return function(){
                var ctx = this, args = arguments;
                clearTimeout(t);
                t = setTimeout(function(){ fn.apply(ctx, args); }, wait);
            };
        }
        // 状态显示映射（后台直接返回 status，前端显示固定文本）
        function renderStatus(status){
            var cls = 'status-badge ';
            var text = (status !== undefined && status !== null) ? String(status) : '';
            switch(String(status)){
                case 'pending': cls += 'status-pending'; text = '待支付'; break;
                case 'paid': cls += 'status-paid'; text = '已支付'; break;
                case 'completed': cls += 'status-completed'; text = '已完成'; break;
                case 'canceled': cls += 'status-canceled'; text = '已取消'; break;
                default:
                    if(status >=0 && status < STATIC_STATUS.length){
                        text = STATIC_STATUS[Number(status)];
                        cls += 'status-canceled';
                    } else {
                        cls += 'status-canceled'; text = status;
                    }
                    break;
            }
            return '<span class="'+ cls +'">'+ (text || '-') +'</span>';
        }
        // 表格渲染
        var tableId = 'orderTable';
        var tableIns = table.render({
            elem: '#'+tableId,
            cols: [[
                {type:'checkbox', fixed:'left'},
                {field:'id', title:'订单ID', width:180, templet:function(d){ return '<span class="ellipsis" title="'+ escapeHtml(d.id) +'">' + escapeHtml(d.id) + '</span>'; }},
                {field:'main_id', title:'主订单ID', width:150, templet:function(d){ return d.main_id? '<span class="ellipsis" title="'+ escapeHtml(d.main_id) +'">'+ escapeHtml(d.main_id) +'</span>' : '-'; }},
                {field:'clientName', title:'用户姓名', width:120, templet:function(d){ return escapeHtml(d.clientName || '-'); }},
                {field:'item_name', title:'项目', minWidth:220, templet:function(d){ return '<span class="ellipsis" title="'+ escapeHtml(d.item_name||'') +'">'+ escapeHtml(d.item_name||'') +'</span>'; }},
                {field:'date', title:'预约时间', width:180},
                {field:'status', title:'状态', width:110, align:'center', templet:function(d){ return renderStatus(d.status); }},
                {field:'comment', title:'备注', minWidth:140, templet:function(d){ return d.comment? '<span title="'+ escapeHtml(d.comment) +'">'+ escapeHtml(d.comment) +'</span>' : '-'; }},
                {title:'操作', toolbar:'#orderToolbar', width:240, align:'center'}
            ]],
            page: true, limit: 12, height: 'full-180',
            url: API.list, method: 'get',
            parseData: function(res){
                if(res && res.code === 0 && res.data){
                    var data = res.data.records || res.data;
                    var total = res.data.total || (Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : 0);
                    return { "code": 0, "msg": res.msg || '', "count": total, "data": data };
                }
                return { "code": res && res.code || 1, "msg": res && res.msg || '加载失败', "count": 0, "data": [] };
            },
            request: { pageName: 'page', limitName: 'size' },
            text: { none: '暂无订单' }
        });
        // 搜索（防抖）
        var doSearch = debounce(function(){
            table.reload(tableId, { where: { q: $('#searchText').val(), status: $('#filterStatus').val(), range: $('#dateRange').val() }, page: { curr: 1 } });
        }, 300);
        $('#btnSearch').on('click', doSearch);
        $('#searchText').on('keyup', function(e){ if(e.key === 'Enter' || e.keyCode === 13) doSearch(); });
        // 导出（当前筛选）
        $('#btnExport').on('click', function(){
            var params = { q: $('#searchText').val(), status: $('#filterStatus').val(), range: $('#dateRange').val() };
            var $form = $('<form method="POST" action="' + API.export + '"></form>');
            $form.append('<input type="hidden" name="params" value=\'' + escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(params)) + '\'>');
            $('body').append($form);
            $form.submit();
            $form.remove();
            layer.msg('导出请求已发起，视后端处理时间而定', {icon: 1, time: 2000});
        });
        // 导出选中
        $('#btnExportSelected').on('click', function(){
            var data = table.checkStatus(tableId).data;
            if(!data || data.length === 0){ layer.msg('请先选择要导出的订单'); return; }
            var ids = data.map(function(i){ return i.id; });
            var $form = $('<form method="POST" action="' + API.export + '"></form>');
            $form.append('<input type="hidden" name="ids" value=\'' + escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(ids)) + '\'>');
            $('body').append($form);
            $form.submit();
            $form.remove();
            layer.msg('导出选中订单请求已发起', {icon:1, time:1500});
        });
        // 表格操作
        table.on('tool(orderTableFilter)', function(obj){
            var d = obj.data, e = obj.event;
            if(e === 'detail'){ openDetail(d.id); }
            else if(e === 'status'){ promptStatusChange(d); }
            else if(e === 'del'){ confirmDelete(d); }
        });
        // 打开详情
        var detailIndex = null;
        function openDetail(id){
            $('#orderDetailContent').html('<div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;color:#888;">加载中...</div>');
            detailIndex = layer.open({ type:1, title:'订单详情', area:['92%','80%'], maxWidth:'900px', content: $('#orderDetailBox') });
            request({ url: API.get + id, method: 'GET' })
                .done(function(res){
                    if(!res || res.code !== 0 || !res.data){
                        $('#orderDetailContent').html('<div style="padding:20px;color:#c00;">加载失败</div>');
                        return;
                    }
                    var d = res.data;
                    var html = [];
                    // 基本信息
                    html.push('<div class="order-row"><div class="label">订单ID：</div><div class="value">' + escapeHtml(d.id||'') + '</div></div>');
                    html.push('<div class="order-row"><div class="label">主订单ID：</div><div class="value">' + escapeHtml(d.main_id||'-') + '</div></div>');
                    // 用户姓名替代用户ID显示
                    html.push('<div class="order-row"><div class="label">用户姓名：</div><div class="value">' + escapeHtml(d.clientName||'-') + '</div></div>');
                    html.push('<div class="order-row"><div class="label">项目：</div><div class="value">' + escapeHtml(d.project_name||d.item_name||'-') + '</div></div>');
                    html.push('<div class="order-row"><div class="label">预约时间：</div><div class="value">' + escapeHtml(d.date||'-') + '</div></div>');
                    // 主诊断/备注
                    html.push('<div class="order-row main-info-area"><div class="label">主诊断/备注：</div><div class="value"><textarea id="mainInfoTextarea" placeholder="填写诊断意见或备注">' + escapeHtml(d.info || d.comment || '') + '</textarea></div></div>');
                    // 附件
                    if(d.pic){
                        html.push('<div class="order-row"><div class="label">附件：</div><div class="value"><img src="'+ escapeHtml(d.pic) +'" alt="附件" style="max-width:240px;cursor:pointer;border-radius:6px;" id="order-detail-pic"></div></div>');
                    }
                    // 子检查项列表
                    html.push('<div style="margin-top:12px;"><div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px;color:#333;">子检查项</div>');
                    var items = d.items || [];
                    var details = d.details || [];
                    var detailMapByItem = {};
                    var detailMapById = {};
                    for(var i=0;i<details.length;i++){
                        var dt = details[i];
                        if(dt.itemId != null) detailMapByItem[dt.itemId] = dt;
                        if(dt.id != null) detailMapById[dt.id] = dt;
                    }
                    if(items.length === 0){
                        html.push('<div style="color:#888;padding:12px;background:#fff;border:1px dashed #eef1f6;border-radius:6px;">该项目暂无子检查项</div>');
                    } else {
                        for(var j=0;j<items.length;j++){
                            var it = items[j];
                            var existDetail = detailMapByItem[it.id] || {};
                            var commentVal = existDetail.comment || '';
                            var detailIdVal = existDetail.id != null ? existDetail.id : '';
                            html.push('<div class="item-row" data-itemid="'+ escapeHtml(it.id) +'" data-detailid="'+ escapeHtml(detailIdVal) +'">');
                            html.push('<div class="item-title">' + escapeHtml(it.name || ('子项 ' + it.id)) + '</div>');
                            if(it.info) html.push('<div class="item-info">' + escapeHtml(it.info) + '</div>');
                            if(it.pic) html.push('<div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><img src="'+ escapeHtml(it.pic) +'" style="max-width:160px;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;" class="item-pic"></div>');
                            html.push('<div><textarea class="item-comment" placeholder="此处填写该子检查项的诊断意见或备注">' + escapeHtml(commentVal) + '</textarea></div>');
                            html.push('</div>');
                        }
                    }
                    html.push('</div>');
                    // 保存按钮区
                    html.push('<div class="detail-actions"><button class="layui-btn layui-btn-normal" id="btnSaveDiagnosis">保存诊断</button><button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="btnCloseDetail2">关闭</button></div>');
                    $('#orderDetailContent').html(html.join(''));
                    // 绑定图片预览
                    $('#order-detail-pic').on('click', function(){
                        layer.photos({ photos: { data: [{src: d.pic}] }, anim: 5 });
                    });
                    $('.item-pic').on('click', function(){
                        var src = $(this).attr('src');
                        layer.photos({ photos: { data: [{src: src}] }, anim: 5 });
                    });
                    // 关闭按钮绑定
                    $('#btnCloseDetail2').off('click').on('click', function(){ if(detailIndex) layer.close(detailIndex); });
                    // 保存诊断按钮事件
                    $('#btnSaveDiagnosis').off('click').on('click', function(){
                        var mainId = d.main_id || d.id;
                        if(!mainId){
                            layer.msg('无法识别主订单ID', {icon:2});
                            return;
                        }
                        var mainInfo = $('#mainInfoTextarea').val();
                        // 收集每个子项的 comment
                        var detailsToSave = [];
                        $('#orderDetailContent .item-row').each(function(){
                            var $row = $(this);
                            var itemId = $row.attr('data-itemid');
                            var detailId = $row.attr('data-detailid') || null;
                            var comment = $row.find('.item-comment').val();
                            detailsToSave.push({
                                id: detailId ? (detailId|0) : null,
                                itemId: itemId ? (itemId|0) : null,
                                comment: comment
                            });
                        });
                        var detailTargetId = d.id || mainId;
                        var url = API.save + detailTargetId + '/save';
                        var payload = { mainId: mainId, info: mainInfo, details: detailsToSave };
                        request({
                            url: url,
                            method: 'POST',
                            data: payload
                        }).done(function(resp){
                            if(resp && resp.code === 0){
                                layer.msg('保存成功', {icon:1});
                                table.reload(tableId);
                                setTimeout(function(){ openDetail(d.id || mainId); }, 600);
                            } else {
                                layer.msg(resp && resp.msg || '保存失败', {icon:2});
                            }
                        }).fail(function(){
                            layer.msg('网络错误，保存失败', {icon:2});
                        });
                    });
                })
                .fail(function(){
                    $('#orderDetailContent').html('<div style="padding:20px;color:#c00;">网络错误</div>');
                });
        }
        // 关闭按钮
        $('#closeOrderDetail').on('click', function(){ if(detailIndex) layer.close(detailIndex); });
        // 修改状态：改为固定四项的下拉选择
        var statusChanging = false;
        function promptStatusChange(row){
            if(statusChanging) return;
            var html = '<div style="padding:12px; min-width:260px;">' +
                '<label>设置新状态：</label>' +
                '<select id="newStatusSelect" style="width:100%; margin-top:6px;">';
            STATIC_STATUS.forEach(function(s, idx){
                var selected = (row.status === idx || (typeof row.status !== 'string' && Number(row.status) === idx)) ? ' selected' : '';
                html += '<option value="' + idx + '"' + selected + '>' + s + '</option>';
            });
            html += '</select></div>';
            layer.open({
                type: 1,
                title: '修改状态',
                area: ['320px','160px'],
                content: html,
                btn: ['确定','取消'],
                yes: function(index){
                    var newStatusVal = $('#newStatusSelect').val();
                    layer.close(index);
                    statusChanging = true;
                    request({ url: API.updateStatus + row.id + '/status', method: 'POST', data: { status: newStatusVal } })
                        .done(function(res){
                            if(res && res.code === 0){
                                layer.msg('修改成功', {icon:1});
                                table.reload(tableId);
                            } else {
                                layer.msg(res && res.msg || '操作失败', {icon:2});
                            }
                        })
                        .fail(function(){ layer.msg('网络错误', {icon:2}); })
                        .always(function(){ statusChanging = false; });
                },
                btn2: function(index){
                    layer.close(index);
                }
            });
        }
        // 删除
        var deleting = false;
        function confirmDelete(row){
            if(deleting) return;
            layer.confirm('确认删除订单 ' + escapeHtml(row.id) + ' ？', function(idx){
                deleting = true;
                request({ url: API.remove + row.id, method: 'DELETE' })
                    .done(function(res){
                        if(res && res.code === 0){ layer.msg('删除成功', {icon:1}); table.reload(tableId); }
                        else layer.msg(res && res.msg || '删除失败', {icon:2});
                    })
                    .fail(function(){ layer.msg('网络错误', {icon:2}); })
                    .always(function(){ deleting = false; layer.close(idx); });
            });
        }
        // HTML 转义
        function escapeHtml(str){
            if(str === null || str === undefined) return '';
            return String(str).replace(/[&<>"'`=\/]/g, function(s){ return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'" :'&#39;','`':'&#96;','=':'&#61;','/':'&#47;'}[s]; });
        }
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

            <svg style="width:18px;height:18px;color:var(--primary)" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"><path d="M22 6h-10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M2 6h6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M17 12H7" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M2 12h2" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M22 18H11" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M2 18h6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>
        </div>
    </div>

        <button data-filter="all" class="active" role="tab">全部</button>

        <button data-filter="pending" role="tab">待检</button>
    </div>

<script>
    layui.use(['layer'], function(){
        const layer = layui.layer;
        // 模拟数据（与之前类似）
        const projects = ['血常规','尿常规','肝功能','肾功能','血糖','血脂','心电图','胸片'];
        const doctors = ['王医生','李医生','张医生','赵医生','陈医生'];
        const names = ['张伟','王芳','李强','刘洋','陈敏','赵磊','孙丽'];
        const genders = ['男','女'];
        function randomDate(start='2023-01-01', end='2025-08-30'){
            const s = new Date(start).getTime(), e = new Date(end).getTime();
            const t = new Date(s + Math.random()*(e-s));
            return t.toISOString().slice(0,10);
        }
        function rand(arr){ return arr[Math.floor(Math.random()*arr.length)]; }
        function sampleDetails(project){
            if(project === '血常规'){
                return [
                    {name:'白细胞(WBC)', value:'5.4', unit:'10^9/L', range:'4.0-10.0', conclusion:'正常'},
                    {name:'红细胞(RBC)', value:'4.6', unit:'10^12/L', range:'3.8-5.5', conclusion:'正常'}
                ];
            }
            return [
                {name:'指标A', value:(Math.random()*10+1).toFixed(2), unit:'单位', range:'参考', conclusion:Math.random()>0.85?'异常':'正常'},
                {name:'指标B', value:(Math.random()*50+5).toFixed(2), unit:'单位', range:'参考', conclusion:Math.random()>0.9?'异常':'正常'},
            ];
        }
        let allData = [];
        for(let i=1;i<=50;i++){
            const proj = rand(projects);
            const r = Math.random();
            const status = r < 0.25 ? 0 : (r < 0.8 ? 1 : 2);
            allData.push({
                id:1000+i,
                examNo:'EX'+(1000+i),
                name:rand(names),
                gender:rand(genders),
                age: 20 + Math.floor(Math.random()*45),
                check_time: randomDate(),
                project: proj,
                doctor: rand(doctors),
                remark: rand(['常规体检','复检','单位体检']),
                status: status,
                result: status===0?'待检':(status===1?'正常':'异常'),
                audit_time: status===0?null: randomDate(),
                abnormal_note: status===2? '检测到轻度异常，建议复查。' : '',
                suggestion: status===2? '建议 2周后复查并带此报告就诊。' : '',
                details: sampleDetails(proj)
            });
        }
        // 分页（简单模拟）
        let pageSize = 12, pageIndex = 0;
        const state = { keyword:'', filter:'all' };
        const listEl = document.getElementById('list');
        const totalCountEl = document.getElementById('totalCount');
        const emptyEl = document.getElementById('empty');
        const loadMoreBtn = document.getElementById('loadMore');
        function esc(s){ if(s==null) return ''; return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;') }
        function badgeClass(status){ return status===1?'normal':(status===2?'abnormal':'pending') }
        function filterData(){
            const kw = (state.keyword||'').trim().toLowerCase();
            let list = allData.slice().reverse();
            if(state.filter !== 'all'){
                if(state.filter === 'normal') list = list.filter(x=>x.status===1);
                if(state.filter === 'abnormal') list = list.filter(x=>x.status===2);
                if(state.filter === 'pending') list = list.filter(x=>x.status===0);
            }
            if(kw){
                list = list.filter(it=>{
                    return (it.examNo && it.examNo.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
                        || (it.name && it.name.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
                        || (it.project && it.project.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
                        || (it.doctor && it.doctor.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
                        || (it.check_time && it.check_time.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
                        || (it.remark && it.remark.toLowerCase().includes(kw));
                });
            }
            return list;
        }
        // 渲染（分页）
        function render(resetPage){
            if(resetPage){ pageIndex = 0; listEl.innerHTML = ''; }
            const filtered = filterData();
            totalCountEl.textContent = filtered.length;
            const start = pageIndex * pageSize;
            const slice = filtered.slice(start, start + pageSize);
            if(start === 0 && slice.length === 0){
                emptyEl.style.display = 'block';
                loadMoreBtn.style.display = 'none';
                return;
            } else {
                emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
            slice.forEach(item=>{
                const card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'card';
                card.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div class="avatar" aria-hidden="true">${esc(item.name.slice(0,1))}</div>
          <div class="meta">
            <div class="nameLine">${esc(item.name)} · ${esc(item.gender)} · ${esc(item.age)}岁</div>
            <div class="subLine">
              <div class="project">${esc(item.project)}</div>
              <div>${esc(item.check_time)}</div>
              <div style="color:var(--muted)">${esc(item.doctor)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="statusCol">
          <div class="badge ${badgeClass(item.status)}">${esc(item.result)}</div>
          <div class="ops">
            <div class="op" data-act="detail" data-id="${item.id}" role="button" aria-label="查看">查看</div>
            <div class="op" data-act="download" data-id="${item.id}" role="button" aria-label="下载">下载</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
                listEl.appendChild(card);
            });
            // 更新 load more visibility
            const moreAvailable = (start + pageSize) < filtered.length;
            loadMoreBtn.style.display = moreAvailable ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
        }
        render(true);
        // 加载更多
        loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
            pageIndex++;
            render(false);
        });
        // 事件代理
        document.body.addEventListener('click', function(e){
            const t = e.target;
            const actEl = t.closest('[data-act]');
            if(actEl){
                const act = actEl.getAttribute('data-act');
                const id = actEl.getAttribute('data-id');
                const item = allData.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
                if(act === 'detail' && item){
                    openDetail(item);
                } else if(act === 'download'){
                    layer.msg('开始下载报告（模拟）...', {offset:'t'});
                    setTimeout(()=>layer.msg('下载完成（模拟）', {offset:'t'}), 700);
                }
                return;
            }
            if(t === document.getElementById('filterBtn')){
                document.getElementById('filters').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
                return;
            }
        });
        // 搜索 Enter
        document.getElementById('kw').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
            if(e.key === 'Enter'){
                state.keyword = this.value || '';
                render(true);
            }
        });
        // 筛选按钮
        document.querySelectorAll('.seg button').forEach(btn=>{
            btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
                document.querySelectorAll('.seg button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
                this.classList.add('active');
                state.filter = this.getAttribute('data-filter');
                render(true);
            });
        });
        // 打开详情（使用 layui layer），增强关闭交互
        function openDetail(item){
            const rows = item.details.map(d=>`<tr><td>${esc(d.name)}</td><td>${esc(d.value)} ${esc(d.unit||'')}</td><td>${esc(d.range)}</td><td>${esc(d.conclusion)}</td></tr>`).join('');
            const html = `
      <div class="modal-body" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="体检报告详情">
        <div class="modal-handle" aria-hidden="true"></div>
        <h3 style="margin:6px 0 6px 0">体检 ${esc(item.examNo)} · ${esc(item.name)}</h3>
        <div class="layui-row layui-col-space5" style="margin-bottom:6px">
          <div class="layui-col-xs6 layui-col-sm6"><div class="small">性别：${esc(item.gender)} · 年龄：${esc(item.age)}岁</div></div>
          <div class="layui-col-xs6 layui-col-sm6" style="text-align:right"><div class="small">${item.audit_time?('审核：'+esc(item.audit_time)) : ''}</div></div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px">
          <div><strong>检查时间：</strong> ${esc(item.check_time)}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:6px"><strong>检查项目：</strong> ${esc(item.project)}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:6px"><strong>主检医生：</strong> ${esc(item.doctor)}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:6px"><strong>备注：</strong> ${esc(item.remark)}</div>
        </div>
        ${item.abnormal_note ? `<div style="margin-top:10px;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;background:#fff6f6;border-left:4px solid var(--danger)"><strong style="color:var(--danger)">异常说明：</strong><div class="small" style="margin-top:6px">${esc(item.abnormal_note)}</div></div>` : ''}
        ${item.suggestion ? `<div style="margin-top:10px;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;background:#f7fff5;border-left:4px solid var(--success)"><strong style="color:var(--success)">建议：</strong><div class="small" style="margin-top:6px">${esc(item.suggestion)}</div></div>` : ''}
        <div style="margin-top:12px;font-weight:700">化验单明细</div>
        <div style="overflow:auto;margin-top:6px">
          <table class="detailTable" role="table" aria-label="化验单明细">
            <thead><tr><th>项目</th><th>结果</th><th>参考范围</th><th>结论</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div class="modalActions">
          <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="printBtn">打印</button>
          <button class="layui-btn" id="downloadBtn">下载</button>
          <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-warm" id="closeBtn">关闭</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
            const idx = layer.open({
                type:1,
                title:false,
                closeBtn:1,
                shadeClose:true,
                area:['85%','86%'],
                offset:'b',
                content: html,
                success: function(layero){
                    // layero 是当前弹层的 jQuery-like DOM（layui v2 返回 layero）
                    // 安全获得 content DOM
                    const content = layero[0].querySelector('.modal-body');
                    // 绑定关闭（确保无论怎样都能关闭）
                    const closeHandler = function(){
                        try{ layer.close(idx); }catch(e){ layer.closeAll('page'); }
                    };
                    const downloadHandler = function(){
                        layer.msg('开始下载报告（模拟）...', {offset:'t'});
                        setTimeout(()=>layer.msg('下载完成（模拟）', {offset:'t'}), 800);
                    };
                    const printHandler = function(){
                        const htmlForPrint = content.innerHTML;
                        const w = window.open('', '_blank');
                        w.document.write('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>打印报告</title><style>body{font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;padding:16px;color:#111}</style></head><body>' + htmlForPrint + '</body></html>');
                        w.document.close();
                        setTimeout(()=>w.print(), 500);
                    };
                    const closeBtn = content.querySelector('#closeBtn');
                    const downloadBtn = content.querySelector('#downloadBtn');
                    const printBtn = content.querySelector('#printBtn');
                    if(closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeHandler);
                    if(downloadBtn) downloadBtn.addEventListener('click', downloadHandler);
                    if(printBtn) printBtn.addEventListener('click', printHandler);
                    // 允许按 Esc 键关闭（针对键盘用户）
                    const escListener = function(e){
                        if(e.key === 'Escape' || e.key === 'Esc') closeHandler();
                    };
                    document.addEventListener('keydown', escListener);
                    // 支持向下拖动关闭（更稳健）
                    const layerBox = layero[0]; // 最外层 DOM
                    let startY = 0, cur = 0, dragging = false;
                    const maxTranslate = window.innerHeight * 0.7; // 最大可拖动距离
                    const onTouchStart = function(e){ if(e.touches.length!==1) return; startY = e.touches[0].clientY; dragging=true; layerBox.style.transition='none'; };
                    const onTouchMove = function(e){ if(!dragging) return; cur = e.touches[0].clientY - startY; if(cur>0){ const t = Math.min(cur, maxTranslate); layerBox.style.transform = 'translateY(' + t + 'px)'; } };
                    const onTouchEnd = function(){ dragging=false; layerBox.style.transition='transform .25s ease'; if(cur > 120){ closeHandler(); } else { layerBox.style.transform = ''; } startY=cur=0; };
                    layerBox.addEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart, {passive:true});
                    layerBox.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, {passive:true});
                    layerBox.addEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd, {passive:true});
                    // 清理：当 layer 关闭时移除监听和按键
                    const origClose = layer.close;
                    // 使用 layer.ready 或监听 layer close 事件并移除 escListener & touch listeners
                    // layui 没有直接 close 回调参数可用，我们用 interval 轮询检测 layer 是否还在 DOM 中（轻量清理）
                    const intervalId = setInterval(()=>{
                        if(!document.body.contains(layerBox)){
                            document.removeEventListener('keydown', escListener);
                            try{
                                layerBox.removeEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart);
                                layerBox.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove);
                                layerBox.removeEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd);
                            }catch(e){}
                            clearInterval(intervalId);
                        }
                    }, 300);
                }
            });
        }
        // 对外接口
        window.healthHistoryLayui = {
            data: allData,
            render: function(arr){
                if(Array.isArray(arr)) allData = arr;
                render(true);
            },
            openDetailById: function(id){
                const it = allData.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
                if(it) openDetail(it);
            }
        };
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    layui.use(['layer'], function(){
        const layer = layui.layer;
        // 模拟建议数据（示例），在集成时请替换为后端接口
        const sample = [
            { id:1, title:'控制每日盐摄入量', category:'diet', excerpt:'成年人每日食盐摄入建议不超过6克，高血压患者应更严格控制。', content:'建议逐步减少口味偏咸的食物，使用蒸、煮、炖等烹饪方式，避免高盐酱料；增加蔬菜水果摄入帮助调节口味。', author:'营养师 李婷', date:'2025-08-01'},
            { id:2, title:'每周至少150分钟中等强度运动', category:'sport', excerpt:'坚持有氧运动有助于心肺健康和血糖血脂控制。', content:'可将运动分散到每周大多数天，每次30分钟左右，如快走、骑行、游泳；结合力量训练每周至少2次。', author:'康复师 王强', date:'2025-07-20'},
            { id:3, title:'改善睡眠习惯以缓解焦虑', category:'psych', excerpt:'规律作息与睡前放松能改善睡眠质量，帮助情绪恢复。', content:'建议建立固定睡眠时间，避免睡前使用电子设备；可尝试渐进性肌肉放松或冥想；白天适量运动帮助夜间入睡。', author:'心理咨询师 赵敏', date:'2025-06-15'},
            { id:4, title:'正确使用降压药物', category:'medic', excerpt:'遵医嘱长期服药是控制血压的关键，不要擅自停药。', content:'按医生处方服用，不随意调整剂量；出现不良反应及时就诊；定期监测血压并携带用药记录就诊。', author:'内科医生 陈亮', date:'2025-04-12'},
            { id:5, title:'多喝白开水，避免含糖饮料', category:'diet', excerpt:'含糖饮料会增加热量摄入并影响血糖、体重。', content:'以白开水为主，不喝或少喝碳酸和果汁饮料；如果需要口感，可在水中加入柠檬片或薄荷。', author:'营养师 李婷', date:'2025-05-30'},
            { id:6, title:'工作间歇做眼保健操', category:'other', excerpt:'长时间近距离用眼建议定期休息以缓解视疲劳。', content:'采用20-20-20规则：每工作20分钟，远眺20英尺（约6米）至少20秒；并做眼保健操，保持良好照明。', author:'眼科 康复团队', date:'2025-03-10'},
        ];
        // 本地收藏存储 key
        const STORAGE_KEY = 'health_advice_favs_v1';
        function loadFavs(){
            try{
                const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
                return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
            }catch(e){ return []; }
        }
        function saveFavs(arr){
            try{ localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(arr)); }catch(e){}
        }
        let favs = loadFavs(); // 存储 id 数组
        let data = sample.slice(); // 可由外部接口替换
        const state = { cat:'all', keyword:'', onlyFav:false };
        const listEl = document.getElementById('list');
        const emptyEl = document.getElementById('empty');
        function esc(s){ if(s==null) return ''; return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;') }
        function tagLabel(cat){
            switch(cat){
                case 'diet': return '<span class="tag diet">饮食</span>';
                case 'sport': return '<span class="tag sport">运动</span>';
                case 'psych': return '<span class="tag psych">心理</span>';
                case 'medic': return '<span class="tag medic">用药</span>';
                default: return '<span class="tag other">其他</span>';
            }
        }
        function render(){
            const kw = (state.keyword||'').trim().toLowerCase();
            let list = data.slice().reverse();
            if(state.cat !== 'all') list = list.filter(i=>i.category === state.cat);
            if(state.onlyFav) list = list.filter(i=> favs.includes(i.id) );
            if(kw){
                list = list.filter(i => (i.title && i.title.toLowerCase().includes(kw)) || (i.excerpt && i.excerpt.toLowerCase().includes(kw)) || (i.content && i.content.toLowerCase().includes(kw)) );
            }
            listEl.innerHTML = '';
            if(list.length === 0){
                emptyEl.style.display = 'block';
                return;
            } else {
                emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
            list.forEach(item=>{
                const isFav = favs.includes(item.id);
                const card = document.createElement('article');
                card.className = 'card';
                card.innerHTML = `
            <div class="cardLeft">
              <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
                ${tagLabel(item.category)}
                <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
                  <div class="headline">${esc(item.title)}</div>
                  <div class="excerpt">${esc(item.excerpt)}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="metaRow">
                <div>${esc(item.author)}</div>
                <div>·</div>
                <div>${esc(item.date)}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="ops">
              <button class="opBtn" data-act="fav" data-id="${item.id}" aria-label="收藏">${isFav?'已收藏':'收藏'}</button>
              <button class="opBtn" data-act="detail" data-id="${item.id}" aria-label="查看">查看</button>
            </div>
          `;
                listEl.appendChild(card);
            });
        }
        // 事件代理：收藏/查看/分享 等
        document.body.addEventListener('click', function(e){
            const t = e.target;
            // 分类按钮
            if(t.classList.contains('catBtn')){
                document.querySelectorAll('.catBtn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
                t.classList.add('active');
                state.cat = t.getAttribute('data-cat');
                render();
                return;
            }
            // 收藏开关
            const favBtn = t.closest('[data-act="fav"]');
            if(favBtn){
                const id = Number(favBtn.getAttribute('data-id'));
                if(favs.includes(id)){
                    favs = favs.filter(x=>x!==id);
                    layer.msg('已取消收藏', {offset:'t'});
                } else {
                    favs.push(id);
                    layer.msg('已加入收藏', {offset:'t'});
                }
                saveFavs(favs);
                render();
                return;
            }
            // 查看详情
            const det = t.closest('[data-act="detail"]');
            if(det){
                const id = Number(det.getAttribute('data-id'));
                const item = data.find(x=>x.id===id);
                if(item) openDetail(item);
                return;
            }
            // 仅显示收藏开关
            if(t.id === 'favFilter'){
                state.onlyFav = !state.onlyFav;
                t.style.background = state.onlyFav ? 'linear-gradient(90deg,#fff7ed,#fff)' : '';
                layer.msg(state.onlyFav ? '仅显示收藏' : '显示全部', {offset:'t'});
                render();
                return;
            }
        });
        // 搜索输入回车
        document.getElementById('kw').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
            if(e.key === 'Enter'){
                state.keyword = this.value || '';
                render();
            }
        });
        // 打开详情（layui 底部弹层）
        function openDetail(item){
            const isFav = favs.includes(item.id);
            const html = `
          <div class="modal-body">
            <div class="modal-handle" aria-hidden="true"></div>
            <h3 style="margin:6px 0 6px 0">${esc(item.title)}</h3>
            <div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${esc(item.author)} · ${esc(item.date)}</div>
            <div class="detail-section">
              <h4>建议要点</h4>
              <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.6">${esc(item.content)}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="detail-section">
              <h4>更多说明</h4>
              <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.6;color:var(--muted)">${esc(item.excerpt)}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="actionsRow">
              <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-primary" id="shareBtn">分享</button>
              <button class="layui-btn" id="favToggle">${isFav ? '取消收藏' : '收藏'}</button>
              <button class="layui-btn layui-btn-warm" id="closeBtn">关闭</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
            const idx = layer.open({
                type:1, title:false, closeBtn:0, shadeClose:true, area:['100%','86%'], offset:'b', content: html,
                success: function(dom){
                    const content = dom;
                    content.querySelector('#closeBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>layer.close(idx));
                    content.querySelector('#favToggle').addEventListener('click', function(){
                        if(favs.includes(item.id)){
                            favs = favs.filter(x=>x!==item.id);
                            layer.msg('已取消收藏', {offset:'t'});
                        } else {
                            favs.push(item.id);
                            layer.msg('已加入收藏', {offset:'t'});
                        }
                        saveFavs(favs);
                        layer.close(idx);
                        render();
                    });
                    content.querySelector('#shareBtn').addEventListener('click', function(){
                        const text = `${item.title}\n\n${item.content}\n\n来自：${item.author}`;
                        if(navigator.share){
                            navigator.share({title: item.title, text: text, url: location.href}).catch(()=>layer.msg('分享取消', {offset:'t'}));
                        } else {
                            copyToClipboard(text).then(()=> layer.msg('分享内容已复制到剪贴板', {offset:'t'})).catch(()=> layer.msg('复制失败', {offset:'t'}));
                        }
                    });
                    // 支持向下拖动关闭（简易）
                    const layerBox = content.parentElement;
                    let startY=0, cur=0, dragging=false;
                    layerBox.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e){ if(e.touches.length!==1) return; startY = e.touches[0].clientY; dragging=true; layerBox.style.transition='none'; }, {passive:true});
                    layerBox.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e){ if(!dragging) return; cur = e.touches[0].clientY - startY; if(cur>0) layerBox.style.transform = 'translateY(' + cur + 'px)'; }, {passive:true});
                    layerBox.addEventListener('touchend', function(){ dragging=false; layerBox.style.transition='transform .2s ease'; if(cur > 120){ layer.close(idx); } else { layerBox.style.transform = ''; } startY=cur=0; }, {passive:true});
                }
            });
        }
        // 复制到剪贴板（Promise）
        function copyToClipboard(text){
            if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){
                return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
            }
            return new Promise(function(resolve, reject){
                try{
                    const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
                    ta.value = text; document.body.appendChild(ta);
                    ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(ta);
                    resolve();
                }catch(e){ reject(e); }
            });
        }
        // 暴露接口，便于外部替换数据或调用
        window.healthAdviceLayui = {
            data: data,
            setData: function(arr){
                if(Array.isArray(arr)) data = arr;
                render();
            },
            addSuggestion: function(item){
                // item 必须包含 id,title,category,excerpt,content,author,date
                data.push(item);
                render();
            },
            openById: function(id){
                const it = data.find(x=>x.id===id);
                if(it) openDetail(it);
            },
            getFavs: function(){ return favs.slice(); }
        };
        // 初次渲染
        render();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>
